243: def cmd_status(repo: Path, db_path: Path, history_reader, as_json: bool):
244:     t0 = time.time()
245:     try:
246:         actions = read_actions(str(repo))
247:     except Exception as exc:
248:         if as_json:
249:             print(json.dumps({"error": str(exc)}))
250:         else:
251:             print(f"Error reading actions: {exc}")
252:         sys.exit(3)
253: 
254:     active_action = actions[0] if actions else None
255:     if not active_action:
256:         if as_json:
257:             print(json.dumps({"error": "No actions registered"}))
258:         else:
259:             print("No actions registered in .moriarty-dev/actions.json")
260:         sys.exit(3)
261: 
262:     snap = load_snapshot(str(repo), active_action["id"], history_reader=history_reader)
263:     decision = assess(snap, active_action)
264: 
265:     capability = f"{active_action.get('requirement', 'SP05')} {active_action.get('capability', 'financial execution')}"
266:     last_result = _derive_last_result(repo)
267:     failures = snap.get("sameDefectFailures")
268:     if failures is not None and failures >= 2:
269:         blocked_action = f"another broad {active_action.get('kind', 'correction')}"
270:         reason = f"{failures} failures of required execution behavior"
271:     elif failures is None or any(
272:         isinstance(item, str) and (item == "operational-history" or item.startswith("operational-history"))
273:         for item in snap.get("missingEvidence", [])
274:     ):
275:         blocked_action = f"implementation/repair of {active_action.get('capability', 'action')}"
276:         reason = "operational history is unresolved or unverified"
277:     else:
278:         blocked_action = "none"
279:         reason = "none"
280: 
281:     # Reporting is always allowed, but that must not hide stale source or missing
282:     # accounting behind the report decision or a generic history-only summary.
283:     evidence_gaps = [
284:         item for item in snap.get("missingEvidence", [])
285:         if isinstance(item, str) and not item.startswith("operational-history")
286:     ]
287:     if evidence_gaps:
288:         if blocked_action == "none":
289:             blocked_action = f"dependent dispatch for {active_action.get('capability', 'action')}"
290:         detail = "current evidence gaps: " + "; ".join(dict.fromkeys(evidence_gaps))
291:         reason = detail if reason == "none" else reason + "; " + detail
292: 
293:     next_action_id = snap.get("nextActionId") or active_action["id"]
294:     undelivered = get_undelivered_txs(db_path, str(repo))
295: 
296:     data = {
297:         "capability": capability,
298:         "lastResult": last_result,
299:         "blockedAction": blocked_action,
300:         "reason": reason,
301:         "nextAction": next_action_id,
302:         "missingEvidence": snap.get("missingEvidence", []),
303:         "pendingTransactions": [tx["txId"] for tx in undelivered],
304:     }
305: 
306:     if as_json:
307:         print(json.dumps(data, indent=2))
308:     else:
309:         print(f"Capability: {capability}")
310:         print(f"Last result: {last_result}")
311:         if blocked_action != "none":
312:             print(f"Blocked action: {blocked_action}")
313:             print(f"Reason: {reason}")
314:         print(f"Next: {next_action_id}")
315:         if undelivered:
316:             print(f"Pending Transactions: {', '.join(tx['txId'] for tx in undelivered)}")
317:         else:
318:             print("Network: no financial transaction evidence recorded")
319: 
320:     if db_path.is_file():
321:         try:
322:             record_admin_interval(db_path, str(repo), t0, time.time(), "status")
323:         except Exception:
324:             pass
325: 
326: 
327: def cmd_next(repo: Path, db_path: Path, history_reader, as_json: bool):
328:     try:
329:         actions = read_actions(str(repo))
330:     except Exception as exc:
331:         sys.stderr.write(f"Error reading actions: {exc}\n")
332:         sys.exit(3)
333: 
334:     if not actions:
335:         sys.stderr.write("No actions found\n")
336:         sys.exit(3)
337: 
338:     best_action = None
339:     best_decision = None
340:     for act in actions:
341:         snap = load_snapshot(str(repo), act["id"], history_reader=history_reader)
342:         dec = assess(snap, act)
343:         if dec["allow"]:
344:             best_action = act
345:             best_decision = dec
346:             break
347: 
348:     if best_action is None:
349:         # Check first action's decision
350:         first_snap = load_snapshot(str(repo), actions[0]["id"], history_reader=history_reader)
351:         best_decision = assess(first_snap, actions[0])
352:         best_action = actions[0]
353: 
354:     out = {
355:         "action": best_action,
356:         "allow": best_decision["allow"],
357:         "reasonCode": best_decision["reasonCode"],
358:         "nextActionId": best_decision["nextActionId"],
359:         "missingEvidence": best_decision["missingEvidence"],
360:     }
361:     if as_json:
362:         print(json.dumps(out, indent=2))
363:     else:
364:         print(f"Next action: {best_decision.get('nextActionId') or best_action['id']}")
365:         print(f"Allowed: {best_decision['allow']}")
366:         print(f"Reason: {best_decision['reasonCode']}")
367: 
368: 
369: def _load_admitted_runner(repo: Path, action: dict) -> tuple[dict, str]:
370:     return load_runner(repo, action)
371: 
372: 
373: def cmd_run(repo: Path, db_path: Path, history_reader, action_id: str, as_json: bool):
374:     t0 = time.time()
375:     try:
376:         actions = read_actions(str(repo))
377:     except Exception as exc:
378:         sys.stderr.write(f"Error reading actions: {exc}\n")
379:         sys.exit(3)
380: 
381:     matching = [a for a in actions if a["id"] == action_id]
382:     if not matching:
383:         sys.stderr.write(f"Unknown action: {action_id}\n")
384:         sys.exit(3)
385:     action = matching[0]
386: 
387:     snap = load_snapshot(str(repo), action_id, history_reader=history_reader)
388:     decision = assess(snap, action)
389: 
390:     if not decision["allow"]:
391:         msg = f"Policy denied action '{action_id}': {decision['reasonCode']}. Next: {decision['nextActionId']}"
392:         if as_json:
393:             print(json.dumps({"error": msg, "decision": decision}))
394:         else:
395:             sys.stderr.write(msg + "\n")
396:         sys.exit(2)
397: 
398:     try:
399:         runner, runner_digest = _load_admitted_runner(repo, action)
400:     except Exception as exc:
401:         msg = f"Launch unavailable for '{action_id}': {exc}"
402:         if as_json:
403:             print(json.dumps({"error": msg, "reasonCode": "RUNNER_UNAVAILABLE"}))
404:         else:
405:             sys.stderr.write(msg + "\n")
406:         sys.exit(2)
407: 
408:     try:
409:         res_id = reserve(db_path, str(repo), action, snap, charge_id=runner["chargeId"])
410:     except ReservationConflictError as exc:
411:         sys.stderr.write(f"Reservation conflict: {exc}\n")
412:         sys.exit(2)
413:     except Exception as exc:
414:         sys.stderr.write(f"Store error during reservation: {exc}\n")
415:         sys.exit(3)
416: 
417:     # Immediately revalidate snapshot and policy after reservation
418:     post_snap = load_snapshot(str(repo), action_id, history_reader=history_reader)
419:     post_snap["primaryActive"] = has_other_primary(db_path, res_id)
420:     post_dec = assess(post_snap, action)
421:     if not post_dec["allow"]:
422:         finish(db_path, res_id, {"error": f"Policy denied after reservation: {post_dec['reasonCode']}"}, status="failed")
423:         msg = f"Policy denied action '{action_id}' after reservation: {post_dec['reasonCode']}"
424:         if as_json:
425:             print(json.dumps({"error": msg, "decision": post_dec}))
426:         else:
427:             sys.stderr.write(msg + "\n")
428:         sys.exit(2)
429: 
430:     command_ref = action.get("commandRef", "")
431:     if not command_ref or action.get("kind") == "report":
432:         finish(db_path, res_id, {"exitCode": 0}, status="finished")
433:         if as_json:
434:             print(json.dumps({"success": True, "action": action_id}))
435:         sys.exit(0)
436: 
437:     # Revalidate action and runner bytes after reservation, before launch.
438:     try:
439:         current_action = next(a for a in read_actions(str(repo)) if a.get("id") == action_id)
440:         current_runner, current_digest = _load_admitted_runner(repo, current_action)
441:         if current_action != action or current_digest != runner_digest:
442:             raise ValueError("admitted action or runner changed after reservation")
443:     except Exception as exc:
444:         finish(db_path, res_id, {"error": f"Runner revalidation failed: {exc}"}, status="failed")
445:         sys.stderr.write(f"Runner revalidation failed: {exc}\n")
446:         sys.exit(2)
447: 
448:     receipt = None
449:     try:
450:         receipt = execute(repo, runner, runner_digest, res_id)
451:         observed = receipt["runnerReceipt"]
452:         # Check current command/input and durable debit again before claiming
453:         # completion. Changed evidence or supervisor loss stays unresolved.
454:         _, terminal_digest = _load_admitted_runner(repo, action)
455:         if terminal_digest != runner_digest or observed["completionAmbiguous"]:
456:             raise StoreError("Runner completion is ambiguous; reservation and charge remain claimed")
457:         verified = bool(observed["behavioralAssertions"])
458:         success = not observed["outputLimitExceeded"] and (
459:             verified if action["kind"] == "reproduce" else receipt["exitCode"] == 0
460:         )
461:         if verified:
462:             record_event(db_path, str(repo), action["requirement"], action["capability"],
463:                          action["candidate"], action_id, "reproducer_verified", receipt)
464:         finish(db_path, res_id, receipt, status="finished" if success else "failed")
465:         result = {"success": success, "action": action_id, "exitCode": receipt["exitCode"],
466:                   "chargeId": runner["chargeId"], "reservationId": res_id}
467:         if action["kind"] == "reproduce":
468:             result["reproducerVerified"] = verified
469:         if as_json:
470:             print(json.dumps(result))
471:         elif not success:
472:             sys.stderr.write("Bound runner did not establish the required result\n")
473:         sys.exit(0 if success else 4)
474:     except Exception as exc:
475:         # Parent/process death is not evidence that its children and charge
476:         # stopped. Keep the reservation active and preserve the uncertainty.
477:         record_event(db_path, str(repo), action["requirement"], action["capability"],
478:                      action["candidate"], action_id, "runner_unresolved", {"error": str(exc), "reservationId": res_id, "receipt": receipt})
479:         sys.stderr.write(f"Runner completion unresolved: {exc}\n")
480:         sys.exit(4)
481: 
482: 
483: def cmd_review(repo: Path, db_path: Path, receipt_path: str, as_json: bool, action_id: str | None = None):
484:     t0 = time.time()
485:     p = Path(receipt_path)
486:     if not p.is_absolute():
487:         p = (repo / p).resolve()
488:     if not p.is_file():
489:         sys.stderr.write(f"Receipt file missing: {p}\n")
490:         sys.exit(3)
491: 
492:     try:
493:         data = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
494:     except Exception as exc:
495:         sys.stderr.write(f"Error reading receipt JSON: {exc}\n")
496:         sys.exit(3)
497: 
498:     if not isinstance(data, dict):
499:         sys.stderr.write("Review rejected: receipt must be a JSON object\n")
500:         sys.exit(3)
501: 
502:     try:
503:         actions = read_actions(str(repo))
504:     except Exception as exc:
505:         sys.stderr.write(f"Error reading actions: {exc}\n")
506:         sys.exit(3)
507: 
508:     target_action = None
509:     if action_id:
510:         matching = [a for a in actions if a.get("id") == action_id]
511:         if not matching:
512:             sys.stderr.write(f"Review rejected: unknown action '{action_id}'\n")
513:             sys.exit(3)
514:         target_action = matching[0]
515:     else:
516:         req_receipt = data.get("requirement")
517:         cap_receipt = data.get("capability")
518:         cand_receipt = data.get("candidateHash") or data.get("candidate")
519:         act_id_receipt = data.get("actionId") or data.get("action")
520: 
521:         if act_id_receipt:
522:             matching = [a for a in actions if a.get("id") == act_id_receipt]
523:             if matching:
524:                 target_action = matching[0]
525: 
526:         if not target_action and req_receipt and cap_receipt:
527:             matching = [a for a in actions if a.get("requirement") == req_receipt and a.get("capability") == cap_receipt]
528:             if matching:
529:                 rev_matching = [a for a in matching if a.get("kind") == "review"]
530:                 target_action = rev_matching[0] if rev_matching else matching[0]
531: 
532:         if not target_action and cand_receipt:
533:             matching = [a for a in actions if a.get("candidate") == cand_receipt]
534:             if matching:
535:                 rev_matching = [a for a in matching if a.get("kind") == "review"]
536:                 target_action = rev_matching[0] if rev_matching else matching[0]
537: 
538:     if not target_action:
539:         sys.stderr.write("Review rejected: receipt does not bind to any registered action or capability in repository\n")
540:         sys.exit(3)
541: 
542:     cand = data.get("candidateHash") or data.get("candidate")
543:     if not cand or not isinstance(cand, str) or len(cand) < 16:
544:         sys.stderr.write("Review rejected: valid candidateHash of at least 16 hex chars required\n")
545:         sys.exit(3)
546: 
547:     action_cand = target_action.get("candidate", "")
548:     if cand != action_cand:
549:         sys.stderr.write(f"Review rejected: candidateHash {cand} does not match action candidate {action_cand}\n")
550:         sys.exit(3)
551: 
552:     receipt_scope = data.get("scope") or data.get("auditScope")
553:     if not receipt_scope or not isinstance(receipt_scope, str) or receipt_scope.strip() in ("", "none", "None"):
554:         sys.stderr.write("Review rejected: valid non-empty scope required\n")
555:         sys.exit(3)
556: 
557:     admission_ref = target_action.get("admissionRef", "")
558:     expected_scope = None
559:     if admission_ref.startswith("campaign:"):
560:         camp_id = admission_ref.split(":", 1)[1]
561:         camp_file = repo / "evidence" / "moriarty-completion-program-2026-09-07" / "report-reconciliation" / "campaign-admission.json"
562:         if camp_file.is_file():
563:             try:
564:                 cdata = json.loads(camp_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
565:                 camp_record = cdata.get("campaigns", {}).get(camp_id, {})
566:                 expected_scope = camp_record.get("scope")
567:             except Exception:
568:                 pass
569: 
570:     if expected_scope and receipt_scope != expected_scope:
571:         sys.stderr.write(f"Review rejected: scope '{receipt_scope}' does not match campaign scope '{expected_scope}'\n")
572:         sys.exit(3)
573: 
574:     history_reader = make_history_reader(db_path)
575:     snap = load_snapshot(str(repo), target_action["id"], history_reader=history_reader)
576:     if snap.get("authorityCurrent") is False:
577:         sys.stderr.write(f"Review rejected: authority unavailable for action {target_action['id']}\n")
578:         sys.exit(3)
579:     if snap.get("candidateCurrent") is False:
580:         sys.stderr.write(f"Review rejected: candidate not current for action {target_action['id']}\n")
581:         sys.exit(3)
582: 
583:     author = data.get("author")
584:     reviewer = data.get("reviewer") or data.get("reviewerObserved") or data.get("reviewerRequested")
585:     if not reviewer or not isinstance(reviewer, str):
586:         sys.stderr.write("Review rejected: reviewer identity required\n")
587:         sys.exit(3)
588:     if author and isinstance(author, str):
589:         a_low = author.lower().strip()
590:         r_low = reviewer.lower().strip()
591:         if a_low in r_low or r_low in a_low:
592:             sys.stderr.write("Review rejected: author self-review is forbidden\n")
593:             sys.exit(3)
594: 
595:     try:
596:         record_review(db_path, str(repo), data, action=target_action)
597:         if db_path.is_file():
598:             try:
599:                 record_admin_interval(db_path, str(repo), t0, time.time(), "review")
600:             except Exception:
601:                 pass
602:     except StoreError as exc:
603:         sys.stderr.write(f"Review rejected: {exc}\n")
604:         sys.exit(3)
605:     except Exception as exc:
606:         sys.stderr.write(f"Store error recording review: {exc}\n")
607:         sys.exit(3)
608: 
609:     if as_json:
610:         print(json.dumps({"success": True, "receipt": str(p), "action": target_action["id"]}))
611:     else:
612:         print(f"Ingested review receipt from {p} for action {target_action['id']}")
613:     sys.exit(0)
614: 
615: 
616: def cmd_report(repo: Path, db_path: Path, history_reader, as_json: bool):
617:     t0 = time.time()
618:     sprint_rows = _derive_sprint_reports(repo)
619:     undelivered = get_undelivered_txs(db_path, str(repo))
620: 
621:     compatibility = inspect_compatibility(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2])
622:     out = {
623:         "allSprints": sprint_rows,
624:         "pendingTransactions": undelivered,
625:         "hostCoverage": observed_coverage(compatibility),
626:         "compatibility": compatibility,
627:     }
628:     if as_json:
629:         print(json.dumps(out, indent=2))
630:     else:
631:         print("# Moriarty Development Status Report\n")
632:         for row in sprint_rows:
633:             print(f"- **{row['sprint']}**: {row['goal']} (Status: {row['status']}) - {row['predicate']}")
634:     if db_path.is_file():
635:         try:
636:             record_admin_interval(db_path, str(repo), t0, time.time(), "report")
637:         except Exception:
638:             pass
639:     sys.exit(0)
640: 
641: 
642: def cmd_doctor(repo: Path, db_path: Path, as_json: bool, expected_roots=()):
643:     actions_file = repo / ".moriarty-dev" / "actions.json"
644:     actions_ok = actions_file.is_file()
645: 
646:     # Registration/source presence is observable; actual host interception is
647:     # not derivable from files. Never promote a self-authored coverage flag.
648:     source = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
649:     compatibility = inspect_compatibility(source, expected_roots)
650:     coverage = observed_coverage(compatibility)
651:     host_installed = coverage == "installed-unverified"
652:     hook_definitions_present = (source / "hooks" / "hooks.json").is_file()
653:     hooks_registered = False  # Activation/trust cannot be inferred from files.
654:     guarantees = "Registered action launch is guarded through CLI run"
655:     limitations = "Host tool interception requires observed host evidence; source or registration presence does not establish coverage"
656: 
657:     data = {
658:         "repository": str(repo),
659:         "storeDb": str(db_path),
660:         "storeInitialized": db_path.is_file(),
661:         "actionsRegistered": actions_ok,
662:         "hostInstalled": host_installed,
663:         "compatibility": compatibility,
664:         "hooksRegistered": hooks_registered,
665:         "hookDefinitionsPresent": hook_definitions_present,
666:         "hostCoverage": coverage,
667:         "guarantees": guarantees,
668:         "limitations": limitations,
669:     }
670:     if as_json:
671:         print(json.dumps(data, indent=2))
672:     else:
673:         print(f"Repository: {repo}")
674:         print(f"Store DB: {db_path} ({'initialized' if db_path.is_file() else 'missing'})")
675:         print(f"Actions file: {'present' if actions_ok else 'missing'}")
676:         print(f"Host coverage: {coverage}")
677:         print(f"Executing plugin: {source}")
678:         for root in compatibility["cachedRoots"] + compatibility["expectedRoots"]:
679:             state = "present (runtime unverified)" if root["usable"] else "missing or incomplete"
680:             print(f"Plugin root: {root['path']} ({state})")
681:         print(f"Guarantees: {guarantees}")
682:         print(f"Limitations: {limitations}")
683:     sys.exit(0)
684: 
685: 
686: if __name__ == "__main__":
687:     main()

FILE plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/compatibility.py
1: """Read-only package observations, never host activation or trust claims."""
2: import hashlib
3: import json
4: import os
5: from pathlib import Path
6: import platform
7: import sys
8: 
9: PLUGIN_NAME = "moriarty-dev"
10: CORE_FILES = (
11:     ".codex-plugin/plugin.json", "hooks/hooks.json",
12:     "scripts/moriarty_dev/__init__.py", "scripts/moriarty_dev/hook.py",
13:     "scripts/moriarty_dev/cli.py", "scripts/moriarty_dev/policy.py",
14:     "scripts/moriarty_dev/records.py", "scripts/moriarty_dev/store.py",
15:     "scripts/moriarty_dev/runner.py", "scripts/moriarty_dev/notifications.py",
16: )
17: 
18: 
19: def _observe_root(root):
20:     root = Path(root).expanduser().resolve()
21:     missing = [name for name in CORE_FILES if not (root / name).is_file()]
22:     errors = []
23:     manifest = {}
24:     try:
25:         manifest = json.loads((root / ".codex-plugin/plugin.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
26:         if not isinstance(manifest, dict) or manifest.get("name") != PLUGIN_NAME:
27:             errors.append("manifest identity mismatch")
28:             manifest = {}
29:     except (OSError, ValueError):
30:         errors.append("manifest unavailable or invalid")
31:     hook_hash = None
32:     try:
33:         hook_hash = hashlib.sha256((root / "scripts/moriarty_dev/hook.py").read_bytes()).hexdigest()
34:     except OSError:
35:         errors.append("hook script unavailable")
36:     return {
37:         "path": str(root), "version": manifest.get("version"),
38:         "usable": not missing and not errors,
39:         "missingFiles": missing, "errors": errors, "hookSha256": hook_hash,
40:     }
41: 
42: 
43: def inspect_compatibility(plugin_root, expected_roots=()):
44:     """Observe source and cache entries without changing any installation state.
45: 
46:     `usable` is a basic package-presence check, not runtime execution evidence.
47:     Cached versions may be inactive. CODEX_HOME comes from the invoking process.
48:     """
49:     codex_home = Path(os.environ.get("CODEX_HOME") or Path.home() / ".codex").expanduser().resolve()
50:     cache = codex_home / "plugins/cache"
51:     candidates = sorted(cache.glob(f"*/{PLUGIN_NAME}/*"))
52:     legacy = codex_home / "plugins" / PLUGIN_NAME
53:     if legacy.exists():
54:         candidates.append(legacy)
55:     roots = sorted({candidate.resolve() for candidate in candidates if candidate.is_dir()})
56:     return {
57:         "codexHome": str(codex_home),
58:         "executingPlugin": _observe_root(plugin_root),
59:         "cachedRoots": [_observe_root(root) for root in roots],
60:         "expectedRoots": [_observe_root(root) for root in expected_roots],
61:         "environmentRoots": {
62:             name: _observe_root(os.environ[name])
63:             for name in ("PLUGIN_ROOT", "CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT") if os.environ.get(name)
64:         },
65:         "activation": "unknown", "hookTrust": "unknown",
66:         "runtime": {
67:             "python": platform.python_version(), "pythonMinimum": "3.11",
68:             "pythonSupported": sys.version_info >= (3, 11),
69:             "platform": sys.platform,
70:             "hookShell": "POSIX shell with python3",
71:             "runnerPlatform": "Linux with the admitted Foreman launcher and strong containment",
72:         },
73:     }
74: 
75: 
76: def observed_coverage(observation):
77:     installed = any(root["usable"] for root in observation["cachedRoots"])
78:     return "installed-unverified" if installed else "unverified"

FILE plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/hook.py
1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """Read-only lifecycle adapter; the guarded CLI remains the launch gate."""
3: from __future__ import annotations
4: 
5: import json
6: import os
7: from pathlib import Path
8: import re
9: import shlex
10: import signal
11: import sys
12: 
13: sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))
14: from moriarty_dev.hook_protocol import (
15:     diagnostic as _diagnostic,
16:     output as _output,
17:     read_payload,
18:     serialize as _bounded_json,
19:     supported_event,
20: )
21: from moriarty_dev.policy import assess
22: from moriarty_dev.records import load_snapshot, read_actions
23: from moriarty_dev.store import get_db_path, get_undelivered_txs, make_history_reader
24: from moriarty_dev.notifications import notification_line
25: 
26: 
27: def _last_result(repo):
28:     try:
29:         data = json.loads((repo / "openspec/moriarty-completion-program.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
30:         stages = data.get("reportReconciliation", {}).get("stageAdmission", {}).get("stages", [])
31:         done = [row["id"] for row in stages if row.get("status") == "complete"]
32:         return "Stages complete: " + (", ".join(done) if done else "none")
33:     except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, AttributeError):
34:         return "No readable program stage record"
35: 
36: 
37: def _pending(repo):
38:     rows = get_undelivered_txs(get_db_path(repo), str(repo), timeout=0.05, limit=5)
39:     if not rows:
40:         return ""
41:     text = "Pending public observations: post dedicated notification message, then use deliver.\n"
42:     text += "\n".join(notification_line(tx) for tx in rows[:4])
43:     if len(rows) > 4:
44:         text += "\nMore pending IDs: use report --json."
45:     return text
46: 
47: 
48: def main():
49:     event = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else ""
50:     timer_supported = hasattr(signal, "setitimer")
51:     previous_handler = None
52: 
53:     class HookDeadline(BaseException):
54:         pass
55: 
56:     def deadline(_signum, _frame):
57:         raise HookDeadline()
58: 
59:     try:
60:         if timer_supported:
61:             previous_handler = signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM, deadline)
62:             signal.setitimer(signal.ITIMER_REAL, 0.75)
63:         payload = read_payload(sys.stdin)
64:         event = event or payload.get("hook_event_name", payload.get("hookEventName", ""))
65:         if not supported_event(event):
66:             raise ValueError("unsupported hook event")
67:         repo = Path(payload.get("cwd", os.getcwd())).resolve()
68:         for ancestor in (repo, *repo.parents):
69:             if (ancestor / ".moriarty-dev/actions.json").is_file():
70:                 repo = ancestor
71:                 break
72:             if (ancestor / ".git").exists():
73:                 break
74:         response = handle_event(event, payload, repo)
75:     except (HookDeadline, Exception):
76:         response = _diagnostic(event)
77:     finally:
78:         if timer_supported and previous_handler is not None:
79:             signal.setitimer(signal.ITIMER_REAL, 0)
80:             signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM, previous_handler)
81:     print(_bounded_json(response))
82: 
83: 
84: def handle_event(event_name, payload, repo):
85:     if not supported_event(event_name):
86:         return _diagnostic(event_name)
87:     if not (repo / ".moriarty-dev/actions.json").is_file():
88:         return _output(event_name)
89:     if event_name == "SessionStart":
90:         return handle_session_start(repo)
91:     if event_name == "PreToolUse":
92:         return handle_pre_tool_use(payload, repo)
93:     if event_name == "PostToolUse":
94:         try:
95:             return _output(event_name, additionalContext=_pending(repo))
96:         except Exception:
97:             return _diagnostic(event_name)
98:     return _output(event_name)  # Stop never blocks or invokes another action.
99: 
100: 
101: def handle_session_start(repo):
102:     try:
103:         actions = read_actions(str(repo))
104:         if not actions:
105:             return _output("SessionStart")
106:         action = next((row for row in actions if row.get("kind") != "report"), actions[0])
107:         snapshot = load_snapshot(str(repo), action["id"], history_reader=make_history_reader(get_db_path(repo), timeout=0.05))
108:         lines = [f"Capability: {action.get('requirement')} {action.get('capability')}",
109:                  f"Last result: {_last_result(repo)}",
110:                  f"Next: {snapshot.get('nextActionId') or action['id']}"]
111:         failures = snapshot.get("sameDefectFailures")
112:         if isinstance(failures, int) and failures >= 2:
113:             lines.append("Blocked: another broad correction; reproduce the unresolved defect.")
114:         elif failures is None:
115:             lines.append("History unresolved; inspect CLI status before dispatch.")
116:         pending = _pending(repo)
117:         if pending:
118:             lines.append(pending)
119:         return _output("SessionStart", additionalContext="\n".join(lines))
120:     except Exception:
121:         return _diagnostic("SessionStart")
122: 
123: 
124: def _registered_argv(repo, action):
125:     ref = action.get("commandRef", "")
126:     if "#" not in ref:
127:         return None
128:     filename, key = ref.split("#", 1)
129:     path = (repo / filename).resolve()
130:     if not path.is_relative_to(repo.resolve()):
131:         return None
132:     try:
133:         data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
134:         argv = data.get("commands", {}).get(key, {}).get("argv")
135:         return argv if isinstance(argv, list) and all(isinstance(value, str) for value in argv) else None
136:     except (OSError, ValueError, AttributeError):
137:         return None
138: 
139: 
140: def _dispatch_request(tokens):
141:     """Recognize simple Python CLI run forms, never arbitrary action mentions.
142: 
143:     Shell composition, wrappers and interpreter code strings are deliberately
144:     outside this recognizer. It is a workflow guard, not a shell parser.
145:     """
146:     if not tokens:
147:         return None
148:     args = list(tokens)
149:     if re.fullmatch(r"python(?:\d+(?:\.\d+)*)?", Path(args[0]).name):
150:         args.pop(0)
151:         while args and args[0] in ("-B", "-u"):
152:             args.pop(0)
153:         if args[:2] == ["-m", "moriarty_dev.cli"]:
154:             args = args[2:]
155:         elif args and Path(args[0]).name == "cli.py":
156:             args.pop(0)
157:         else:
158:             return None
159:     elif Path(args[0]).name == "cli.py":
160:         args.pop(0)
161:     else:
162:         return None
163:     target_repo = None
164:     while args:
165:         if args[0] == "--json":
166:             args.pop(0)
167:         elif args[0] == "--repo" and len(args) > 1:
168:             target_repo = args[1]
169:             args = args[2:]
170:         elif args[0].startswith("--repo="):
171:             target_repo = args.pop(0).split("=", 1)[1]
172:         else:
173:             break
174:     if not args or args.pop(0) != "run":
175:         return None
176:     action_id = None
177:     while args:
178:         flag = args.pop(0)
179:         if flag == "--json":
180:             continue
181:         if flag == "--action" and args:
182:             action_id = args.pop(0)
183:         elif flag.startswith("--action="):
184:             action_id = flag.split("=", 1)[1]
185:         else:
186:             return None
187:     return (action_id, target_repo) if action_id else None
188: 
189: 
190: def handle_pre_tool_use(payload, repo):
191:     event = "PreToolUse"
192:     name = payload.get("tool_name", payload.get("toolName", ""))
193:     if not isinstance(name, str):
194:         return _diagnostic(event)
195:     if name not in ("Bash", "bash", "shell", "exec_command", "execute_command"):
196:         return _output(event)
197:     try:
198:         inp = payload.get("tool_input", payload.get("toolInput", {}))
199:         if not isinstance(inp, dict):
200:             return _diagnostic(event)
201:         value = next((inp[key] for key in ("command", "cmd", "argv") if key in inp), "")
202:         tokens = shlex.split(value) if isinstance(value, str) else value
203:         if not isinstance(tokens, list) or not all(isinstance(token, str) for token in tokens):
204:             return _diagnostic(event)
205:         request = _dispatch_request(tokens)
206:         if request and request[1] is not None:
207:             repo = (Path(payload.get("cwd", repo)) / request[1]).resolve()
208:         actions = read_actions(str(repo))
209:         for action in actions:
210:             argv = _registered_argv(repo, action)
211:             if argv and tokens == argv:
212:                 return _output(event, permissionDecision="deny",
213:                                permissionDecisionReason=f"Registered dispatch requires guarded CLI run --action {action['id']}.")
214:             if not request or action["id"] != request[0]:
215:                 continue
216:             snapshot = load_snapshot(str(repo), action["id"], history_reader=make_history_reader(get_db_path(repo), timeout=0.05))
217:             decision = assess(snapshot, action)
218:             if not decision["allow"]:
219:                 return _output(event, permissionDecision="deny",
220:                                permissionDecisionReason=f"Repeated unresolved failure or unavailable admission ({decision.get('reasonCode')}). Run {decision.get('nextActionId') or 'CLI status'}.")
221:     except Exception:
222:         return _diagnostic(event)
223:     return _output(event)
224: 
225: 
226: if __name__ == "__main__":
227:     main()

FILE plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/hook_protocol.py
1: """Conservative JSON wire contract shared by supported hook hosts.
2: 
3: The host owns permissions. Absence of a decision is the only permitted-call
4: response we emit; Stop uses common output only. No host version guessing.
5: """
6: import json
7: 
8: EVENTS = frozenset({"SessionStart", "PreToolUse", "PostToolUse", "Stop"})
9: INPUT_LIMIT = 8192
10: OUTPUT_LIMIT = 2048
11: DIAGNOSTIC = (
12:     "Moriarty hook input or records unavailable; use guarded CLI status/run. "
13:     "Host coverage remains unverified."
14: )
15: 
16: 
17: def supported_event(value):
18:     return isinstance(value, str) and value in EVENTS
19: 
20: 
21: def output(event, **fields):
22:     if event == "Stop" or not supported_event(event):
23:         return {}
24:     return {"hookSpecificOutput": {"hookEventName": event, **fields}}
25: 
26: 
27: def diagnostic(event):
28:     return {**output(event), "systemMessage": DIAGNOSTIC}
29: 
30: 
31: def read_payload(stream):
32:     """Read at most limit+1 bytes; reject damaged input before repository work."""
33:     raw = getattr(stream, "buffer", stream).read(INPUT_LIMIT + 1)
34:     if isinstance(raw, str):  # StringIO/in-process callers, not host stdin.
35:         raw = raw.encode("utf-8")
36:     if len(raw) > INPUT_LIMIT:
37:         raise ValueError("hook input exceeds byte limit")
38:     text = raw.decode("utf-8")
39:     payload = json.loads(text) if text.strip() else {}
40:     if not isinstance(payload, dict):
41:         raise ValueError("hook input must be an object")
42:     if "cwd" in payload:
43:         cwd = payload["cwd"]
44:         if not isinstance(cwd, str) or not cwd or "\x00" in cwd:
45:             raise ValueError("invalid hook cwd")
46:         cwd.encode("utf-8")
47:     return payload
48: 
49: 
50: def serialize(response):
51:     """Keep denial semantics even when escaped JSON exceeds the byte budget."""
52:     response = dict(response)
53:     inner = dict(response.get("hookSpecificOutput", {}))
54:     if "hookSpecificOutput" in response:
55:         response["hookSpecificOutput"] = inner
56:     for owner, key, limit in ((inner, "additionalContext", 1300),
57:                               (inner, "permissionDecisionReason", 350),
58:                               (response, "systemMessage", 180)):
59:         if isinstance(owner.get(key), str):
60:             owner[key] = owner[key].encode("utf-8", errors="replace")[:limit].decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
61:     # ASCII JSON also works under non-UTF8 host stdout encodings.
62:     text = json.dumps(response, ensure_ascii=True, separators=(",", ":"))
63:     if len(text.encode("ascii")) + 1 <= OUTPUT_LIMIT:
64:         return text
65:     if inner.get("permissionDecision") == "deny":
66:         response = output("PreToolUse", permissionDecision="deny",
67:                           permissionDecisionReason="Moriarty denied this action; use CLI status for details.")
68:     else:
69:         response = {"systemMessage": "Moriarty hook output exceeded bound; use CLI status."}
70:     return json.dumps(response, separators=(",", ":"))

FILE plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/notifications.py
1: """Observe an emitted public message in the current Codex session.
2: 
3: Local session files are host evidence within the non-adversarial local-user
4: boundary. They establish an emitted message, not that a person read it or that
5: Midnight finalized a transaction. No transcript text is copied into the store.
6: """
7: from datetime import datetime, timezone
8: import hashlib
9: import json
10: import os
11: from pathlib import Path
12: import re
13: 
14: from moriarty_dev.store import StoreError
15: 
16: MAX_TAIL = 2 * 1024 * 1024
17: 
18: 
19: def notification_line(tx):
20:     return f"Midnight Preview transaction {tx['txId']}: {tx['status']}."
21: 
22: 
23: def _time(value):
24:     value = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace('Z', '+00:00'))
25:     if value.tzinfo is None:
26:         raise ValueError('Timestamp must include timezone')
27:     return value
28: 
29: 
30: def _contains_line(text, expected):
31:     # A closed message format avoids confusing examples, quotes, hidden HTML
32:     # or Markdown code blocks with an actual notification. No Markdown parser.
33:     lines = [line for line in text.splitlines() if line]
34:     pattern = r'Midnight Preview transaction [A-Za-z0-9:_-]{1,256}: (submitted|unknown-finality|failed|confirmed)\.'
35:     return expected in lines and all(re.fullmatch(pattern, line) for line in lines)
36: 
37: 
38: def observe_delivery(tx):
39:     thread = os.environ.get('CODEX_THREAD_ID') or os.environ.get('CODEX_SESSION_ID', '')
40:     if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9a-fA-F-]{36}', thread):
41:         raise StoreError('Current Codex session identity unavailable; notification remains pending')
42:     home = Path(os.environ.get('CODEX_HOME', Path.home() / '.codex'))
43:     sessions = home / 'sessions'
44:     paths = list(sessions.glob(f'*/*/*/rollout-*{thread}.jsonl'))
45:     if len(paths) != 1:
46:         raise StoreError('Unique current host transcript unavailable; notification remains pending')
47:     path = paths[0]
48:     expected = notification_line(tx)
49:     since = _time(tx['enqueuedAt'])
50:     now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
51:     with path.open('rb') as stream:
52:         header = json.loads(stream.readline(65536))
53:         if header.get('type') != 'session_meta' or header.get('payload', {}).get('id') != thread:
54:             raise StoreError('Host transcript identity mismatch')
55:         stream.seek(0, 2)
56:         size = stream.tell()
57:         start = max(0, size - MAX_TAIL)
58:         stream.seek(start)
59:         if start:
60:             stream.readline(MAX_TAIL)
61:         tail = stream.read(MAX_TAIL)
62:     for raw in reversed(tail.splitlines()):
63:         try:
64:             record = json.loads(raw)
65:             payload = record.get('payload', {})
66:             if (record.get('type') != 'response_item' or payload.get('type') != 'message'
67:                     or payload.get('role') != 'assistant' or payload.get('phase') not in ('commentary', 'final_answer')):
68:                 continue
69:             observed = _time(record['timestamp'])
70:             if observed < since or observed > now:
71:                 continue
72:             delivery_id = payload.get('id')
73:             if not isinstance(delivery_id, str) or not delivery_id:
74:                 continue
75:             text = '\n'.join(item['text'] for item in payload.get('content', [])
76:                              if item.get('type') == 'output_text' and isinstance(item.get('text'), str))
77:             if not _contains_line(text, expected):
78:                 continue
79:             return {'txId':tx['txId'], 'status':tx['status'], 'enqueuedAt':tx['enqueuedAt'],
80:                     'source':'codex-session-message', 'sessionId':thread, 'deliveryId':delivery_id,
81:                     'observedAt':record['timestamp'], 'contentSha256':hashlib.sha256(text.encode()).hexdigest()}
82:         except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, AttributeError):
83:             continue
84:     raise StoreError('No matching emitted host message in bounded transcript tail; notification remains pending')

FILE plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/policy.py
1: """Pure next-action assessment for the Moriarty development plugin.
2: 
3: ``assess(snapshot, action)`` is deterministic and has no I/O. It does not
4: grant admission. Booleans and integers are distinct: ``bool`` is not ``int``.
5: Unknown keys, missing keys and wrong types yield ``INPUT_INVALID``.
6: 
7: Check order for a well-typed input:
8: 
9: 1. unknown action kind
10: 2. read-only ``report`` (always allowed)
11: 3. current authority
12: 4. current candidate bytes/hash
13: 5. task entry eligibility
14: 6. resource admission
15: 7. named admission/command gaps
16: 8. unresolved operational history
17: 9. primary implementation reservation
18: 10. repeated same-defect failures
19: 11. administrative cycle/time limits
20: 12. allow
21: 
22: A focused repair after two same-defect failures requires both
23: ``reproducerVerified`` and ``approachChanged``. Repair occupies the primary
24: implementation slot. Report remains allowed when dispatch is denied.
25: Reproduce remains allowed for repeated-failure, history, admin and
26: concurrency limits, but not for admission/command gaps.
27: """
28: from __future__ import annotations
29: 
30: ACTION_KEYS = (
31:     "id",
32:     "requirement",
33:     "capability",
34:     "kind",
35:     "candidate",
36:     "admissionRef",
37:     "commandRef",
38:     "evidenceProfile",
39: )
40: SNAPSHOT_KEYS = (
41:     "authorityCurrent",
42:     "entryEligible",
43:     "candidateCurrent",
44:     "resourceAdmitted",
45:     "sameDefectFailures",
46:     "adminCycles",
47:     "adminSeconds",
48:     "primaryActive",
49:     "reproducerVerified",
50:     "approachChanged",
51:     "nextActionId",
52:     "missingEvidence",
53: )
54: DECISION_KEYS = ("allow", "reasonCode", "nextActionId", "missingEvidence")
55: KNOWN_KINDS = frozenset(
56:     ("implement", "reproduce", "repair", "verify", "review", "admin", "report")
57: )
58: BOOL_SNAPSHOT_KEYS = (
59:     "authorityCurrent",
60:     "entryEligible",
61:     "candidateCurrent",
62:     "resourceAdmitted",
63:     "primaryActive",
64:     "reproducerVerified",
65:     "approachChanged",
66: )
67: INT_SNAPSHOT_KEYS = ("sameDefectFailures", "adminCycles", "adminSeconds")
68: HISTORY_EXEMPT_KINDS = frozenset(("report", "reproduce", "review", "verify"))
69: PRIMARY_BLOCKED_KINDS = frozenset(("implement", "repair", "admin"))
70: RETRY_BLOCKED_KINDS = frozenset(("implement", "admin"))
71: ADMIN_SECONDS_LIMIT = 1800
72: ADMIN_CYCLE_LIMIT = 2
73: FAILURE_RETRY_LIMIT = 2
74: 
75: 
76: def assess(snapshot, action):
77:     problems = []
78:     snap_ok = _validate_snapshot(snapshot, problems)
79:     act_ok = _validate_action(action, problems)
80:     if not snap_ok or not act_ok:
81:         return _decision(False, "INPUT_INVALID", None, problems)
82: 
83:     kind = action["kind"]
84:     missing = list(snapshot["missingEvidence"])
85:     next_id = snapshot["nextActionId"]
86:     if kind not in KNOWN_KINDS:
87:         return _decision(False, "UNKNOWN_KIND", next_id, missing)
88:     if kind == "report":
89:         return _decision(True, "ALLOWED", next_id, missing)
90:     if snapshot["authorityCurrent"] is False:
91:         return _decision(False, "AUTHORITY_UNAVAILABLE", next_id, missing)
92:     if snapshot["candidateCurrent"] is False:
93:         return _decision(False, "EVIDENCE_STALE", next_id, missing)
94:     if snapshot["entryEligible"] is False:
95:         return _decision(False, "ENTRY_INELIGIBLE", next_id, missing)
96:     if snapshot["resourceAdmitted"] is False:
97:         return _decision(False, "RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED", next_id, missing)
98:     if _admission_gap(missing):
99:         return _decision(False, "ADMISSION_UNRESOLVED", next_id, missing)
100:     if _history_gap(missing) and kind not in HISTORY_EXEMPT_KINDS:
101:         return _decision(False, "HISTORY_UNRESOLVED", next_id, missing)
102:     if snapshot["primaryActive"] is True and kind in PRIMARY_BLOCKED_KINDS:
103:         return _decision(False, "PRIMARY_ACTIVE", next_id, missing)
104:     same_failures = snapshot.get("sameDefectFailures")
105:     if same_failures is not None and same_failures >= FAILURE_RETRY_LIMIT:
106:         if kind in RETRY_BLOCKED_KINDS:
107:             return _decision(False, "REPRODUCE_BEFORE_RETRY", next_id, missing)
108:         if kind == "repair":
109:             ready = (
110:                 snapshot["reproducerVerified"] is True
111:                 and snapshot["approachChanged"] is True
112:             )
113:             if not ready:
114:                 return _decision(False, "REPAIR_UNREADY", next_id, missing)
115:     if kind == "admin" and _admin_limit(snapshot):
116:         return _decision(False, "ADMIN_LIMIT", next_id, missing)
117:     return _decision(True, "ALLOWED", next_id, missing)
118: 
119: 
120: def _decision(allow, reason_code, next_action_id, missing_evidence):
121:     return {
122:         "allow": allow,
123:         "reasonCode": reason_code,
124:         "nextActionId": next_action_id,
125:         "missingEvidence": list(missing_evidence),
126:     }
127: 
128: 
129: def _validate_snapshot(snapshot, problems):
130:     if type(snapshot) is not dict:
131:         problems.append("snapshot-not-object")
132:         return False
133:     extra = [key for key in snapshot if key not in SNAPSHOT_KEYS]
134:     absent = [key for key in SNAPSHOT_KEYS if key not in snapshot]
135:     for key in extra:
136:         problems.append("unknown-snapshot-field:" + key)
137:     for key in absent:
138:         problems.append("missing-snapshot-field:" + key)
139:     if extra or absent:
140:         return False
141:     valid = True
142:     for key in BOOL_SNAPSHOT_KEYS:
143:         if type(snapshot[key]) is not bool:
144:             problems.append("snapshot-type:" + key)
145:             valid = False
146:     has_hist_gap = _history_gap(snapshot.get("missingEvidence", [])) if isinstance(snapshot.get("missingEvidence"), list) else False
147:     for key in INT_SNAPSHOT_KEYS:
148:         value = snapshot[key]
149:         if has_hist_gap and value is None:
150:             continue
151:         if value is None and key in ("adminCycles", "adminSeconds"):
152:             continue
153:         if type(value) is not int or value < 0:
154:             problems.append("snapshot-type:" + key)
155:             valid = False
156:     next_id = snapshot["nextActionId"]
157:     if next_id is not None and type(next_id) is not str:
158:         problems.append("snapshot-type:nextActionId")
159:         valid = False
160:     evidence = snapshot["missingEvidence"]
161:     if type(evidence) is not list:
162:         problems.append("snapshot-type:missingEvidence")
163:         valid = False
164:     elif any(type(item) is not str for item in evidence):
165:         problems.append("snapshot-type:missingEvidence")
166:         valid = False
167:     return valid
168: 
169: 
170: def _validate_action(action, problems):
171:     if type(action) is not dict:
172:         problems.append("action-not-object")
173:         return False
174:     extra = [key for key in action if key not in ACTION_KEYS]
175:     absent = [key for key in ACTION_KEYS if key not in action]
176:     for key in extra:
177:         problems.append("unknown-action-field:" + key)
178:     for key in absent:
179:         problems.append("missing-action-field:" + key)
180:     if extra or absent:
181:         return False
182:     valid = True
183:     for key in ACTION_KEYS:
184:         if type(action[key]) is not str:
185:             problems.append("action-type:" + key)
186:             valid = False
187:     return valid
188: 
189: 
190: def _admission_gap(missing):
191:     markers = (
192:         "commandRef-",
193:         "admissionRef-",
194:         "duplicate-action-id:",
195:         "unknown-actions-field:",
196:         "actions-schema-unresolved:",
197:         "binding-schema-",
198:         "campaign-schema-unresolved:",
199:         "unknown-command-field:",
200:         "unknown-nested-",
201:         "entryGate-requires-unresolved",
202:     )
203:     for item in missing:
204:         if item.startswith(markers):
205:             return True
206:     return False
207: 
208: 
209: def _history_gap(missing: list) -> bool:
210:     return any(
211:         isinstance(item, str) and (item == "operational-history" or item.startswith("operational-history"))
212:         for item in missing
213:     )
214: 
215: 
216: def _admin_limit(snapshot):
217:     cycles = snapshot.get("adminCycles")
218:     seconds = snapshot.get("adminSeconds")
219:     return (
220:         (cycles is not None and cycles >= ADMIN_CYCLE_LIMIT)
221:         or (seconds is not None and seconds >= ADMIN_SECONDS_LIMIT)
222:     )

FILE plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/records.py
1: """Exact-source readers for Moriarty development plugin snapshots.
2: 
3: ``load_snapshot(repo, action_id, history_reader=None)`` and ``read_actions(repo)``
4: read current bytes. They never write, never launch ``commandRef`` argv, never
5: import referenced code, and never open secret paths. Missing or conflicting
6: sources become named ``missingEvidence`` strings. Missing operational history
7: is ``operational-history`` and is not a verified zero-failure count.
8: 
9: This module does not grant admission. New action mappings cannot create
10: acceptance or resource permission. Existing program, sprint and campaign
11: records remain authoritative.
12: 
13: On-disk reader contract (paths are relative to the supplied repository root):
14: 
15: - ``.moriarty-dev/actions.json``
16:   Closed object. Required keys: ``schema``, ``actions``.
17:   ``schema`` must be ``moriarty-dev.actions/1``.
18:   ``actions`` is a list of closed action objects with exactly:
19:   ``id``, ``requirement``, ``capability``, ``kind``, ``candidate``,
20:   ``admissionRef``, ``commandRef``, ``evidenceProfile`` (all ``str``).
21:   Duplicate ids, duplicate JSON keys, unknown keys and non-string values
22:   are unresolved. The catalog maps references. It is not an acceptance
23:   register.
24: 
25: - ``openspec/moriarty-completion-program.json``
26:   ``schemaVersion`` must be the integer ``1``. Task eligibility uses
27:   ``reportReconciliation.stageAdmission`` stages and sprint ``entryGates``.
28:   ``stageAdmission`` and each stage are closed shapes. ``dispatchEnabled``
29:   never admits work. Whole-sprint ``status`` never decides entry.
30: 
31: - ``openspec/sprints/sprints.json``
32:   ``schemaVersion`` must be the integer ``1``. Entry uses the gate that
33:   lists the action ``requirement``. ``requires`` and ``campaignOwner`` are
34:   required. A blocked ``f0`` stage does not make an SP05 task ineligible
35:   unless that task's own gate lists ``f0``.
36: 
37: - Campaign store from ``stageAdmission.campaignRecordStore``.
38:   Schema ``moriarty.campaign-admission/1``. ``admissionRef`` form
39:   ``campaign:<id>``. The record is not permission. The action gate stage
40:   and campaignOwner must match the current campaign, the program stage
41:   ``campaignRecordId``, and the accepted profile. Complete historical
42:   design records do not admit source implementation.
43: 
44: - Binding files: schema ``moriarty.sp01-execution-binding/1`` only.
45:   Bytes must match ``bindingSha256``. Binding inputs/ownedFiles and
46:   authority are references, not self-authenticating permission.
47: 
48: - Candidate currentness uses ``candidateManifest`` (candidateSha256,
49:   digestAlgorithm, ownedFiles, inputs) plus acceptance/review when
50:   present. Frozen owned-file digests are the candidate commitment.
51:   ``unchangedPayloadHashes`` must equal those freeze digests; they
52:   cannot rewrite them. A publication bridge authenticates original
53:   freeze bytes. Only the demonstrated ``subsets.RP01-MC02`` transform
54:   may differ: nested design-only acceptance, candidate/review metadata,
55:   frozen-reviewed-design outputs, status ``complete``, and the exact
56:   pending-review closure replacement. Nested acceptance is closed.
57:   Extra approval flags, unbound candidateHashStatus, unsupported
58:   output statuses and blanket obligation-closure claims reject.
59:   Missing original bytes are unavailable. Prerequisites reuse binding,
60:   candidate, review and acceptance checks. ``binding.stage``, campaign
61:   ``stage`` and required ``stage.id`` must be identical. Accepted
62:   profile comes from a hash-verified ``moriarty-bounds/1`` input
63:   ``semanticProfile``. Loan-design evidence cannot satisfy
64:   atomic-prepare, atomic-accept or finalized settlement. Acceptance
65:   status is ``complete-design-subset-only``. Review pass is
66:   ``APPROVED`` with scope ``RP01-MC02 specified design consistency
67:   only``. Unknown complete-* statuses stay unresolved.
68: 
69: - Resource files: authority path, matching scope and a positive integer
70:   allocation or additionalSeconds. Live remaining uses an explicit
71:   ``currentAccounting`` current-runner record of family
72:   ``moriarty.supervised-accounting/1``, never
73:   ``resourceRuntimeSnapshot``. Planning charges, reservations,
74:   amendments, dispatch limits and the action's bounded cost apply. A
75:   historical integer snapshot is not credit. The existing runner owns
76:   charging.
77: 
78: - ``commandRef``: empty only for ``report``. Actionful kinds need
79:   ``<contained-json>#<name>`` with schema ``moriarty-dev.commands/1`` and
80:   a nonempty argv list of strings. The argv list is never executed.
81: 
82: Reads are bounded at 8 MiB. Duplicate JSON keys are rejected. Contained
83: reads apply to fixed program/sprint files and references. Resolved path
84: components are checked for secret names before opening.
85: 
86: ``history_reader`` is ``reader({"repository", "requirement", "capability"})``.
87: ``repository`` is the resolved git-common-dir. Source reads use the
88: selected worktree. Absence of a reader is ``operational-history``.
89: ``nextActionId`` is selected from currently admitted catalog actions in
90: order reproduce, repair, review, verify, implement, admin, report.
91: Named absence is ``next-action-none``.
92: """
93: from __future__ import annotations
94: 
95: from datetime import datetime
96: import hashlib
97: import json
98: import os
99: from pathlib import Path
100:

416: class DuplicateKeyError(ValueError):
417:     """JSON object contained a repeated key."""

420: def load_snapshot(repo, action_id, history_reader=None):
421:     missing = []
422:     root = Path(repo)
423:     try:
424:         root = root.resolve()
425:     except OSError:
426:         missing.append("repository-unresolved:" + str(repo))
427:         return _snapshot(missing=missing)
428:     if not root.is_dir():
429:         missing.append("repository-unresolved:" + str(repo))
430:         return _snapshot(missing=missing)
431: 
432:     actions = _load_actions_catalog(root, missing)
433:     action = _find_action(actions, action_id, missing)
434:     program = _load_register(root, PROGRAM_REL, missing)
435:     sprints = _load_register(root, SPRINTS_REL, missing)
436:     program_ok = _validate_program(program, missing)
437:     sprints_ok = _validate_sprints(sprints, missing)
438:     campaign_rel = DEFAULT_CAMPAIGN_REL
439:     if program_ok:
440:         campaign_rel = program["reportReconciliation"]["stageAdmission"][
441:             "campaignRecordStore"
442:         ]
443:         if type(campaign_rel) is not str or campaign_rel == "":
444:             missing.append("campaignRecordStore-unresolved")
445:             campaign_rel = DEFAULT_CAMPAIGN_REL
446:             program_ok = False
447:     campaigns = None
448:     campaign_ok = False
449:     campaign_path = _contained_file(root, campaign_rel, missing, "campaign-store")
450:     if campaign_path is not None:
451:         campaigns = _load_json_path(campaign_path, missing, campaign_rel)
452:         campaign_ok = _validate_campaigns(root, campaigns, missing)
453: 
454:     snap = {
455:         "authorityCurrent": False,
456:         "entryEligible": False,
457:         "candidateCurrent": False,
458:         "resourceAdmitted": False,
459:         "sameDefectFailures": 0,
460:         "adminCycles": 0,
461:         "adminSeconds": 0,
462:         "primaryActive": False,
463:         "reproducerVerified": False,
464:         "approachChanged": False,
465:         "nextActionId": None,
466:         "missingEvidence": missing,
467:     }
468:     if action is not None:
469:         flags = _action_flags(
470:             root,
471:             action,
472:             program,
473:             sprints,
474:             campaigns,
475:             program_ok,
476:             sprints_ok,
477:             campaign_ok,
478:             missing,
479:         )
480:         snap.update(flags)
481:         _apply_history(history_reader, root, action, snap, missing)
482:         history_fields = {
483:             "sameDefectFailures": snap["sameDefectFailures"],
484:             "adminCycles": snap["adminCycles"],
485:             "adminSeconds": snap["adminSeconds"],
486:             "primaryActive": snap["primaryActive"],
487:             "reproducerVerified": snap["reproducerVerified"],
488:             "approachChanged": snap["approachChanged"],
489:         }
490:         snap["nextActionId"] = _select_next(
491:             root,
492:             actions,
493:             action,
494:             history_fields,
495:             history_reader,
496:             program,
497:             sprints,
498:             campaigns,
499:             program_ok,
500:             sprints_ok,
501:             campaign_ok,
502:             missing,
503:         )
504:     else:
505:         if history_reader is None:
506:             missing.append("operational-history")
507:     snap["missingEvidence"] = list(missing)
508:     return _snapshot(**snap)

511: def read_actions(repo):
512:     missing = []
513:     try:
514:         root = Path(repo).resolve()
515:     except OSError:
516:         raise ValueError("actions-unresolved:repository")
517:     actions = _load_actions_catalog(root, missing)
518:     if missing and not actions:
519:         raise ValueError("actions-unresolved:" + ",".join(missing))
520:     return actions
