522: 
523: def _snapshot(**fields):
524:     missing = list(fields.get("missingEvidence", fields.get("missing", [])))
525:     values = {
526:         "authorityCurrent": fields.get("authorityCurrent", False),
527:         "entryEligible": fields.get("entryEligible", False),
528:         "candidateCurrent": fields.get("candidateCurrent", False),
529:         "resourceAdmitted": fields.get("resourceAdmitted", False),
530:         "sameDefectFailures": fields.get("sameDefectFailures", 0),
531:         "adminCycles": fields.get("adminCycles", 0),
532:         "adminSeconds": fields.get("adminSeconds", 0),
533:         "primaryActive": fields.get("primaryActive", False),
534:         "reproducerVerified": fields.get("reproducerVerified", False),
535:         "approachChanged": fields.get("approachChanged", False),
536:         "nextActionId": fields.get("nextActionId", None),
537:         "missingEvidence": missing,
538:     }
539:     return {key: values[key] for key in SNAPSHOT_KEYS}
540: 
541: 
542: def _object_pairs(pairs):
543:     obj = {}
544:     for key, value in pairs:
545:         if key in obj:
546:             raise DuplicateKeyError(key)
547:         obj[key] = value
548:     return obj
549: 
550: 
551: def _load_register(root, relative, missing):
552:     path = _contained_file(root, relative, missing, relative)
553:     if path is None:
554:         if not any(relative in item for item in missing):
555:             missing.append(relative)
556:         return None
557:     return _load_json_path(path, missing, relative)
558: 
559: 
560: def _load_json_path(path, missing, label):
561:     data = _read_bytes(path, missing, label)
562:     if data is None:
563:         return None
564:     return _parse_json(data, missing, label)
565: 
566: 
567: def _read_bytes(path, missing, label):
568:     try:
569:         with open(path, "rb") as handle:
570:             data = handle.read(MAX_BYTES + 1)
571:     except OSError:
572:         missing.append("read-failed:" + label)
573:         return None
574:     if len(data) > MAX_BYTES:
575:         missing.append("bounded-read-exceeded:" + label)
576:         return None
577:     return data
578: 
579: 
580: def _parse_json(data, missing, label):
581:     try:
582:         text = data.decode("utf-8")
583:     except UnicodeDecodeError:
584:         missing.append("encoding:" + label)
585:         return None
586:     try:
587:         return json.loads(text, object_pairs_hook=_object_pairs)
588:     except DuplicateKeyError as exc:
589:         missing.append("duplicate-key:" + label + ":" + str(exc))
590:         return None
591:     except json.JSONDecodeError:
592:         missing.append("json:" + label)
593:         return None
594: 
595: 
596: def _load_actions_catalog(root, missing):
597:     path = _contained_file(root, ACTIONS_REL, missing, "actions")
598:     if path is None:
599:         return []
600:     payload = _load_json_path(path, missing, ACTIONS_REL)
601:     if type(payload) is not dict:
602:         if payload is not None:
603:             missing.append("actions-not-object")
604:         return []
605:     extra = [key for key in payload if key not in ("schema", "actions")]
606:     for key in extra:
607:         missing.append("unknown-actions-field:" + key)
608:     schema = payload.get("schema")
609:     if schema != ACTIONS_SCHEMA:
610:         missing.append("actions-schema-unresolved:" + str(schema))
611:         return []
612:     rows = payload.get("actions")
613:     if type(rows) is not list:
614:         missing.append("actions-not-list")
615:         return []
616:     actions = []
617:     seen = set()
618:     for index, row in enumerate(rows):
619:         parsed = _closed_action(row, missing, index)
620:         if parsed is None:
621:             continue
622:         if parsed["id"] in seen:
623:             missing.append("duplicate-action-id:" + parsed["id"])
624:             continue
625:         seen.add(parsed["id"])
626:         actions.append(parsed)
627:     return actions
628: 
629: 
630: def _closed_action(row, missing, index):
631:     if type(row) is not dict:
632:         missing.append("action-not-object:" + str(index))
633:         return None
634:     extra = [key for key in row if key not in ACTION_KEYS]
635:     absent = [key for key in ACTION_KEYS if key not in row]
636:     for key in extra:
637:         missing.append("unknown-action-field:" + key)
638:     for key in absent:
639:         missing.append("missing-action-field:" + key)
640:     if extra or absent:
641:         return None
642:     for key in ACTION_KEYS:
643:         if type(row[key]) is not str:
644:             missing.append("action-field-type:" + key)
645:             return None
646:     return {key: row[key] for key in ACTION_KEYS}
647: 
648: 
649: def _find_action(actions, action_id, missing):
650:     if type(action_id) is not str:
651:         missing.append("action-id-not-str")
652:         return None
653:     for row in actions:
654:         if row["id"] == action_id:
655:             return row
656:     missing.append("action-unresolved:" + action_id)
657:     return None
658: 
659: 
660: def _validate_program(program, missing):
661:     if program is None:
662:         return False
663:     if type(program) is not dict:
664:         missing.append("program-not-object")
665:         return False
666:     extra_program = [key for key in program if key not in PROGRAM_KEYS]
667:     for key in extra_program:
668:         missing.append("unknown-program-field:" + key)
669:     if extra_program:
670:         return False
671:     if "schemaVersion" not in program:
672:         missing.append("schemaVersion-missing")
673:         return False
674:     if type(program["schemaVersion"]) is not int:
675:         missing.append("schemaVersion-not-int")
676:         return False
677:     if program["schemaVersion"] != PROGRAM_VERSION:
678:         missing.append("schemaVersion-unsupported:" + str(program["schemaVersion"]))
679:         return False
680:     reconciliation = program.get("reportReconciliation")
681:     if type(reconciliation) is not dict:
682:         missing.append("reportReconciliation-unresolved")
683:         return False
684:     admission = reconciliation.get("stageAdmission")
685:     if type(admission) is not dict:
686:         missing.append("stageAdmission-unresolved")
687:         return False
688:     extra = [key for key in admission if key not in STAGE_ADMISSION_KEYS]
689:     for key in extra:
690:         missing.append("unknown-stageAdmission-field:" + key)
691:     if extra:
692:         return False
693:     if type(admission.get("dispatchEnabled")) is not bool:
694:         missing.append("dispatchEnabled-not-bool")
695:         return False
696:     if type(admission.get("campaignRecordStore")) is not str:
697:         missing.append("campaignRecordStore-not-str")
698:         return False
699:     if type(admission.get("rule")) is not str:
700:         missing.append("stageAdmission-unresolved")
701:         return False
702:     if admission.get("schema") != STAGE_ADMISSION_SCHEMA:
703:         missing.append(
704:             "stageAdmission-schema-unresolved:" + str(admission.get("schema"))
705:         )
706:         return False
707:     stages = admission.get("stages")
708:     if type(stages) is not list:
709:         missing.append("stages-not-list")
710:         return False
711:     valid = True
712:     seen = set()
713:     for stage in stages:
714:         if type(stage) is not dict:
715:             missing.append("stage-not-object")
716:             valid = False
717:             continue
718:         extra_stage = [key for key in stage if key not in STAGE_KEYS]
719:         for key in extra_stage:
720:             missing.append("unknown-nested-stage-field:" + key)
721:             valid = False
722:         absent = [key for key in STAGE_KEYS if key not in stage]
723:         for key in absent:
724:             missing.append("missing-stage-field:" + key)
725:             valid = False
726:         if extra_stage or absent:
727:             continue
728:         stage_id = stage.get("id")
729:         if type(stage_id) is not str:
730:             missing.append("stage-id-not-str")
731:             valid = False
732:             continue
733:         if stage_id in seen:
734:             missing.append("duplicate-stage:" + stage_id)
735:             valid = False
736:         seen.add(stage_id)
737:         if type(stage.get("status")) is not str:
738:             missing.append("stage-status-not-str:" + stage_id)
739:             valid = False
740:         owners = stage.get("owners")
741:         if type(owners) is not list or any(type(item) is not str for item in owners):
742:             missing.append("stage-owners-unresolved:" + stage_id)
743:             valid = False
744:         requires = stage.get("requires")
745:         if type(requires) is not list or any(type(item) is not str for item in requires):
746:             missing.append("stage-requires-unresolved:" + stage_id)
747:             valid = False
748:         if type(stage.get("purpose")) is not str:
749:             missing.append("stage-purpose-unresolved:" + stage_id)
750:             valid = False
751:         for field in ("acceptedProfile", "candidateHash", "campaignRecordId"):
752:             value = stage.get(field)
753:             if value is not None and type(value) is not str:
754:                 missing.append("stage-" + field + "-type:" + stage_id)
755:                 valid = False
756:     return valid
757: 
758: 
759: def _validate_sprints(sprints, missing):
760:     if sprints is None:
761:         return False
762:     if type(sprints) is not dict:
763:         missing.append("sprints-not-object")
764:         return False
765:     if type(sprints.get("schemaVersion")) is not int:
766:         missing.append("sprints-schemaVersion-not-int")
767:         return False
768:     if sprints.get("schemaVersion") != SPRINTS_VERSION:
769:         missing.append("sprints-schemaVersion-unsupported:" + str(sprints.get("schemaVersion")))
770:         return False
771:     if type(sprints.get("dispatchEnabled")) is not bool:
772:         missing.append("sprints-dispatchEnabled-not-bool")
773:         return False
774:     if type(sprints.get("resourceAllocationGranted")) is not bool:
775:         missing.append("sprints-resourceAllocationGranted-not-bool")
776:         return False
777:     rows = sprints.get("sprints")
778:     if type(rows) is not list:
779:         missing.append("sprints-list-unresolved")
780:         return False
781:     valid = True
782:     for sprint in rows:
783:         if type(sprint) is not dict:
784:             missing.append("sprint-not-object")
785:             valid = False
786:             continue
787:         gates = sprint.get("entryGates", [])
788:         if type(gates) is not list:
789:             missing.append("entryGates-not-list:" + str(sprint.get("id")))
790:             valid = False
791:             continue
792:         for gate in gates:
793:             if type(gate) is not dict:
794:                 missing.append("entryGate-not-object")
795:                 valid = False
796:                 continue
797:             extra = [key for key in gate if key not in GATE_KEYS]
798:             for key in extra:
799:                 missing.append("unknown-entryGate-field:" + key)
800:                 valid = False
801:             if "requires" not in gate:
802:                 missing.append("entryGate-requires-unresolved")
803:                 valid = False
804:             if "campaignOwner" not in gate:
805:                 missing.append("entryGate-campaignOwner-unresolved")
806:                 valid = False
807:             if "stage" not in gate or type(gate.get("stage")) is not str:
808:                 missing.append("entryGate-stage-unresolved")
809:                 valid = False
810:             tasks = gate.get("tasks", [])
811:             requires = gate.get("requires")
812:             owners = gate.get("campaignOwner")
813:             if type(tasks) is not list or any(type(item) is not str for item in tasks):
814:                 missing.append("entryGate-tasks-unresolved")
815:                 valid = False
816:             if requires is not None and (
817:                 type(requires) is not list
818:                 or any(type(item) is not str for item in requires)
819:             ):
820:                 missing.append("entryGate-requires-unresolved")
821:                 valid = False
822:             if owners is not None and (
823:                 type(owners) is not list
824:                 or any(type(item) is not str for item in owners)
825:             ):
826:                 missing.append("entryGate-campaignOwner-unresolved")
827:                 valid = False
828:     return valid
829: 
830: 
831: def _validate_campaigns(root, campaigns, missing):
832:     if campaigns is None:
833:         return False
834:     if type(campaigns) is not dict:
835:         missing.append("campaign-store-not-object")
836:         return False
837:     extra = [key for key in campaigns if key not in ("schema", "campaigns")]
838:     for key in extra:
839:         missing.append("unknown-campaign-store-field:" + key)
840:     schema = campaigns.get("schema")
841:     if schema != CAMPAIGN_SCHEMA:
842:         missing.append("campaign-schema-unresolved:" + str(schema))
843:         return False
844:     table = campaigns.get("campaigns")
845:     if type(table) is not dict:
846:         missing.append("campaigns-not-object")
847:         return False
848:     valid = extra == []
849:     for campaign_id, record in table.items():
850:         if type(campaign_id) is not str or campaign_id == "":
851:             missing.append("campaign-id-unresolved")
852:             valid = False
853:             continue
854:         if type(record) is not dict:
855:             missing.append("campaign-not-object:" + campaign_id)
856:             valid = False
857:             continue
858:         extra_record = [key for key in record if key not in CAMPAIGN_RECORD_KEYS]
859:         for key in extra_record:
860:             missing.append("unknown-campaign-field:" + key)
861:             valid = False
862:         if HISTORICAL_K_FIELDS.intersection(record):
863:             if not _validate_historical_k(root, campaign_id, record, missing):
864:                 valid = False
865:     return valid
866: 
867: 
868: def _validate_historical_k(root, campaign_id, record, missing):
869:     """Read the closed observation family without promoting it to admission.
870: 
871:     Provenance paths must remain available and contained; their contents and the
872:     retained counters do not replace binding, candidate, review or live accounting
873:     verification. The three historical statuses cannot authorize actionful work.
874:     """
875:     valid = True
876:     if not _status_in(record.get("status"), HISTORICAL_K_STATUSES):
877:         missing.append("historical-campaign-status-unsupported:" + campaign_id)
878:         valid = False
879:     if not HISTORICAL_K_FIELDS.issubset(record):
880:         missing.append("historical-campaign-observation-incomplete:" + campaign_id)
881:         valid = False
882:     for field in ("admission", "result"):
883:         if _contained_file(root, record.get(field), missing, "historical-campaign-" + field) is None:
884:             valid = False
885:     consumed = record.get("consumed")
886:     if (type(consumed) is not dict or set(consumed) != HISTORICAL_K_COUNTERS
887:             or any(type(value) is not int or value < 0 for value in consumed.values())):
888:         missing.append("historical-campaign-consumed-invalid:" + campaign_id)
889:         valid = False
890:     return valid
891: 
892: 
893: def _program_stages(program):
894:     stages = {}
895:     rows = program["reportReconciliation"]["stageAdmission"]["stages"]
896:     for stage in rows:
897:         if type(stage) is dict and type(stage.get("id")) is str:
898:             stages[stage["id"]] = stage
899:     return stages
900: 
901: 
902: def _find_gate(sprints, requirement):
903:     for sprint in sprints.get("sprints", []):
904:         if type(sprint) is not dict:
905:             continue
906:         for gate in sprint.get("entryGates", []):
907:             if type(gate) is not dict:
908:                 continue
909:             tasks = gate.get("tasks", [])
910:             if type(tasks) is list and requirement in tasks:
911:                 return gate
912:     return None
913: 
914: 
915: def _entry_eligible(sprints, program, campaigns, root, requirement, missing):
916:     gate = _find_gate(sprints, requirement)
917:     if gate is None:
918:         missing.append("entryGate-unresolved:" + requirement)
919:         return False
920:     if "requires" not in gate:
921:         missing.append("entryGate-requires-unresolved")
922:         return False
923:     requires = gate.get("requires")
924:     if type(requires) is not list or any(type(item) is not str for item in requires):
925:         missing.append("entryGate-requires-unresolved")
926:         return False
927:     stages = _program_stages(program)
928:     visiting = set()
929:     done = set()
930:     for required in requires:
931:         if not _stage_complete(
932:             required, stages, campaigns, root, missing, visiting, done
933:         ):
934:             return False
935:     return True
936: 
937: 
938: def _stage_complete(stage_id, stages, campaigns, root, missing, visiting, done):
939:     if stage_id in done:
940:         return True
941:     if stage_id in visiting:
942:         missing.append("stage-cycle:" + stage_id)
943:         return False
944:     stage = stages.get(stage_id)
945:     if stage is None:
946:         missing.append("required-stage-missing:" + stage_id)
947:         return False
948:     if stage.get("status") != "complete":
949:         missing.append(
950:             "required-stage-incomplete:" + stage_id + ":" + str(stage.get("status"))
951:         )
952:         return False
953:     for field in ("acceptedProfile", "candidateHash", "campaignRecordId"):
954:         value = stage.get(field)
955:         if type(value) is not str or value == "":
956:             missing.append("required-stage-evidence-missing:" + stage_id + ":" + field)
957:             return False
958:     if not _prerequisite_campaign(stage, campaigns, root, missing):
959:         return False
960:     requires = stage.get("requires")
961:     if type(requires) is not list:
962:         missing.append("stage-requires-unresolved:" + stage_id)
963:         return False
964:     visiting.add(stage_id)
965:     for req in requires:
966:         if not _stage_complete(
967:             req, stages, campaigns, root, missing, visiting, done
968:         ):
969:             visiting.discard(stage_id)
970:             return False
971:     visiting.discard(stage_id)
972:     done.add(stage_id)
973:     return True
974: 
975: 
976: def _status_in(status, allowed):
977:     return type(status) is str and status in allowed
978: 
979: 
980: def _campaign_is_complete(status):
981:     return _status_in(status, CAMPAIGN_COMPLETE)
982: 
983: 
984: def _acceptance_is_complete(status):
985:     return _status_in(status, ACCEPTANCE_COMPLETE)
986: 
987: 
988: def _prerequisite_campaign(stage, campaigns, root, missing):
989:     stage_id = stage["id"]
990:     campaign_id = stage.get("campaignRecordId")
991:     if type(campaign_id) is not str or campaign_id == "":
992:         missing.append("required-stage-evidence-missing:" + stage_id + ":campaignRecordId")
993:         return False
994:     if campaigns is None or type(campaigns.get("campaigns")) is not dict:
995:         missing.append("required-stage-campaign-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
996:         return False
997:     record = campaigns["campaigns"].get(campaign_id)
998:     if type(record) is not dict:
999:         missing.append("required-stage-campaign-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1000:         return False
1001:     rec_hash = record.get("candidateHash")
1002:     if rec_hash is None:
1003:         rec_hash = record.get("candidateSha256")
1004:     stage_hash = stage.get("candidateHash")
1005:     if type(rec_hash) is not str or rec_hash == "":
1006:         missing.append("required-stage-candidate-unresolved:" + stage_id)
1007:         return False
1008:     if type(stage_hash) is not str or stage_hash == "":
1009:         missing.append("required-stage-evidence-missing:" + stage_id + ":candidateHash")
1010:         return False
1011:     if rec_hash.lower() != stage_hash.lower():
1012:         missing.append("required-stage-candidate-conflict:" + stage_id)
1013:         return False
1014:     rec_profile = record.get("acceptedProfile")
1015:     stage_profile = stage.get("acceptedProfile")
1016:     if type(rec_profile) is not str or rec_profile == "":
1017:         missing.append("required-stage-profile-unresolved:" + stage_id)
1018:         return False
1019:     if type(stage_profile) is not str or stage_profile == "" or rec_profile != stage_profile:
1020:         missing.append("required-stage-profile-conflict:" + stage_id)
1021:         return False
1022:     rec_stage = record.get("stage")
1023:     if rec_stage != stage_id:
1024:         missing.append("required-stage-mismatch:" + stage_id)
1025:         return False
1026:     rec_status = record.get("status")
1027:     if rec_status == "blocked" or not _campaign_is_complete(rec_status):
1028:         missing.append("required-stage-campaign-status:" + stage_id + ":" + str(rec_status))
1029:         return False
1030:     rec_owners = _owners_of(record.get("owner"))
1031:     stage_owners = stage.get("owners")
1032:     if type(stage_owners) is not list or not rec_owners.intersection(set(stage_owners)):
1033:         missing.append("required-stage-owner-mismatch:" + stage_id)
1034:         return False
1035:     binding_ok, _binding = _verify_binding(
1036:         root, campaign_id, record, missing, expected_stage=stage_id
1037:     )
1038:     if not binding_ok:
1039:         return False
1040:     return _verify_candidate(root, campaign_id, record, missing)
1041: 
1042: 
1043: def _resolve_command(root, action, missing):
1044:     ref = action["commandRef"]
1045:     if ref == "":
1046:         if action["kind"] in ACTIONFUL_KINDS:
1047:             missing.append("commandRef-unresolved:" + action["id"])
1048:             return False
1049:         return True
1050:     if ref.startswith("/") or ref.startswith("\\") or _has_drive(ref):
1051:         missing.append("commandRef-absolute:" + ref)
1052:         return False
1053:     if "#" not in ref:
1054:         missing.append("commandRef-unresolved:" + ref)
1055:         return False
1056:     file_part, fragment = ref.split("#", 1)
1057:     if file_part == "" or fragment == "":
1058:         missing.append("commandRef-unresolved:" + ref)
1059:         return False
1060:     path = _contained_file(root, file_part, missing, "commandRef")
1061:     if path is None:
1062:         missing.append("commandRef-unresolved:" + ref)
1063:         return False
1064:     payload = _load_json_path(path, missing, file_part)
1065:     if type(payload) is not dict:
1066:         missing.append("commandRef-unresolved:" + ref)
1067:         return False
1068:     extra = [key for key in payload if key not in ("schema", "commands")]
1069:     for key in extra:
1070:         missing.append("unknown-command-field:" + key)
1071:     if payload.get("schema") != COMMANDS_SCHEMA:
1072:         missing.append("commandRef-unsupported-schema:" + str(payload.get("schema")))
1073:         missing.append("commandRef-unresolved:" + ref)
1074:         return False
1075:     commands = payload.get("commands")
1076:     if type(commands) is not dict or fragment not in commands:
1077:         missing.append("commandRef-unresolved:" + ref)
1078:         return False
1079:     spec = commands[fragment]
1080:     if type(spec) is not dict:
1081:         missing.append("commandRef-unresolved:" + ref)
1082:         return False
1083:     # ``argv`` remains a declarative command reference for admission and review.
1084:     # Only the CLI launch boundary requires ``admittedRunner``; accepting argv
1085:     # here must never authorize raw dispatch.
1086:     extra_spec = [key for key in spec if key not in ("argv", "admittedRunner")]
1087:     for key in extra_spec:
1088:         missing.append("unknown-command-field:" + key)
1089:     runner = spec.get("admittedRunner")
1090:     if runner is None:
1091:         argv = spec.get("argv")
1092:         if type(argv) is not list or argv == [] or any(type(item) is not str or item == "" for item in argv):
1093:             missing.append("commandRef-unresolved:" + ref)
1094:             return False
1095:         return extra == [] and extra_spec == []
1096:     if type(runner) is not dict:
1097:         missing.append("commandRef-unresolved:" + ref)
1098:         return False
1099:     runner_extra = [key for key in runner if key not in ("argv", "actionId", "candidateHash", "timeoutSeconds")]
1100:     if runner_extra:
1101:         missing.append("unknown-command-runner-field:" + runner_extra[0])
1102:         return False
1103:     argv = runner.get("argv")
1104:     if (type(argv) is not list or argv == [] or any(type(item) is not str or item == "" for item in argv)
1105:             or type(runner.get("actionId")) is not str or type(runner.get("candidateHash")) is not str
1106:             or type(runner.get("timeoutSeconds")) is not int):
1107:         missing.append("commandRef-unresolved:" + ref)
1108:         return False
1109:     return extra == [] and extra_spec == []
1110: 
1111: 
1112: def _action_flags(
1113:     root,
1114:     action,
1115:     program,
1116:     sprints,
1117:     campaigns,
1118:     program_ok,
1119:     sprints_ok,
1120:     campaign_ok,
1121:     missing,
1122: ):
1123:     entry_eligible = False
1124:     if program_ok and sprints_ok:
1125:         entry_eligible = _entry_eligible(
1126:             sprints,
1127:             program,
1128:             campaigns if campaign_ok else None,
1129:             root,
1130:             action["requirement"],
1131:             missing,
1132:         )
1133:     command_ok = _resolve_command(root, action, missing)
1134:     admitted, candidate_current, resource_admitted = _resolve_admission(
1135:         root,
1136:         action,
1137:         program if program_ok else None,
1138:         sprints if sprints_ok else None,
1139:         campaigns if campaign_ok else None,
1140:         missing,
1141:     )
1142:     authority = bool(
1143:         program_ok
1144:         and sprints_ok
1145:         and campaign_ok
1146:         and command_ok
1147:         and admitted
1148:         and not _structural_block(missing)
1149:     )
1150:     return {
1151:         "authorityCurrent": authority,
1152:         "entryEligible": entry_eligible,
1153:         "candidateCurrent": candidate_current,
1154:         "resourceAdmitted": resource_admitted,
1155:     }
1156: 
1157: 
1158: def _resolve_admission(root, action, program, sprints, campaigns, missing):
1159:     ref = action["admissionRef"]
1160:     if ref.startswith("campaign:"):
1161:         return _resolve_campaign_admission(
1162:             root, action, program, sprints, campaigns, missing
1163:         )
1164:     return _resolve_file_admission(root, ref, missing)
1165: 
1166: 
1167: def _resolve_campaign_admission(root, action, program, sprints, campaigns, missing):
1168:     ref = action["admissionRef"]
1169:     campaign_id = ref[len("campaign:") :]
1170:     if campaign_id == "":
1171:         missing.append("admissionRef-unresolved:campaign:")
1172:         return False, False, False
1173:     if campaigns is None or program is None or sprints is None:
1174:         missing.append("admissionRef-unresolved:" + ref)
1175:         return False, False, False
1176:     table = campaigns.get("campaigns")
1177:     if type(table) is not dict or campaign_id not in table:
1178:         missing.append("admissionRef-unresolved:" + ref)
1179:         return False, False, False
1180:     record = table[campaign_id]
1181:     if type(record) is not dict:
1182:         missing.append("admissionRef-unresolved:" + ref)
1183:         return False, False, False
1184:     gate = _find_gate(sprints, action["requirement"])
1185:     stages = _program_stages(program)
1186:     matched = _campaign_matches_gate(
1187:         record, gate, action, stages, campaign_id, missing
1188:     )
1189:     binding_ok, binding_obj = _verify_binding(
1190:         root, campaign_id, record, missing, expected_stage=gate.get("stage") if gate else None
1191:     )
1192:     candidate_ok = _verify_candidate(root, campaign_id, record, missing)
1193:     resource_ok = _verify_resource(
1194:         root, campaign_id, record, program, binding_obj, action, missing
1195:     )
1196:     return matched and binding_ok, candidate_ok, resource_ok
1197: 
1198: 
1199: def _campaign_matches_gate(record, gate, action, stages, campaign_id, missing):
1200:     if gate is None:
1201:         missing.append("entryGate-unresolved:" + action["requirement"])
1202:         return False
1203:     gate_stage = gate.get("stage")
1204:     if type(gate_stage) is not str or gate_stage == "":
1205:         missing.append("entryGate-stage-unresolved:" + action["requirement"])
1206:         return False
1207:     rec_stage = record.get("stage")
1208:     if type(rec_stage) is not str or rec_stage != gate_stage:
1209:         missing.append("campaign-stage-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
1210:         return False
1211:     owners = gate.get("campaignOwner")
1212:     if type(owners) is not list or any(type(item) is not str for item in owners):
1213:         missing.append("entryGate-campaignOwner-unresolved:" + action["requirement"])
1214:         return False
1215:     rec_owners = _owners_of(record.get("owner"))
1216:     if not rec_owners.intersection(set(owners)):
1217:         missing.append("campaign-owner-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
1218:         return False
1219:     stage = stages.get(gate_stage)
1220:     if stage is None:
1221:         missing.append("action-stage-missing:" + gate_stage)
1222:         return False
1223:     if stage.get("status") == "blocked":
1224:         missing.append("action-stage-blocked:" + gate_stage)
1225:         return False
1226:     record_id = stage.get("campaignRecordId")
1227:     if type(record_id) is not str or record_id == "":
1228:         missing.append("stage-campaignRecordId-null:" + gate_stage)
1229:         return False
1230:     if record_id != campaign_id:
1231:         missing.append("stage-campaignRecordId-mismatch:" + gate_stage)
1232:         return False
1233:     rec_profile = record.get("acceptedProfile")
1234:     stage_profile = stage.get("acceptedProfile")
1235:     status = record.get("status")
1236:     complete = _campaign_is_complete(status)
1237:     if complete:
1238:         if stage.get("status") != "complete":
1239:             missing.append("action-stage-incomplete:" + gate_stage)
1240:             return False
1241:         if type(rec_profile) is not str or rec_profile == "":
1242:             missing.append("acceptedProfile-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1243:             return False
1244:         if type(stage_profile) is not str or stage_profile == "":
1245:             missing.append("stage-acceptedProfile-unresolved:" + gate_stage)
1246:             return False
1247:         if rec_profile != stage_profile:
1248:             missing.append("acceptedProfile-conflict:" + campaign_id)
1249:             return False
1250:     elif (
1251:         type(rec_profile) is str
1252:         and type(stage_profile) is str
1253:         and rec_profile != stage_profile
1254:     ):
1255:         missing.append("acceptedProfile-conflict:" + campaign_id)
1256:         return False
1257:     if action["evidenceProfile"] not in EVIDENCE_PROFILES:
1258:         missing.append("evidenceProfile-unsupported:" + action["evidenceProfile"])
1259:         return False
1260:     if not _evidence_applicable(action["evidenceProfile"], status):
1261:         missing.append("evidenceProfile-inapplicable:" + action["evidenceProfile"])
1262:         return False
1263:     rec_hash = record.get("candidateHash")
1264:     if rec_hash is None:
1265:         rec_hash = record.get("candidateSha256")
1266:     stage_hash = stage.get("candidateHash")
1267:     action_hash = action["candidate"]
1268:     if complete:
1269:         if type(rec_hash) is not str or rec_hash == "":
1270:             missing.append("candidateHash-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1271:             return False
1272:         if type(stage_hash) is not str or stage_hash == "":
1273:             missing.append("stage-candidateHash-null:" + gate_stage)
1274:             return False
1275:     if type(rec_hash) is str and action_hash.lower() != rec_hash.lower():
1276:         missing.append("action-candidate-conflict:" + campaign_id)
1277:         return False
1278:     if type(stage_hash) is str and action_hash.lower() != stage_hash.lower():
1279:         missing.append("stage-candidate-conflict:" + gate_stage)
1280:         return False
1281:     texts = [
1282:         str(stage.get("purpose") or ""),
1283:         str(record.get("scope") or ""),
1284:         str(action["requirement"]),
1285:     ]
1286:     if not _capability_agrees(action["capability"], texts):
1287:         missing.append("action-capability-unresolved:" + action["capability"])
1288:         return False
1289:     if not _status_allows(status, action["kind"]):
1290:         missing.append("campaign-status-unsupported:" + str(status))
1291:         return False
1292:     return True
1293: 
1294: 
1295: def _evidence_applicable(profile, status):
1296:     if profile not in EVIDENCE_PROFILES:
1297:         return False
1298:     rank = EVIDENCE_PROFILES.index(profile)
1299:     if type(status) is not str or status == "":
1300:         return False
1301:     if _campaign_is_complete(status):
1302:         return rank <= EVIDENCE_PROFILES.index("syntax")
1303:     if status.startswith("admitted-"):
1304:         return rank <= EVIDENCE_PROFILES.index("local-runtime")
1305:     return False
1306: 
1307: 
1308: def _capability_agrees(capability, texts):
1309:     if type(capability) is not str or capability == "":
1310:         return False
1311:     blob = " ".join(texts).lower().replace("/", " ").replace("_", "-")
1312:     for stem in capability.lower().replace("_", "-").split("-"):
1313:         if stem == "" or stem in STOP_WORDS:
1314:             continue
1315:         if stem not in blob:
1316:             return False
1317:     return True
1318: 
1319: 
1320: def _owners_of(value):
1321:     if type(value) is not str:
1322:         return set()
1323:     parts = set()
1324:     for chunk in value.replace(",", "/").split("/"):
1325:         item = chunk.strip()
1326:         if item:
1327:             parts.add(item)
1328:     return parts
1329: 
1330: 
1331: def _status_allows(status, kind):
1332:     if type(status) is not str or status == "":
1333:         return False
1334:     if status == "complete-but-revoked":
1335:         return False
1336:     if kind == "report":
1337:         return True
1338:     if kind in ("review", "reproduce", "verify"):
1339:         return status == "complete" or status.startswith("admitted-")
1340:     if kind in IMPLEMENT_KINDS:
1341:         return status.startswith("admitted-")
1342:     return False
1343: 
1344: 
1345: def _verify_binding(root, campaign_id, record, missing, expected_stage=None):
1346:     binding_rel = record.get("binding")
1347:     expected = record.get("bindingSha256")
1348:     if binding_rel is None and expected is None:
1349:         missing.append("binding-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1350:         return False, None
1351:     if type(binding_rel) is not str or type(expected) is not str:
1352:         missing.append("binding-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1353:         return False, None
1354:     path = _contained_file(root, binding_rel, missing, "binding")
1355:     if path is None:
1356:         missing.append("binding-missing:" + campaign_id)
1357:         return False, None
1358:     data = _read_bytes(path, missing, binding_rel)
1359:     if data is None:
1360:         return False, None
1361:     digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
1362:     if digest != expected.lower():
1363:         missing.append("bindingSha256-stale:" + campaign_id)
1364:         return False, None
1365:     payload = _parse_json(data, missing, binding_rel)
1366:     if type(payload) is not dict:
1367:         missing.append("binding-not-object:" + campaign_id)
1368:         return False, None
1369:     if payload == {}:
1370:         missing.append("binding-schema-unresolved:empty")
1371:         return False, None
1372:     schema = payload.get("schema")
1373:     if type(schema) is not str:
1374:         missing.append("binding-schema-unresolved:" + type(schema).__name__)
1375:         return False, None
1376:     if schema != BINDING_SCHEMA:
1377:         missing.append("binding-schema-unresolved:" + schema)
1378:         return False, None
1379:     for key in ("status", "authority", "scope", "resources"):
1380:         if key not in payload:
1381:             missing.append("binding-field-missing:" + key)
1382:             return False, None
1383:         if key != "resources" and type(payload[key]) is not str:
1384:             missing.append("binding-field-type:" + key)
1385:             return False, None
1386:     if payload.get("status") != "admitted-design-subset-only":
1387:         missing.append("binding-status-unsupported:" + str(payload.get("status")))
1388:         return False, None
1389:     if _contained_file(root, payload["authority"], missing, "binding-authority") is None:
1390:         return False, None
1391:     resources = payload["resources"]
1392:     if type(resources) is not dict:
1393:         missing.append("binding-resources-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1394:         return False, None
1395:     seconds = resources.get("allocationSeconds")
1396:     if seconds is not None and type(seconds) is not int:
1397:         missing.append("binding-allocation-invalid:" + campaign_id)
1398:         return False, None
1399:     if not _verify_path_map(root, payload.get("inputs"), missing, "binding-input"):
1400:         return False, None
1401:     owned = payload.get("ownedFiles")
1402:     if owned is None:
1403:         pass
1404:     elif type(owned) is list:
1405:         for item in owned:
1406:             if type(item) is not str:
1407:                 missing.append("binding-ownedFiles-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1408:                 return False, None
1409:             if _contained_file(root, item, missing, "binding-owned") is None:
1410:                 return False, None
1411:     elif type(owned) is dict:
1412:         if not _verify_path_map(root, owned, missing, "binding-owned"):
1413:             return False, None
1414:     else:
1415:         missing.append("binding-ownedFiles-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1416:         return False, None
1417:     if not _binding_stage_and_profile(
1418:         root, payload, record, expected_stage, campaign_id, missing
1419:     ):
1420:         return False, None
1421:     return True, payload
1422: 
1423: 
1424: def _binding_stage_and_profile(
1425:     root, payload, record, expected_stage, campaign_id, missing
1426: ):
1427:     complete = _campaign_is_complete(record.get("status"))
1428:     if not complete:
1429:         return True
1430:     bind_stage = payload.get("stage")
1431:     rec_stage = record.get("stage")
1432:     if type(bind_stage) is not str or bind_stage == "":
1433:         missing.append("binding-stage-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1434:         return False
1435:     if type(rec_stage) is not str or rec_stage == "" or bind_stage != rec_stage:
1436:         missing.append("binding-stage-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
1437:         return False
1438:     if type(expected_stage) is str and expected_stage != "" and bind_stage != expected_stage:
1439:         missing.append("binding-stage-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
1440:         return False
1441:     derived = _bounds_profile(root, payload, missing, campaign_id)
1442:     if derived is None:
1443:         missing.append("acceptedProfile-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
1444:         return False
1445:     rec_profile = record.get("acceptedProfile")
1446:     if rec_profile != derived:
1447:         missing.append("acceptedProfile-conflict:" + campaign_id)
1448:         return False
1449:     return True
1450: 
1451: 
1452: def _bounds_profile(root, payload, missing, campaign_id):
1453:     mapping = payload.get("inputs")
1454:     if type(mapping) is not dict:
1455:         return None
1456:     found = None
1457:     for rel in mapping:
1458:         if type(rel) is not str or rel == "":
1459:             continue
1460:         path = _contained_file(root, rel, missing, "binding-bounds")
1461:         if path is None:
1462:             return None
1463:         data = _read_bytes(path, missing, rel)
1464:         if data is None:
1465:             return None
1466:         parsed = _optional_object(data)
1467:         if parsed is None or parsed.get("schemaVersion") != BOUNDS_SCHEMA:
1468:             continue
1469:         profile = parsed.get("semanticProfile")
1470:         if type(profile) is not str or profile == "":
1471:             missing.append("binding-profile-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1472:             return None
1473:         if found is not None and found != profile:
1474:             missing.append("binding-profile-conflict:" + campaign_id)
1475:             return None
1476:         found = profile
1477:     return found
1478: 
1479: 
1480: def _optional_object(data):
1481:     try:
1482:         parsed = json.loads(data.decode("utf-8"), object_pairs_hook=_object_pairs)
1483:     except (UnicodeDecodeError, DuplicateKeyError, json.JSONDecodeError):
1484:         return None
1485:     if type(parsed) is dict:
1486:         return parsed
1487:     return None
1488: 
1489: 
1490: def _verify_path_map(root, mapping, missing, kind):
1491:     if mapping is None:
1492:         return True
1493:     if type(mapping) is not dict:
1494:         missing.append(kind + "-unresolved")
1495:         return False
1496:     for rel, digest in mapping.items():
1497:         if type(rel) is not str or type(digest) is not str:
1498:             missing.append(kind + "-unresolved")
1499:             return False
1500:         path = _contained_file(root, rel, missing, kind)
1501:         if path is None:
1502:             return False
1503:         data = _read_bytes(path, missing, rel)
1504:         if data is None:
1505:             return False
1506:         if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != digest.lower():
1507:             missing.append(kind + "-stale:" + rel)
1508:             return False
1509:     return True

1511: 
1512: def _verify_candidate(root, campaign_id, record, missing):
1513:     manifest_rel = record.get("candidateManifest")
1514:     if type(manifest_rel) is not str or manifest_rel == "":
1515:         missing.append("candidateManifest-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1516:         return False
1517:     man_path = _contained_file(root, manifest_rel, missing, "candidateManifest")
1518:     if man_path is None:
1519:         return False
1520:     manifest = _load_json_path(man_path, missing, manifest_rel)
1521:     if type(manifest) is not dict:
1522:         missing.append("candidateManifest-not-object:" + campaign_id)
1523:         return False
1524:     extra = [key for key in manifest if key not in MANIFEST_KEYS]
1525:     for key in extra:
1526:         missing.append("unknown-manifest-field:" + key)
1527:         return False
1528:     if "schema" in manifest:
1529:         missing.append("candidateManifest-schema-unresolved:" + str(manifest.get("schema")))
1530:         return False
1531:     owned = manifest.get("ownedFiles")
1532:     if type(owned) is not dict or not owned:
1533:         missing.append("candidate-ownedFiles-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1534:         return False
1535:     for rel, digest in owned.items():
1536:         if type(rel) is not str or type(digest) is not str:
1537:             missing.append("candidate-ownedFiles-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1538:             return False
1539:     algo = manifest.get("digestAlgorithm")
1540:     candidate_sha = manifest.get("candidateSha256")
1541:     if type(candidate_sha) is not str or candidate_sha == "":
1542:         missing.append("candidateSha256-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1543:         return False
1544:     if type(algo) is not str or "SHA256" not in algo:
1545:         missing.append("digestAlgorithm-unsupported:" + campaign_id)
1546:         return False
1547:     compact = json.dumps(owned, separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True)
1548:     if hashlib.sha256(compact.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() != candidate_sha.lower():
1549:         missing.append("candidateSha256-stale:" + campaign_id)
1550:         return False
1551:     record_hash = record.get("candidateHash")
1552:     if record_hash is not None and type(record_hash) is not str:
1553:         missing.append("candidateHash-type:" + campaign_id)
1554:         return False
1555:     if type(record_hash) is str and record_hash.lower() != candidate_sha.lower():
1556:         missing.append("candidateHash-conflict:" + campaign_id)
1557:         return False
1558:     inputs = manifest.get("inputs")
1559:     if type(inputs) is not dict:
1560:         missing.append("candidate-inputs-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1561:         return False
1562:     review_rel = record.get("review")
1563:     if type(review_rel) is str and review_rel != "":
1564:         if not _apply_review(root, review_rel, record, candidate_sha, campaign_id, missing):
1565:             return False
1566:     elif _campaign_is_complete(record.get("status")):
1567:         missing.append("review-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1568:         return False
1569:     published_skip = None
1570:     acc_rel = record.get("acceptance")
1571:     if type(acc_rel) is str and acc_rel != "":
1572:         published_skip = _apply_acceptance(
1573:             root, acc_rel, man_path, candidate_sha, owned, campaign_id, missing, expected_review=record.get("review")
1574:         )
1575:         if published_skip is False:
1576:             return False
1577:     elif _campaign_is_complete(record.get("status")):
1578:         missing.append("acceptance-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1579:         return False
1580:     profile = record.get("acceptedProfile")
1581:     if profile is not None and type(profile) is not str:
1582:         missing.append("acceptedProfile-type:" + campaign_id)
1583:         return False
1584:     freeze_bytes = {
1585:         rel: digest
1586:         for rel, digest in owned.items()
1587:         if rel != published_skip
1588:     }
1589:     if not _verify_path_map(root, freeze_bytes, missing, "candidate-source"):
1590:         return False
1591:     return _verify_path_map(root, inputs, missing, "candidate-input")
1592: 
1593: 
1594: def _freeze_archive_rel(manifest_rel, published_rel):
1595:     posix = manifest_rel.replace("\\", "/")
1596:     published = published_rel.replace("\\", "/")
1597:     if not posix.endswith("-freeze.json"):
1598:         return None
1599:     parts = [part for part in posix.split("/") if part not in ("", ".")]
1600:     if not parts:
1601:         return None
1602:     stem = parts[-1][: -len("-freeze.json")]
1603:     if stem == "":
1604:         return None
1605:     archived = parts[:-1] + [stem] + [part for part in published.split("/") if part not in ("", ".")]
1606:     if any(part == ".." for part in archived):
1607:         return None
1608:     return "/".join(archived)

1610: 
1611: def _publication_core(payload):
1612:     core = json.loads(json.dumps(payload))
1613:     subsets = core.get("subsets")
1614:     if type(subsets) is not dict:
1615:         return core
1616:     subset = subsets.get(PUBLICATION_SUBSET)
1617:     if type(subset) is not dict:
1618:         return core
1619:     for key in PUBLICATION_MUTABLE:
1620:         subset.pop(key, None)
1621:     outputs = subset.get("outputs")
1622:     if type(outputs) is list:
1623:         for item in outputs:
1624:             if type(item) is dict:
1625:                 item.pop("sha256", None)
1626:                 item.pop("status", None)
1627:     summaries = subset.get("rowSummaries")
1628:     if type(summaries) is dict:
1629:         for row in summaries.values():
1630:             if type(row) is dict:
1631:                 row.pop("closureTask", None)
1632:     return core

1634: 
1635: def _publication_transform_ok(
1636:     root,
1637:     original_bytes,
1638:     current_bytes,
1639:     owned,
1640:     candidate_sha,
1641:     acceptance,
1642:     published_path,
1643:     missing,
1644: ):
1645:     original = _parse_json(original_bytes, missing, "publication-original")
1646:     current = _parse_json(current_bytes, missing, published_path)
1647:     if type(original) is not dict or type(current) is not dict:
1648:         missing.append("publication-control-substitution:" + published_path)
1649:         return False
1650:     if original.get("schemaVersion") != PUBLICATION_SCHEMA:
1651:         missing.append("publication-control-substitution:" + published_path)
1652:         return False
1653:     if current.get("schemaVersion") != original.get("schemaVersion"):
1654:         missing.append("publication-control-substitution:" + published_path)
1655:         return False
1656:     if _publication_core(original) != _publication_core(current):
1657:         missing.append("publication-control-substitution:" + published_path)
1658:         return False
1659:     subsets = current.get("subsets")
1660:     subset = subsets.get(PUBLICATION_SUBSET) if type(subsets) is dict else None
1661:     if type(subset) is not dict:
1662:         missing.append("publication-control-substitution:" + published_path)
1663:         return False
1664:     if subset.get("status") != PUBLICATION_STATUS:
1665:         missing.append("publication-control-status:" + published_path)
1666:         return False
1667:     digest = subset.get("candidateHash")
1668:     if type(digest) is not str or digest.lower() != candidate_sha.lower():
1669:         missing.append("publication-control-candidateHash:" + published_path)
1670:         return False
1671:     bound = subset.get("candidateHashStatus")
1672:     if bound != PUBLICATION_HASH_STATUS:
1673:         missing.append("publication-control-candidateHashStatus:" + published_path)
1674:         return False
1675:     if not _publication_acceptance_ok(
1676:         subset.get("acceptance"), acceptance, root, published_path, missing
1677:     ):
1678:         return False
1679:     if not _publication_reviews_ok(
1680:         root, subset.get("reviews"), acceptance, published_path, missing, candidate_sha=candidate_sha
1681:     ):
1682:         return False
1683:     outputs = subset.get("outputs")
1684:     if type(outputs) is not list:
1685:         missing.append("publication-control-outputs:" + published_path)
1686:         return False
1687:     for item in outputs:
1688:         if type(item) is not dict:
1689:             missing.append("publication-control-outputs:" + published_path)
1690:             return False
1691:         path = item.get("path")
1692:         digest = item.get("sha256")
1693:         if type(path) is not str or type(digest) is not str:
1694:             missing.append("publication-control-outputs:" + published_path)
1695:             return False
1696:         if item.get("status") != PUBLICATION_OUTPUT_STATUS:
1697:             missing.append("publication-control-outputs:" + published_path)
1698:             return False
1699:         frozen = owned.get(path)
1700:         if type(frozen) is not str or frozen.lower() != digest.lower():
1701:             missing.append("publication-control-outputs:" + published_path)
1702:             return False
1703:     orig_subsets = original.get("subsets")
1704:     orig_sub = orig_subsets.get(PUBLICATION_SUBSET) if type(orig_subsets) is dict else None
1705:     if not _publication_closures_ok(orig_sub, subset, published_path, missing):
1706:         return False
1707:     return True

1710: def _publication_acceptance_ok(pub_acc, acceptance, root, published_path, missing):
1711:     if type(pub_acc) is not dict:
1712:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1713:         return False
1714:     extra = [key for key in pub_acc if key not in PUBLICATION_ACCEPTANCE_KEYS]
1715:     absent = [key for key in PUBLICATION_ACCEPTANCE_KEYS if key not in pub_acc]
1716:     if extra or absent:
1717:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1718:         return False
1719:     for key in PUBLICATION_ACCEPTANCE_KEYS:
1720:         if type(pub_acc.get(key)) is not str or pub_acc[key] == "":
1721:             missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1722:             return False
1723:     if pub_acc["scope"] != DESIGN_SCOPE:
1724:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1725:         return False
1726:     if pub_acc["candidateManifest"] != acceptance.get("sourceCandidate"):
1727:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1728:         return False
1729:     if pub_acc["review"] != acceptance.get("review"):
1730:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1731:         return False
1732:     if pub_acc["networkMilestones"] != PUBLICATION_NETWORK:
1733:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1734:         return False
1735:     if pub_acc["metadataRule"] != PUBLICATION_METADATA:
1736:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1737:         return False
1738:     if pub_acc["acceptedAtomicCode"] != "c30aaf5c0d091b1efea7e042d0fcbd539ca6cc51":
1739:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1740:         return False
1741:     rec_at = pub_acc.get("recordedAt")
1742:     try:
1743:         datetime.fromisoformat(rec_at.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
1744:     except Exception:
1745:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1746:         return False
1747:     static_ver = pub_acc.get("staticVerification")
1748:     if (
1749:         type(static_ver) is not str
1750:         or os.path.normpath(static_ver)
1751:         != "evidence/moriarty-completion-program-2026-09-07/SP01/loan-swap-subset-01/verify3-receipt.json"
1752:     ):
1753:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1754:         return False
1755:     stat_file = _contained_file(root, static_ver, missing, "publication-static")
1756:     if stat_file is None:
1757:         return False
1758:     body = _read_bytes(stat_file, missing, static_ver)
1759:     if body is None:
1760:         return False
1761:     if (
1762:         hashlib.sha256(body).hexdigest()
1763:         != "eeef0e21216de0348ac96250508f38c2fc89d657c63176a822a7f6c8e0f3c8f2"
1764:     ):
1765:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1766:         return False
1767:     receipt_data = _parse_json(body, missing, static_ver)
1768:     if type(receipt_data) is not dict:
1769:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1770:         return False
1771:     if receipt_data.get("kind") != "verify3":
1772:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1773:         return False
1774:     if receipt_data.get("exitCode") != 0:
1775:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1776:         return False
1777:     if (
1778:         receipt_data.get("scope")
1779:         != "SP01 loan/swap design subset only. No proof, ledger or full RP01 acceptance."
1780:     ):
1781:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1782:         return False
1783:     if receipt_data.get("unauthorizedGitActivity") is not False:
1784:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1785:         return False
1786:     if receipt_data.get("storageStop") is not False:
1787:         missing.append("publication-control-acceptance:" + published_path)
1788:         return False
1789:     return True
1790: 
1791: 
1792: def _publication_reviews_ok(root, reviews, acceptance, published_path, missing, candidate_sha=""):
1793:     if type(reviews) is not list or reviews == []:
1794:         missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
1795:         return False
1796:     matched = False
1797:     wanted = acceptance.get("review")
1798:     for item in reviews:
1799:         if type(item) is not dict:
1800:             missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
1801:             return False
1802:         extra = [key for key in item if key not in PUBLICATION_REVIEW_ITEM_KEYS]
1803:         if extra:
1804:             missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
1805:             return False
1806:         path = item.get("path")
1807:         digest = item.get("sha256")
1808:         verdict = item.get("verdict")
1809:         if type(path) is not str or type(digest) is not str:
1810:             missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
1811:             return False
1812:         if type(verdict) is not str or verdict != REVIEW_VERDICT_PASS:
1813:             missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
1814:             return False
1815:         if item.get("scope") != DESIGN_SCOPE:
1816:             missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
1817:             return False
1818:         file_path = _contained_file(root, path, missing, "publication-review")
1819:         if file_path is None:
1820:             return False
1821:         body = _read_bytes(file_path, missing, path)
1822:         if body is None:
1823:             return False
1824:         if hashlib.sha256(body).hexdigest() != digest.lower():
1825:             missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
1826:             return False
1827:         review_obj = _parse_json(body, missing, path)
1828:         if type(review_obj) is not dict:
1829:             missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
1830:             return False
1831:         if review_obj.get("verdict") != REVIEW_VERDICT_PASS:
1832:             missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
1833:             return False
1834:         if review_obj.get("scope") != DESIGN_SCOPE:
1835:             missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
1836:             return False
1837:         if candidate_sha and str(review_obj.get("candidateSha256", "")).lower() != candidate_sha.lower():
1838:             missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
1839:             return False
1840:         if path == wanted:
1841:             matched = True
1842:         else:
1843:             missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
1844:             return False
1845:     if not matched:
1846:         missing.append("publication-control-reviews:" + published_path)
1847:         return False
1848:     return True
1849: 
1850: 
1851: def _publication_closures_ok(orig_sub, subset, published_path, missing):
1852:     summaries = subset.get("rowSummaries")
1853:     orig_rows = orig_sub.get("rowSummaries") if type(orig_sub) is dict else None
1854:     if type(summaries) is not dict or type(orig_rows) is not dict:
1855:         missing.append("publication-control-closure:" + published_path)
1856:         return False
1857:     if set(summaries.keys()) != set(orig_rows.keys()):
1858:         missing.append("publication-control-closure:" + published_path)
1859:         return False
1860:     for key, row in summaries.items():
1861:         if type(row) is not dict:
1862:             missing.append("publication-control-closure:" + published_path)
1863:             return False
1864:         orig_row = orig_rows.get(key)
1865:         if type(orig_row) is not dict:
1866:             missing.append("publication-control-closure:" + published_path)
1867:             return False
1868:         if "closureTask" not in row or "closureTask" not in orig_row:
1869:             missing.append("publication-control-closure:" + published_path)
1870:             return False
1871:         current = row["closureTask"]
1872:         original = orig_row["closureTask"]
1873:         if type(current) is not str or type(original) is not str:
1874:             missing.append("publication-control-closure:" + published_path)
1875:             return False
1876:         if not _closure_allowed(original, current):
1877:             missing.append("publication-control-closure:" + published_path)
1878:             return False
1879:     return True
1880: 
1881: 
1882: def _closure_allowed(original, current):
1883:     if type(current) is not str or type(original) is not str:
1884:         return False
1885:     if current == original:
1886:         return True
1887:     if CLOSURE_PENDING not in original:
1888:         return False
1889:     expected = original.replace(CLOSURE_PENDING, CLOSURE_DESIGN, 1)
1890:     return current == expected
1891: 
1892: 
1893: def _apply_acceptance(root, acc_rel, man_path, candidate_sha, owned, campaign_id, missing, expected_review=None):
1894:     acc_path = _contained_file(root, acc_rel, missing, "acceptance")
1895:     if acc_path is None:
1896:         return False
1897:     acceptance = _load_json_path(acc_path, missing, acc_rel)
1898:     if type(acceptance) is not dict:
1899:         missing.append("acceptance-not-object:" + campaign_id)
1900:         return False
1901:     extra = [key for key in acceptance if key not in ACCEPTANCE_KEYS]
1902:     for key in extra:
1903:         missing.append("unknown-acceptance-field:" + key)
1904:         return False
1905:     for field in ACCEPTANCE_REQUIRED:
1906:         value = acceptance.get(field)
1907:         if type(value) is not str or value == "":
1908:             missing.append("acceptance-field-unresolved:" + field)
1909:             return False
1910:     status = acceptance.get("status")
1911:     if not _acceptance_is_complete(status):
1912:         missing.append("acceptance-status-unsupported:" + str(status))
1913:         return False
1914:     acc_hash = acceptance.get("candidateHash")
1915:     if acc_hash.lower() != candidate_sha.lower():
1916:         missing.append("acceptance-candidateHash-conflict:" + campaign_id)
1917:         return False
1918:     if expected_review is not None and acceptance.get("review") != expected_review:
1919:         missing.append("acceptance-review-conflict:" + campaign_id)
1920:         return False
1921:     source = acceptance.get("sourceCandidate")
1922:     src_path = _contained_file(root, source, missing, "sourceCandidate")
1923:     if src_path is None or src_path != man_path:
1924:         missing.append("acceptance-sourceCandidate-conflict:" + campaign_id)
1925:         return False
1926:     unchanged = acceptance.get("unchangedPayloadHashes")
1927:     if unchanged is None:
1928:         unchanged = {}
1929:     if type(unchanged) is not dict:
1930:         missing.append("acceptance-unchanged-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1931:         return False
1932:     for rel, digest in unchanged.items():
1933:         if type(rel) is not str or type(digest) is not str:
1934:             missing.append("acceptance-unchanged-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
1935:             return False
1936:         frozen = owned.get(rel)
1937:         if type(frozen) is not str or frozen.lower() != digest.lower():
1938:             missing.append("acceptance-unchanged-conflict:" + rel)
1939:             return False
1940:     published_path = acceptance.get("publishedControlPath")
1941:     published_sha = acceptance.get("publishedControlSha256")
1942:     frozen_digest = owned.get(published_path)
1943:     if type(frozen_digest) is not str or frozen_digest == "":
1944:         missing.append("publication-bridge-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
1945:         return False
1946:     pub_path = _contained_file(root, published_path, missing, "published-control")
1947:     if pub_path is None:
1948:         missing.append("publication-bridge-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
1949:         return False
1950:     data = _read_bytes(pub_path, missing, published_path)
1951:     if data is None:
1952:         return False
1953:     current_digest = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
1954:     if current_digest != published_sha.lower():
1955:         missing.append("publication-bridge-stale:" + published_path)
1956:         return False
1957:     if current_digest == frozen_digest.lower():
1958:         return None
1959:     archive_rel = _freeze_archive_rel(source, published_path)
1960:     if archive_rel is None:
1961:         missing.append("publication-original-unavailable:" + published_path)
1962:         return False
1963:     archive_path = _contained_file(root, archive_rel, missing, "publication-original")
1964:     if archive_path is None:
1965:         missing.append("publication-original-unavailable:" + published_path)
1966:         return False
1967:     original = _read_bytes(archive_path, missing, archive_rel)
1968:     if original is None:
1969:         return False
1970:     if hashlib.sha256(original).hexdigest() != frozen_digest.lower():
1971:         missing.append("publication-original-stale:" + archive_rel)
1972:         return False
1973:     if not _publication_transform_ok(
1974:         root,
1975:         original,
1976:         data,
1977:         owned,
1978:         candidate_sha,
1979:         acceptance,
1980:         published_path,
1981:         missing,
1982:     ):
1983:         return False
1984:     return published_path
1985: 
1986: 
1987: def _apply_review(root, review_rel, record, candidate_sha, campaign_id, missing):
1988:     rev_path = _contained_file(root, review_rel, missing, "review")
1989:     if rev_path is None:
1990:         return False
1991:     review = _load_json_path(rev_path, missing, review_rel)
1992:     if type(review) is not dict:
1993:         missing.append("review-not-object:" + campaign_id)
1994:         return False
1995:     extra = [key for key in review if key not in REVIEW_KEYS]
1996:     for key in extra:
1997:         missing.append("unknown-review-field:" + key)
1998:         return False
1999:     for field in REVIEW_REQUIRED:
2000:         value = review.get(field)
2001:         if type(value) is not str or value == "":
2002:             missing.append("review-field-unresolved:" + field)
2003:             return False
2004:     findings = review.get("findings")
2005:     if type(findings) is not list:
2006:         missing.append("review-findings-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2007:         return False
2008:     rev_hash = review.get("candidateSha256")
2009:     if rev_hash.lower() != candidate_sha.lower():
2010:         missing.append("review-candidateSha256-conflict:" + campaign_id)
2011:         return False
2012:     rev_scope = review.get("scope")
2013:     camp_scope = record.get("scope")
2014:     if rev_scope != DESIGN_SCOPE:
2015:         missing.append("review-scope-unsupported:" + campaign_id)
2016:         return False
2017:     if type(camp_scope) is str and rev_scope != camp_scope:
2018:         missing.append("review-scope-conflict:" + campaign_id)
2019:         return False
2020:     if record.get("stage") != "rp01-mc02":
2021:         missing.append("review-scope-unsupported:" + campaign_id)
2022:         return False
2023:     verdict = review.get("verdict")
2024:     if verdict != REVIEW_VERDICT_PASS:
2025:         if type(verdict) is str and verdict.upper() in REVIEW_VERDICT_FAIL:
2026:             missing.append("review-verdict-blocked:" + campaign_id)
2027:             return False
2028:         missing.append("review-verdict-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2029:         return False
2030:     reviewer = review.get("reviewer")
2031:     lowered = reviewer.lower()
2032:     if lowered.startswith("author") or "author-" in lowered:
2033:         missing.append("review-reviewer-not-independent:" + reviewer)
2034:         return False
2035:     return True

2038: def _verify_resource(root, campaign_id, record, program, binding_obj, action, missing):
2039:     relative = record.get("resourceAmendment")
2040:     if type(relative) is not str or relative == "":
2041:         missing.append("resourceAmendment-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2042:         return False
2043:     path = _contained_file(root, relative, missing, "resource")
2044:     if path is None:
2045:         missing.append("resourceAmendment-missing:" + campaign_id)
2046:         return False
2047:     payload = _load_json_path(path, missing, relative)
2048:     if type(payload) is not dict:
2049:         missing.append("resourceAmendment-not-object:" + campaign_id)
2050:         return False
2051:     if payload == {}:
2052:         missing.append("resource-empty:" + campaign_id)
2053:         return False
2054:     extra_amendment = [k for k in payload if k not in RESOURCE_AMENDMENT_KEYS]
2055:     if extra_amendment:
2056:         for k in extra_amendment:
2057:             missing.append("unknown-resourceAmendment-field:" + k)
2058:         return False
2059:     schema = payload.get("schema")
2060:     if schema is not None and schema not in RESOURCE_SCHEMAS:
2061:         missing.append("resource-schema-unresolved:" + str(schema))
2062:         return False
2063:     authority = payload.get("authority")
2064:     if type(authority) is not str or authority == "":
2065:         missing.append("resource-authority-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2066:         return False
2067:     if _contained_file(root, authority, missing, "resource-authority") is None:
2068:         return False
2069:     alloc = _amendment_seconds(payload, campaign_id, missing)
2070:     if alloc is None:
2071:         return False
2072:     res_scope = payload.get("scope")
2073:     camp_scope = record.get("scope")
2074:     if res_scope is not None:
2075:         if type(res_scope) is not str or res_scope == "":
2076:             missing.append("resource-scope-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2077:             return False
2078:         if type(camp_scope) is str and res_scope != camp_scope:
2079:             missing.append("resource-scope-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
2080:             return False
2081:     successors = record.get("successorAmendments")
2082:     if successors is None:
2083:         successors = []
2084:     if type(successors) is not list:
2085:         missing.append("successorAmendments-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2086:         return False
2087:     for item in successors:
2088:         if type(item) is not str:
2089:             missing.append("successorAmendments-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2090:             return False
2091:         extra = _load_amendment_file(root, item, campaign_id, missing)
2092:         if extra is None:
2093:             return False
2094:         alloc += extra
2095:     if type(binding_obj) is dict:
2096:         resources = binding_obj.get("resources")
2097:         if type(resources) is dict:
2098:             bound = resources.get("allocationSeconds")
2099:             if bound is not None:
2100:                 if type(bound) is not int:
2101:                     missing.append("binding-allocation-invalid:" + campaign_id)
2102:                     return False
2103:                 alloc += bound
2104:     if alloc <= 0:
2105:         missing.append("resource-allocation-invalid:" + campaign_id)
2106:         return False
2107:     historical = None
2108:     if type(program) is dict:
2109:         runtime = program.get("resourceRuntimeSnapshot")
2110:         if type(runtime) is dict:
2111:             historical = runtime.get("path")
2112:     spec = record.get("currentAccounting")
2113:     if spec is None:
2114:         missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
2115:         return False
2116:     remaining = _remaining_from_current(
2117:         root, spec, record, binding_obj, action, alloc, historical, campaign_id, missing
2118:     )
2119:     if remaining is None:
2120:         return False
2121:     if remaining < 0:
2122:         missing.append("resource-exhausted:" + campaign_id)
2123:         return False
2124:     return True

2127: def _amendment_seconds(payload, campaign_id, missing):
2128:     if "allocationSeconds" in payload:
2129:         value = payload["allocationSeconds"]
2130:         if type(value) is not int or value <= 0:
2131:             missing.append("resource-allocation-invalid:" + campaign_id)
2132:             return None
2133:         additional = payload.get("additionalSeconds")
2134:         if additional is None:
2135:             return value
2136:         if type(additional) is not int or additional < 0:
2137:             missing.append("resource-allocation-invalid:" + campaign_id)
2138:             return None
2139:         return value + additional
2140:     additional = payload.get("additionalSeconds")
2141:     if type(additional) is not int or additional <= 0:
2142:         missing.append("resource-allocation-invalid:" + campaign_id)
2143:         return None
2144:     return additional

2149:     if path is None:
2150:         return None
2151:     payload = _load_json_path(path, missing, relative)
2152:     if type(payload) is not dict:
2153:         missing.append("successorAmendments-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2154:         return None
2155:     authority = payload.get("authority")
2156:     if type(authority) is str and authority != "":
2157:         if _contained_file(root, authority, missing, "successor-authority") is None:
2158:             return None
2159:     additional = payload.get("additionalSeconds")
2160:     if type(additional) is not int or additional < 0:
2161:         missing.append("successorAmendments-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2162:         return None
2163:     return additional
