2166: def _remaining_from_current(
2167:     root, spec, record, binding_obj, action, alloc, historical, campaign_id, missing
2168: ):
2169:     if type(spec) is not dict:
2170:         missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
2171:         return None
2172:     extra = [key for key in spec if key not in CURRENT_ACCOUNTING_KEYS]
2173:     for key in extra:
2174:         missing.append("unknown-currentAccounting-field:" + key)
2175:         return None
2176:     if spec.get("schema") != ACCOUNTING_SCHEMA:
2177:         missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
2178:         return None
2179:     relative = spec.get("path")
2180:     if type(relative) is not str or relative == "":
2181:         missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
2182:         return None
2183:     if type(historical) is str and os.path.normpath(relative) == os.path.normpath(historical):
2184:         missing.append("resource-historical-snapshot:" + campaign_id)
2185:         return None
2186:     path = _contained_file(root, relative, missing, "current-accounting")
2187:     if path is None:
2188:         missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
2189:         return None
2190:     payload = _load_json_path(path, missing, relative)
2191:     if type(payload) is not dict:
2192:         missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
2193:         return None
2194:     extra_acc = [key for key in payload if key not in ACCOUNTING_KEYS]
2195:     for key in extra_acc:
2196:         missing.append("unknown-accounting-field:" + key)
2197:         return None
2198:     if payload.get("schema") != ACCOUNTING_SCHEMA:
2199:         missing.append("resource-snapshot-schema-unresolved:" + str(payload.get("schema")))
2200:         return None
2201:     auth_sha = payload.get("authority_sha256")
2202:     if type(auth_sha) is not str or auth_sha.lower() != "f6b7f17d1bda437d5f83abc656ccf4a0d9c7a57a83995668387c29bd0328f492":
2203:         missing.append("resource-authority-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
2204:         return None
2205:     planning = _nonneg_number(payload.get("planning_charge_seconds"), True)
2206:     overhead = _nonneg_number(payload.get("planning_overhead_reserved_seconds"), True)
2207:     if planning is None or overhead is None:
2208:         missing.append("resource-planning-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2209:         return None
2210:     master_limit = payload.get("master_limit_seconds")
2211:     package_limit = payload.get("package_limit_seconds")
2212:     if type(master_limit) is not int or master_limit <= 0:
2213:         missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
2214:         return None
2215:     if type(package_limit) is not int or package_limit <= 0:
2216:         missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
2217:         return None
2218:     package = payload.get("package")
2219:     rec_owners = _owners_of(record.get("owner"))
2220:     if type(package) is not str or package == "":
2221:         missing.append("resource-scope-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
2222:         return None
2223:     if rec_owners and package not in rec_owners:
2224:         missing.append("resource-scope-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
2225:         return None
2226:     charges = _seconds_list(payload.get("charges"), missing, campaign_id)
2227:     reserved = _seconds_list(payload.get("reserved"), missing, campaign_id)
2228:     external = _seconds_list(payload.get("externalPackageCharges"), missing, campaign_id)
2229:     if charges is None or reserved is None or external is None:
2230:         return None
2231:     master_used = planning + overhead + charges + reserved + external
2232:     package_used = planning + overhead + charges
2233:     pass_binding = _optional_pass_record(
2234:         root, spec.get("passBinding"), PASS_BINDING_OPTIONAL, missing, "pass-binding"
2235:     )
2236:     if pass_binding is False:
2237:         return None
2238:     if type(pass_binding) is dict:
2239:         if pass_binding.get("schema") == "moriarty.plugin-focused-source-pass/1" and record.get("stage") != "plugin":
2240:             missing.append("resource-scope-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
2241:             return None
2242:         pb_scope = pass_binding.get("scope")
2243:         rec_scope = record.get("scope")
2244:         if type(pb_scope) is not str or (rec_scope and pb_scope != rec_scope and pass_binding.get("schema") != "moriarty.plugin-focused-source-pass/1"):
2245:             missing.append("resource-scope-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
2246:             return None
2247:         pb_worktree = pass_binding.get("worktree")
2248:         if type(pb_worktree) is not str or os.path.normpath(pb_worktree) != os.path.normpath(str(root)):
2249:             missing.append("resource-worktree-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
2250:             return None
2251:         pb_parent = pass_binding.get("parentCandidate")
2252:         if type(pb_parent) is not str or pb_parent != record.get("candidateHash"):
2253:             missing.append("resource-candidate-mismatch:" + campaign_id)
2254:             return None
2255:     pass_ledger = _optional_pass_record(
2256:         root, spec.get("passLedger"), PASS_LEDGER_KEYS, missing, "pass-ledger"
2257:     )
2258:     if pass_ledger is False:
2259:         return None
2260:     if type(pass_ledger) is dict and pass_ledger.get("stopped") is True:
2261:         missing.append("resource-pass-stopped:" + campaign_id)
2262:         return None
2263:     cost = _action_cost(action, binding_obj, pass_binding, campaign_id, missing)
2264:     if cost is None:
2265:         return None
2266:     prepaid = False
2267:     runners = binding_obj.get("runners", {}) if type(binding_obj) is dict else {}
2268:     if type(runners) is dict and action["id"] in runners:
2269:         # A command declaration cannot create credit. Resolve the campaign's
2270:         # hash-bound runner and its exact existing debit before recognizing it.
2271:         # load_runner checks source/debit identity without calling this reader.
2272:         try:
2273:             from moriarty_dev.runner import load_runner
2274:             plan, _ = load_runner(root, action)
2275:             if not cost <= plan["chargedSeconds"] <= alloc:
2276:                 raise ValueError("debit does not match the admitted action bound")
2277:             prepaid = True
2278:         except Exception as exc:
2279:             missing.append("resource-runner-debit-unresolved:" + str(exc))
2280:             return None
2281:     envelope_remain = None
2282:     if type(pass_binding) is dict:
2283:         envelope_id = pass_binding.get("envelope")
2284:         envelopes = payload.get("successorEnvelopes")
2285:         if type(envelope_id) is str and envelope_id != "":
2286:             if type(envelopes) is not dict or envelope_id not in envelopes:
2287:                 missing.append("resource-envelope-unresolved:" + envelope_id)
2288:                 return None
2289:             env = envelopes[envelope_id]
2290:             if type(env) is not dict:
2291:                 missing.append("resource-envelope-unresolved:" + envelope_id)
2292:                 return None
2293:             envelope_remain = env.get("remainingReservedSeconds")
2294:             if type(envelope_remain) is not int:
2295:                 missing.append("resource-envelope-unresolved:" + envelope_id)
2296:                 return None
2297:             env_dispatches = env.get("workerDispatches")
2298:             env_limit = env.get("workerDispatchLimit")
2299:             if action["kind"] in IMPLEMENT_KINDS:
2300:                 if type(env_dispatches) is not int or type(env_limit) is not int:
2301:                     missing.append("resource-dispatch-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2302:                     return None
2303:                 if env_dispatches >= env_limit:
2304:                     missing.append("resource-dispatch-exhausted:" + campaign_id)
2305:                     return None
2306:     if action["kind"] in IMPLEMENT_KINDS:
2307:         dispatches = payload.get("worker_dispatches")
2308:         dispatch_limit = payload.get("worker_dispatch_limit")
2309:         if type(dispatches) is not int or type(dispatch_limit) is not int:
2310:             missing.append("resource-dispatch-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2311:             return None
2312:         if dispatches >= dispatch_limit:
2313:             missing.append("resource-dispatch-exhausted:" + campaign_id)
2314:             return None
2315:     remainders = [
2316:         master_limit - master_used,
2317:         package_limit - package_used,
2318:         alloc,
2319:     ]
2320:     if type(envelope_remain) is int:
2321:         remainders.append(envelope_remain)
2322:     # The existing debit already contributes to master_used/package_used.
2323:     remaining = min(remainders) - (0 if prepaid else cost)
2324:     return remaining
2325: 
2326: 
2327: def _nonneg_number(value, required):
2328:     if value is None:
2329:         return None if required else 0
2330:     if type(value) is bool:
2331:         return None
2332:     if type(value) is int:
2333:         return value if value >= 0 else None
2334:     if type(value) is float:
2335:         if value != value or value in (float("inf"), float("-inf")):
2336:             return None
2337:         return value if value >= 0 else None
2338:     return None

2341: def _seconds_list(rows, missing, campaign_id):
2342:     if rows is None or type(rows) is not list:
2343:         missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
2344:         return None
2345:     total = 0
2346:     for row in rows:
2347:         if type(row) is not dict:
2348:             missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
2349:             return None
2350:         seconds = row.get("seconds")
2351:         if type(seconds) is bool or type(seconds) is not int or seconds < 0:
2352:             missing.append("resource-live-state-unavailable:" + campaign_id)
2353:             return None
2354:         total += seconds
2355:     return total

2358: def _optional_pass_record(root, relative, allowed, missing, kind):
2359:     if relative is None:
2360:         return None
2361:     if type(relative) is not str or relative == "":
2362:         missing.append(kind + "-unresolved")
2363:         return False
2364:     path = _contained_file(root, relative, missing, kind)
2365:     if path is None:
2366:         return False
2367:     payload = _load_json_path(path, missing, relative)
2368:     if type(payload) is not dict:
2369:         missing.append(kind + "-not-object")
2370:         return False
2371:     extra = [key for key in payload if key not in allowed]
2372:     for key in extra:
2373:         missing.append("unknown-" + kind + "-field:" + key)
2374:         return False
2375:     return payload

2399: def _action_cost(action, binding_obj, pass_binding, campaign_id, missing):
2400:     kind = action["kind"]
2401:     if kind == "report":
2402:         return 0
2403:     resources = {}
2404:     if type(binding_obj) is dict and type(binding_obj.get("resources")) is dict:
2405:         resources = binding_obj["resources"]
2406:     if type(pass_binding) is dict and type(pass_binding.get("resources")) is dict:
2407:         merged = dict(resources)
2408:         merged.update(pass_binding["resources"])
2409:         resources = merged
2410:     commands = resources.get("verificationCommands")
2411:     if type(commands) is not dict:
2412:         commands = {}
2413:     if kind == "review":
2414:         value = commands.get("review")
2415:         if type(value) is int and value > 0:
2416:             return value
2417:         gpt = resources.get("gpt6ReviewSeconds")
2418:         if type(gpt) is int and gpt > 0:
2419:             return gpt
2420:         missing.append("resource-action-cost-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2421:         return None
2422:     if kind == "verify":
2423:         value = commands.get("verify")
2424:         if type(value) is int and value > 0:
2425:             return value
2426:         verify = resources.get("verificationSeconds")
2427:         if type(verify) is int and verify > 0:
2428:             return verify
2429:         missing.append("resource-action-cost-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2430:         return None
2431:     if kind == "reproduce":
2432:         value = commands.get("reproduce")
2433:         if type(value) is int and value > 0:
2434:             return value
2435:         verify = resources.get("verificationSeconds")
2436:         if type(verify) is int and verify > 0:
2437:             return verify
2438:         missing.append("resource-action-cost-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2439:         return None
2440:     if kind in IMPLEMENT_KINDS or kind == "admin":
2441:         workers = resources.get("workerSeconds")
2442:         if type(workers) is list and workers and type(workers[0]) is int and workers[0] > 0:
2443:             return workers[0]
2444:         bound = resources.get("allocationSeconds")
2445:         if type(bound) is int and bound > 0:
2446:             return bound
2447:         missing.append("resource-action-cost-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2448:         return None
2449:     missing.append("resource-action-cost-unresolved:" + campaign_id)
2450:     return None

2453: def _resolve_file_admission(root, ref, missing):
2454:     if ref == "":
2455:         missing.append("admissionRef-unresolved:")
2456:         return False, False, False
2457:     path = _contained_file(root, ref, missing, "admissionRef")
2458:     if path is None:
2459:         return False, False, False
2460:     payload = _load_json_path(path, missing, ref)
2461:     if type(payload) is not dict:
2462:         missing.append("admissionRef-unresolved-shape:" + ref)
2463:         return False, False, False
2464:     schema = payload.get("schema")
2465:     missing.append("admissionRef-unsupported-schema:" + str(schema))
2466:     return False, False, False

2469: def _select_next(
2470:     root,
2471:     actions,
2472:     current,
2473:     history_fields,
2474:     history_reader,
2475:     program,
2476:     sprints,
2477:     campaigns,
2478:     program_ok,
2479:     sprints_ok,
2480:     campaign_ok,
2481:     missing,
2482: ):
2483:     ranked = sorted(
2484:         actions,
2485:         key=lambda row: (
2486:             NEXT_KIND_ORDER.index(row["kind"])
2487:             if row["kind"] in NEXT_KIND_ORDER
2488:             else 99,
2489:             row["id"],
2490:         ),
2491:     )
2492:     history_missing = [
2493:         item
2494:         for item in missing
2495:         if item == "operational-history" or item.startswith("operational-history")
2496:     ]
2497:     for other in ranked:
2498:         local = []
2499:         flags = _action_flags(
2500:             root,
2501:             other,
2502:             program,
2503:             sprints,
2504:             campaigns,
2505:             program_ok,
2506:             sprints_ok,
2507:             campaign_ok,
2508:             local,
2509:         )
2510:         snap = _snapshot(**flags, missingEvidence=list(local))
2511:         same_lineage = (
2512:             other["requirement"] == current["requirement"]
2513:             and other["capability"] == current["capability"]
2514:         )
2515:         if same_lineage:
2516:             snap.update(history_fields)
2517:             for item in history_missing:
2518:                 if item not in local:
2519:                     local.append(item)
2520:         else:
2521:             _apply_history(history_reader, root, other, snap, local)
2522:         snap["missingEvidence"] = list(local)
2523:         snap["nextActionId"] = None
2524:         decision = assess(snap, other)
2525:         if decision.get("allow") is True:
2526:             return other["id"]
2527:     missing.append("next-action-none")
2528:     return None

2531: def _apply_history(history_reader, root, action, snap, missing):
2532:     if history_reader is None:
2533:         missing.append("operational-history")
2534:         snap["sameDefectFailures"] = None
2535:         snap["adminCycles"] = None
2536:         snap["adminSeconds"] = None
2537:         return
2538:     if not callable(history_reader):
2539:         missing.append("operational-history-not-callable")
2540:         snap["sameDefectFailures"] = None
2541:         snap["adminCycles"] = None
2542:         snap["adminSeconds"] = None
2543:         return
2544:     lineage = {
2545:         "repository": _repository_identity(root, missing),
2546:         "requirement": action["requirement"],
2547:         "capability": action["capability"],
2548:     }
2549:     try:
2550:         payload = history_reader(lineage)
2551:     except Exception as exc:
2552:         missing.append("operational-history-error:" + type(exc).__name__)
2553:         snap["sameDefectFailures"] = None
2554:         snap["adminCycles"] = None
2555:         snap["adminSeconds"] = None
2556:         return
2557:     if payload is None:
2558:         missing.append("operational-history")
2559:         snap["sameDefectFailures"] = None
2560:         snap["adminCycles"] = None
2561:         snap["adminSeconds"] = None
2562:         return
2563:     if type(payload) is not dict:
2564:         missing.append("operational-history-invalid")
2565:         snap["sameDefectFailures"] = None
2566:         snap["adminCycles"] = None
2567:         snap["adminSeconds"] = None
2568:         return
2569:     extra = [key for key in payload if key not in HISTORY_KEYS]
2570:     if extra:
2571:         missing.append("operational-history-invalid")
2572:         snap["sameDefectFailures"] = None
2573:         snap["adminCycles"] = None
2574:         snap["adminSeconds"] = None
2575:         return
2576:     for key in HISTORY_INT_KEYS:
2577:         value = payload.get(key)
2578:         if value is None:
2579:             snap[key] = None
2580:             continue
2581:         if type(value) is not int or value < 0:
2582:             missing.append("operational-history-type:" + key)
2583:             return
2584:         snap[key] = value
2585:     for key in HISTORY_BOOL_KEYS:
2586:         value = payload.get(key)
2587:         if type(value) is not bool:
2588:             missing.append("operational-history-type:" + key)
2589:             return
2590:         snap[key] = value

2593: def _repository_identity(root, missing):
2594:     repo_path = Path(root).resolve()
2595:     git_entry = repo_path / ".git"
2596:     try:
2597:         if git_entry.is_file():
2598:             text = git_entry.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
2599:             if text.startswith("gitdir:"):
2600:                 gitdir = Path(text.split("gitdir:", 1)[1].strip())
2601:                 if not gitdir.is_absolute():
2602:                     gitdir = (repo_path / gitdir).resolve()
2603:                 commondir_file = gitdir / "commondir"
2604:                 if commondir_file.is_file():
2605:                     cd_text = commondir_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
2606:                     common = Path(cd_text)
2607:                     if not common.is_absolute():
2608:                         common = (gitdir / common).resolve()
2609:                     return str(common)
2610:                 return str(gitdir)
2611:         elif git_entry.is_dir():
2612:             return str(git_entry.resolve())
2613:     except Exception:
2614:         pass
2615:     missing.append("git-common-dir-unresolved")
2616:     return str(repo_path)

2618: 
2619: def _contained_file(root, relative, missing, kind):
2620:     if type(relative) is not str or relative == "":
2621:         missing.append(kind + "-path-empty")
2622:         return None
2623:     posix = relative.replace("\\", "/")
2624:     if posix.startswith("/") or _has_drive(posix):
2625:         missing.append(kind + "-path-not-contained:" + relative)
2626:         return None
2627:     parts = [part for part in posix.split("/") if part not in ("", ".")]
2628:     if any(part == ".." for part in parts):
2629:         missing.append("path-escape:" + relative)
2630:         return None
2631:     if _secret_parts(parts):
2632:         missing.append("secret-path-refused:" + relative)
2633:         return None
2634:     candidate = root.joinpath(*parts)
2635:     try:
2636:         resolved = candidate.resolve()
2637:     except OSError:
2638:         missing.append(kind + "-path-unresolved:" + relative)
2639:         return None
2640:     if not resolved.is_relative_to(root):
2641:         missing.append("path-containment-rejected-symlink:" + relative)
2642:         return None
2643:     try:
2644:         resolved_rel = resolved.relative_to(root)
2645:         if _secret_parts(resolved_rel.parts):
2646:             missing.append("secret-path-refused:" + relative)
2647:             return None
2648:     except ValueError:
2649:         missing.append("path-containment-rejected:" + relative)
2650:         return None
2651:     if not resolved.is_file():
2652:         missing.append(kind + "-missing:" + relative)
2653:         return None
2654:     return resolved
2655: 
2656: 
2657: def _secret_parts(parts):
2658:     for part in parts:
2659:         lowered = part.lower()
2660:         for secret in SECRET_PARTS:
2661:             if lowered == secret or lowered.startswith(secret + "."):
2662:                 return True
2663:             if secret in lowered.split("."):
2664:                 return True
2665:             if secret in lowered and secret in ("wallet", "seed", "witness"):
2666:                 return True
2667:     return False
2668: 
2669: 
2670: def _has_drive(path):
2671:     return len(path) >= 2 and path[1] == ":" and path[0].isalpha()
2672: 
2673: 
2674: def _structural_block(missing):
2675:     markers = (
2676:         "duplicate-",
2677:         "unknown-",
2678:         "schemaVersion-",
2679:         "sprints-schemaVersion",
2680:         "actions-schema-",
2681:         "secret-path-refused:",
2682:         "path-escape:",
2683:         "path-containment-",
2684:         "binding-schema-",
2685:         "campaign-schema-",
2686:         "entryGate-requires-",
2687:         "commandRef-",
2688:         "unsupported-",
2689:         "unknown-nested-",
2690:         "unknown-actions-field:",
2691:         "unknown-command-field:",
2692:         "unknown-stageAdmission-field:",
2693:         "unknown-entryGate-field:",
2694:         "unknown-program-field:",
2695:         "unknown-campaign-field:",
2696:         "unknown-manifest-field:",
2697:         "unknown-acceptance-field:",
2698:         "unknown-review-field:",
2699:         "unknown-accounting-field:",
2700:         "stageAdmission-schema-",
2701:         "resource-schema-unresolved:",
2702:         "candidateManifest-schema-",
2703:     )
2704:     return any(item.startswith(markers) for item in missing)

FILE plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/runner.py
1: """Adapter for a campaign-bound, already charged Foreman invocation.
2: 
3: Campaign binding bytes authorize the command. The existing budget runner must
4: record its debit before calling the CLI; this adapter never changes that budget.
5: Child stdout is data, never authority for charges or completion metadata.
6: """
7: import hashlib
8: import json
9: import os
10: from pathlib import Path
11: import selectors
12: import signal
13: import subprocess
14: import time
15: 
16: from moriarty_dev import records
17: 
18: FOREMAN_LAUNCHER = Path("/home/charl/foreman/skills/foreman/runtime/dist/foreman-launch.js")
19: 
20: 
21: def _contained(root, relative):
22:     missing = []
23:     path = records._contained_file(root, relative, missing, "runner")
24:     if path is None:
25:         raise ValueError("runner path unavailable: " + ", ".join(missing))
26:     return path
27: 
28: 
29: def _json(path):
30:     missing = []
31:     result = records._load_json_path(path, missing, "runner")
32:     if type(result) is not dict or missing:
33:         raise ValueError("runner record unavailable: " + ", ".join(missing))
34:     return result
35: 
36: 
37: def _hash(path):
38:     with path.open("rb") as stream:
39:         return hashlib.file_digest(stream, "sha256").hexdigest()
40: 
41: 
42: def _check_files(root, mapping, executable):
43:     if type(mapping) is not dict or executable not in mapping:
44:         raise ValueError("runner executable is not hash-bound")
45:     for name, expected in mapping.items():
46:         if type(name) is not str or type(expected) is not str or len(expected) != 64:
47:             raise ValueError("invalid runner file commitment")
48:         path = Path(name)
49:         if path.is_absolute():
50:             # External inputs are limited to the interpreter and Foreman itself.
51:             if name != executable and path != FOREMAN_LAUNCHER:
52:                 raise ValueError("unapproved external runner input")
53:         else:
54:             path = _contained(root, name)
55:         if not path.is_file() or _hash(path) != expected:
56:             raise ValueError("runner file changed: " + name)
57: 
58: 
59: def load_runner(root, action):
60:     """Resolve through the same campaign and hashed binding as source admission."""
61:     root = Path(root).resolve()
62:     program = _json(_contained(root, "openspec/moriarty-completion-program.json"))
63:     store = program["reportReconciliation"]["stageAdmission"]["campaignRecordStore"]
64:     campaigns = _json(_contained(root, store))["campaigns"]
65:     if not action["admissionRef"].startswith("campaign:"):
66:         raise ValueError("runner requires existing campaign authority")
67:     campaign_id = action["admissionRef"][len("campaign:"):]
68:     campaign = campaigns[campaign_id]
69:     missing = []
70:     valid, binding = records._verify_binding(root, campaign_id, campaign, missing)
71:     if not valid or not records._verify_candidate(root, campaign_id, campaign, missing):
72:         raise ValueError("runner admission changed: " + ", ".join(missing))
73:     plan = binding.get("runners", {}).get(action["id"])
74:     if type(plan) is not dict:
75:         raise ValueError("no authenticated admitted-runner authority is registered")
76:     required = {"schema", "action", "argv", "files", "launcher", "launcherFiles",
77:                 "timeoutSeconds", "graceSeconds", "outputLimitBytes", "chargeId", "chargedSeconds", "accountingPath"}
78:     if set(plan) - (required | {"assertion"}) or not required.issubset(plan):
79:         raise ValueError("unsupported runner binding fields")
80:     if plan["schema"] != "moriarty.bound-runner/1" or plan["action"] != action or campaign["candidateHash"] != action["candidate"]:
81:         raise ValueError("runner action/candidate binding mismatch")
82:     ref, fragment = action["commandRef"].split("#", 1)
83:     entry = _json(_contained(root, ref))["commands"][fragment]
84:     argv = plan["argv"]
85:     if type(argv) is not list or not argv or any(type(x) is not str or not x for x in argv):
86:         raise ValueError("invalid bound argv")
87:     if entry != {"argv": argv}:
88:         raise ValueError("command mapping differs from admitted runner")
89:     if not Path(argv[0]).is_absolute() or not os.access(argv[0], os.X_OK):
90:         raise ValueError("runner executable must be an absolute executable path")
91:     _check_files(root, plan["files"], argv[0])
92:     for arg in argv[1:]:
93:         if not arg.startswith("-") and (root / arg).is_file() and arg not in plan["files"]:
94:             raise ValueError("argv file is not hash-bound: " + arg)
95:     launcher = plan["launcher"]
96:     if type(launcher) is not list or len(launcher) != 2 or launcher[1] != str(FOREMAN_LAUNCHER):
97:         raise ValueError("runner must use the existing Foreman launcher")
98:     if not Path(launcher[0]).is_absolute() or not os.access(launcher[0], os.X_OK):
99:         raise ValueError("launcher executable must be absolute")
100:     if str(FOREMAN_LAUNCHER) not in plan["launcherFiles"]:
101:         raise ValueError("Foreman bytes are not hash-bound")
102:     _check_files(root, plan["launcherFiles"], launcher[0])
103:     for key, low, high in (("timeoutSeconds", 1, 1800), ("graceSeconds", 1, 10), ("outputLimitBytes", 256, 1048576)):
104:         if type(plan[key]) is not int or not low <= plan[key] <= high:
105:             raise ValueError("invalid runner bound: " + key)
106:     if type(plan["chargedSeconds"]) is not int or plan["chargedSeconds"] < plan["timeoutSeconds"] + plan["graceSeconds"] + 30:
107:         raise ValueError("charge does not cover startup, execution and cleanup")
108:     if type(plan["chargeId"]) is not str or not plan["chargeId"].strip():
109:         raise ValueError("runner charge identity unavailable")
110:     assertion = plan.get("assertion")
111:     if action["kind"] == "reproduce":
112:         if type(assertion) is not dict or set(assertion) != {"id", "exitCode", "stdoutSha256"}:
113:             raise ValueError("reproducer requires an admitted behavioral assertion")
114:         if type(assertion["id"]) is not str or not assertion["id"].strip():
115:             raise ValueError("empty behavioral assertion ID")
116:         if type(assertion["exitCode"]) is not int or not 2 <= assertion["exitCode"] <= 123:
117:             raise ValueError("behavioral exit must differ from ordinary failure and timeout")
118:         if type(assertion["stdoutSha256"]) is not str or len(assertion["stdoutSha256"]) != 64:
119:             raise ValueError("behavioral output commitment unavailable")
120:     elif assertion is not None:
121:         raise ValueError("assertion supplied for a non-reproducer")
122:     digest = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(plan, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode()).hexdigest()
123:     if campaign["currentAccounting"]["path"] != plan["accountingPath"]:
124:         raise ValueError("charge source differs from current campaign accounting")
125:     budget = _json(_contained(root, plan["accountingPath"]))
126:     charges = budget["charges"] + budget["externalPackageCharges"]
127:     matching = [row for row in charges if row.get("id") == plan["chargeId"]]
128:     if len(matching) != 1:
129:         raise ValueError("exactly one existing debit is required")
130:     charge = matching[0]
131:     expected = {"seconds": plan["chargedSeconds"], "candidateHash": action["candidate"],
132:                 "actionId": action["id"], "runnerDigest": digest}
133:     if any(charge.get(k) != v for k, v in expected.items()):
134:         raise ValueError("existing debit is not bound to this runner/action/candidate")
135:     return plan, digest
136: 
137: 
138: def execute(root, plan, digest, reservation_id):
139:     """Observe the actual process; retain bounded output while hashing all bytes."""
140:     command = [*plan["launcher"], "--timeout", str(plan["timeoutSeconds"]),
141:                "--grace", str(plan["graceSeconds"]), "--require-containment", "strong", "--", *plan["argv"]]
142:     started = time.monotonic()
143:     proc = subprocess.Popen(command, cwd=root, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
144:                             stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, start_new_session=True)
145:     selector = selectors.DefaultSelector()
146:     streams = {"stdout": bytearray(), "stderr": bytearray()}
147:     hashes = {name: hashlib.sha256() for name in streams}
148:     sizes = {name: 0 for name in streams}
149:     for pipe, name in ((proc.stdout, "stdout"), (proc.stderr, "stderr")):
150:         selector.register(pipe, selectors.EVENT_READ, name)
151:     limit = plan["outputLimitBytes"]
152:     ambiguous = False
153:     try:
154:         while selector.get_map() or proc.poll() is None:
155:             if time.monotonic() - started > plan["timeoutSeconds"] + plan["graceSeconds"] + 30:
156:                 ambiguous = True
157:                 os.killpg(proc.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
158:                 break
159:             for key, _ in selector.select(0.1):
160:                 chunk = os.read(key.fileobj.fileno(), 16384)
161:                 if not chunk:
162:                     selector.unregister(key.fileobj)
163:                     continue
164:                 name = key.data
165:                 hashes[name].update(chunk)
166:                 sizes[name] += len(chunk)
167:                 streams[name].extend(chunk[:max(0, limit - len(streams[name]))])
168:         ret = proc.wait(timeout=5)
169:         # A signal, launcher failure, or namespace signal encoding does not
170:         # establish completion. Foreman's confirmed timeout uses exit 124.
171:         ambiguous = ambiguous or ret < 0 or ret >= 125
172:     finally:
173:         selector.close()
174:         proc.stdout.close()
175:         proc.stderr.close()
176:     overflow = any(size > limit for size in sizes.values())
177:     assertion = plan.get("assertion")
178:     verified = bool(not ambiguous and not overflow and assertion and ret == assertion["exitCode"]
179:                     and hashes["stdout"].hexdigest() == assertion["stdoutSha256"])
180:     observed = {"actionId": plan["action"]["id"], "candidateHash": plan["action"]["candidate"],
181:                 "runnerDigest": digest, "chargeId": plan["chargeId"], "reservationId": reservation_id,
182:                 "argv": command, "exitCode": ret, "elapsedSeconds": time.monotonic() - started,
183:                 "outputSha256": {name: h.hexdigest() for name, h in hashes.items()}, "outputBytes": sizes,
184:                 "completionAmbiguous": ambiguous, "outputLimitExceeded": overflow,
185:                 "behavioralAssertions": [{"assertionId": assertion["id"], "candidateHash": plan["action"]["candidate"],
186:                                           "outcome": "defect-observed"}] if verified else []}
187:     return {"exitCode": ret, "stdout": streams["stdout"].decode("utf-8", errors="replace"),
188:             "stderr": streams["stderr"].decode("utf-8", errors="replace"), "runnerReceipt": observed}

84: def get_db_path(repo_root: str | Path) -> Path:
85:     repo_path = Path(repo_root).resolve()
86:     git_entry = repo_path / ".git"
87:     try:
88:         if git_entry.is_file():
89:             text = git_entry.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
90:             if text.startswith("gitdir:"):
91:                 gitdir = Path(text.split("gitdir:", 1)[1].strip())
92:                 if not gitdir.is_absolute():
93:                     gitdir = (repo_path / gitdir).resolve()
94:                 commondir_file = gitdir / "commondir"
95:                 if commondir_file.is_file():
96:                     cd_text = commondir_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
97:                     common = Path(cd_text)
98:                     if not common.is_absolute():
99:                         common = (gitdir / common).resolve()
100:                     target = common / "moriarty-dev" / "state.sqlite3"
101:                     return target
102:                 return gitdir / "moriarty-dev" / "state.sqlite3"
103:         elif git_entry.is_dir():
104:             return git_entry / "moriarty-dev" / "state.sqlite3"
105:     except Exception:
106:         pass
107:     fallback = repo_path / ".moriarty-dev" / "state.sqlite3"
108:     return fallback

110: 
111: def _owns_store_db(db_path: Path, repo: str | Path) -> bool:
112:     """One operational database belongs to one repository/common directory.
113: 
114:     Readers may identify the checkout or its common Git directory. Historical
115:     rows retain their original checkout keys, including removed worktrees.
116:     Ownership is checked before projecting all rows in the shared database.
117:     """
118:     root = Path(repo).resolve()
119:     database = Path(db_path).resolve()
120:     return database in {
121:         get_db_path(root).resolve(),
122:         (root / "moriarty-dev" / "state.sqlite3").resolve(),
123:     }
124: 
125: 
126: def _resolution_ids(receipt):
127:     value = receipt.get("resolvedFindings", receipt.get("resolved", []))
128:     if not isinstance(value, list) or any(not isinstance(item, str) or not item.strip() for item in value):
129:         raise StoreError("resolvedFindings/resolved must be a list of nonempty finding IDs")
130:     return value
131: 
132: 
133: def init_db(db_path: Path) -> sqlite3.Connection:
134:     if db_path.is_file():
135:         # Safeguard: verify existing file is a valid SQLite DB before executing statements.
136:         # If corrupt, raise StoreError and preserve original corrupt bytes for diagnosis.
137:         if db_path.stat().st_size > 0:
138:             try:
139:                 with open(db_path, "rb") as f:
140:                     header = f.read(16)
141:                 if header != b"SQLite format 3\x00":
142:                     raise StoreError(f"Database file is corrupt: {db_path}; invalid file header, preserving original bytes for diagnosis")
143:                 with closing(sqlite3.connect(str(db_path), timeout=5.0)) as conn_test:
144:                     conn_test.execute("PRAGMA schema_version;")
145:             except StoreError:
146:                 raise
147:             except Exception as exc:
148:                 raise StoreError(f"Database file is corrupt: {db_path}; preserving original bytes for diagnosis: {exc}")
149: 
150:     db_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
151:     conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path), timeout=10.0, isolation_level=None)
152:     try:
153:         conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;")
154:         conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000;")
155:         conn.executescript(SCHEMA_SQL)
156:     except Exception:
157:         conn.close()
158:         raise
159:     return conn
160: 
161: 
162: def _now_iso() -> str:
163:     return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
164: 
165: 
166: def make_history_reader(db_path: Path, *, timeout: float = 10.0):
167:     def history_reader(lineage: dict) -> dict | None:
168:         repo = lineage.get("repository", "")
169:         req = lineage.get("requirement", "")
170:         cap = lineage.get("capability", "")
171:         return get_history(db_path, repo, req, cap, timeout=timeout)
172:     return history_reader
173: 
174: 
175: def get_history(db_path: Path, repo: str, requirement: str, capability: str, *, timeout: float = 10.0) -> dict | None:
176:     if not db_path.is_file() or not _owns_store_db(db_path, repo):
177:         return None
178: 
179:     try:
180:         if db_path.stat().st_size > 0:
181:             with open(db_path, "rb") as f:
182:                 header = f.read(16)
183:             if header != b"SQLite format 3\x00":
184:                 return None
185:         conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path), timeout=timeout)
186:     except Exception:
187:         return None
188: 
189:     try:
190:         cur = conn.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='events'")
191:         if not cur.fetchone():
192:             return None
193: 
194:         # Preserve old checkout-keyed rows without a migration. Database
195:         # ownership was verified above; linked/removed worktrees share history.
196:         repo_ids = [row[0] for row in conn.execute(
197:             "SELECT repository FROM events UNION SELECT repository FROM admin_intervals"
198:         )]
199:         placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in repo_ids)
200: 
201:         # Total events for repository: schema-only or unrecorded database returns None
202:         cur = conn.execute(
203:             f"SELECT count(*) FROM events WHERE repository IN ({placeholders})",
204:             repo_ids,
205:         )
206:         total_events = cur.fetchone()[0]
207:         if total_events == 0:
208:             return None
209: 
210:         # This database belongs to one Git common directory. All linked
211:         # worktrees share its primary slot. A dead parent is ambiguous: its
212:         # child and charge can survive, so only recorded completion releases it.
213:         primary_active = conn.execute(
214:             "SELECT 1 FROM reservations WHERE status = 'active' "
215:             "AND action_kind IN ('implement', 'repair') LIMIT 1"
216:         ).fetchone() is not None
217: 
218:         cur = conn.execute(
219:             f"""
220:             SELECT event_kind, payload_json FROM events
221:             WHERE repository IN ({placeholders}) AND requirement = ? AND capability = ?
222:             ORDER BY id ASC
223:             """,
224:             repo_ids + [requirement, capability],
225:         )
226:         rows = cur.fetchall()
227: 
228:         unresolved_findings = set()
229:         reproducer_verified = False
230:         approach_changed = False
231: 
232:         for kind, payload_raw in rows:
233:             try:
234:                 payload = json.loads(payload_raw) if isinstance(payload_raw, str) else (payload_raw or {})
235:             except Exception:
236:                 payload = {}
237: 
238:             if kind == "defect_failure":
239:                 fid = (
240:                     payload.get("findingId")
241:                     or payload.get("defectId")
242:                     or payload.get("id")
243:                     or payload.get("defectClass")
244:                 )
245:                 if fid:
246:                     unresolved_findings.add(str(fid))
247:                 else:
248:                     unresolved_findings.add(f"defect-{len(unresolved_findings)+1}")
249:                 reproducer_verified = False
250:                 approach_changed = False
251: 
252:             elif kind == "result_review_failure":
253:                 review_findings = payload.get("findings") or payload.get("blockers") or []
254:                 if not review_findings:
255:                     fid = payload.get("findingId") or f"REVIEW-FAIL-{payload.get('candidateHash', '')[:8]}"
256:                     unresolved_findings.add(str(fid))
257:                     reproducer_verified = False
258:                     approach_changed = False
259: 
260:             elif kind == "approach_changed":
261:                 approach_changed = True
262: 
263:             elif kind == "reproducer_verified":
264:                 reproducer_verified = True
265: 
266:             elif kind == "result_review":
267:                 verdict = payload.get("verdict") or payload.get("scopedVerdict")
268:                 if verdict == "APPROVED":
269:                     try:
270:                         resolved = _resolution_ids(payload)
271:                     except StoreError:
272:                         resolved = []  # Historical malformed receipts clear nothing.
273:                     unresolved_findings.difference_update(resolved)
274:             # Notice: kind == "defect_resolved" is ignored; author events cannot erase blockers
275: 
276:         # Admin cycles
277:         cur = conn.execute(
278:             f"""
279:             SELECT count(*) FROM events
280:             WHERE repository IN ({placeholders}) AND event_kind = 'admin_cycle'
281:             """,
282:             repo_ids,
283:         )
284:         admin_cycle_count = cur.fetchone()[0]
285: 
286:         # Admin intervals
287:         cur = conn.execute(
288:             f"SELECT count(*) FROM admin_intervals WHERE repository IN ({placeholders})",
289:             repo_ids,
290:         )
291:         total_intervals = cur.fetchone()[0]
292: 
293:         if total_intervals == 0:
294:             admin_seconds = None
295:             admin_cycles = admin_cycle_count if admin_cycle_count > 0 else None
296:         else:
297:             cur = conn.execute(
298:                 f"""
299:                 SELECT start_time, end_time FROM admin_intervals
300:                 WHERE repository IN ({placeholders})
301:                   AND activity_type NOT IN ('test', 'testing', 'afk', 'idle')
302:                 ORDER BY start_time ASC
303:                 """,
304:                 repo_ids,
305:             )
306:             intervals = cur.fetchall()
307:             admin_seconds = int(_merge_intervals(intervals))
308:             admin_cycles = admin_cycle_count
309: 
310:         return {
311:             "sameDefectFailures": len(unresolved_findings),
312:             "adminCycles": admin_cycles,
313:             "adminSeconds": admin_seconds,
314:             "primaryActive": primary_active,
315:             "reproducerVerified": reproducer_verified,
316:             "approachChanged": approach_changed,
317:         }
318:     except Exception:
319:         return None
320:     finally:
321:         try:
322:             conn.close()
323:         except Exception:
324:             pass
325: 
326: 
327: def _derive_authoritative_findings(repo_path: Path) -> list[dict]:
328:     findings = []
329:     postmortems = repo_path / "docs" / "postmortems"
330:     obs_file = postmortems / "2026-09-08-ledger-source-observation.json"
331:     if obs_file.is_file():
332:         cand = "daac83578f105b0a70ce44d88c579018881daae5"
333:         try:
334:             obs_data = json.loads(obs_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
335:             cand = obs_data.get("candidateBase", cand)
336:         except Exception:
337:             pass
338:         findings.append({
339:             "findingId": "SP05-DRIVER-DEFECT-RECURRENCE-01",
340:             "defectClass": "driver-admitted-not-executed",
341:             "requirement": "SP05.1",
342:             "capability": "fixed-financial-driver",
343:             "candidate": cand,
344:             "provenance": "docs/postmortems/2026-09-06-moriarty-verification-detour.md",
345:             "description": "September 6 post-mortem recurrence 1: ledger driver returns admitted-not-executed",
346:         })
347:         findings.append({
348:             "findingId": "SP05-DRIVER-DEFECT-RECURRENCE-02",
349:             "defectClass": "driver-admitted-not-executed",
350:             "requirement": "SP05.1",
351:             "capability": "fixed-financial-driver",
352:             "candidate": cand,
353:             "provenance": "docs/postmortems/2026-09-08-orchestration-recurrence.md",
354:             "description": "September 8 post-mortem recurrence 2: ledger driver returns admitted-not-executed",
355:         })
356:     return findings

359: def bootstrap_store(db_path: Path, repo: str, initial_findings: list[dict] | None = None) -> sqlite3.Connection:
360:     conn = init_db(db_path)
361:     now = _now_iso()
362:     repo_path = Path(repo).resolve()
363:     if initial_findings is None:
364:         initial_findings = _derive_authoritative_findings(repo_path)
365:     try:
366:         with conn:
367:             conn.execute(
368:                 """
369:                 INSERT INTO events (
370:                     repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
371:                     action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
372:                 ) VALUES (?, '', '', '', 'bootstrap', 'bootstrap', ?, ?)
373:                 """,
374:                 (str(repo), now, json.dumps({
375:                     "bootstrappedAt": now,
376:                     "repository": str(repo),
377:                     "coverage": [f.get("findingId") for f in initial_findings],
378:                 })),
379:             )
380:             for f in initial_findings:
381:                 req = f.get("requirement", "SP05.1")
382:                 cap = f.get("capability", "fixed-financial-driver")
383:                 cand = f.get("candidate", "daac83578f105b0a70ce44d88c579018881daae5")
384:                 conn.execute(
385:                     """
386:                     INSERT INTO events (
387:                         repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
388:                         action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
389:                     ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 'bootstrap-finding', 'defect_failure', ?, ?)
390:                     """,
391:                     (str(repo), req, cap, cand, now, json.dumps(f)),
392:                 )
393:     except Exception:
394:         pass
395:     finally:
396:         conn.close()
397:     return conn

400: def _is_pid_alive(pid: int) -> bool:
401:     try:
402:         os.kill(pid, 0)
403:         return True
404:     except (OSError, ProcessLookupError):
405:         return False
406: 
407: 
408: def _merge_intervals(intervals: list[tuple[float, float]]) -> float:
409:     if not intervals:
410:         return 0.0
411:     valid = []
412:     for pair in intervals:
413:         try:
414:             s, e = float(pair[0]), float(pair[1])
415:             if e >= s:
416:                 valid.append((s, e))
417:         except (ValueError, TypeError, IndexError):
418:             continue
419:     if not valid:
420:         return 0.0
421:     valid.sort(key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1]))
422:     merged = [list(valid[0])]
423:     for s, e in valid[1:]:
424:         prev = merged[-1]
425:         if s <= prev[1]:
426:             prev[1] = max(prev[1], e)
427:         else:
428:             merged.append([s, e])
429:     return sum(e - s for s, e in merged)
430:

432: def reserve(db_path: Path, repo: str, action: dict, snapshot: dict, charge_id: str | None = None) -> str:
433:     conn = init_db(db_path)
434:     try:
435:         conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
436:         action_kind = action.get("kind", "")
437:         if action_kind in ("implement", "repair"):
438:             active = conn.execute(
439:                 "SELECT id FROM reservations WHERE status = 'active' "
440:                 "AND action_kind IN ('implement', 'repair') LIMIT 1"
441:             ).fetchone()
442:             if active:
443:                 raise ReservationConflictError(
444:                     f"Unresolved primary implementation reservation already exists: {active[0]}"
445:                 )
446: 
447:         res_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
448:         if charge_id is not None:
449:             try:
450:                 conn.execute("INSERT INTO runner_claims (charge_id, reservation_id) VALUES (?, ?)", (charge_id, res_id))
451:             except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:
452:                 raise ReservationConflictError("Existing charge already claimed: " + charge_id) from exc
453:         pid = os.getpid()
454:         now = _now_iso()
455:         conn.execute(
456:             """
457:             INSERT INTO reservations (
458:                 id, repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
459:                 action_id, action_kind, pid, status, reserved_at
460:             ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'active', ?)
461:             """,
462:             (
463:                 res_id,
464:                 repo,
465:                 action.get("requirement", ""),
466:                 action.get("capability", ""),
467:                 action.get("candidate", ""),
468:                 action.get("id", ""),
469:                 action_kind,
470:                 pid,
471:                 now,
472:             ),
473:         )
474: 
475:         conn.execute(
476:             """
477:             INSERT INTO events (
478:                 repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
479:                 action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
480:             ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'action_start', ?, ?)
481:             """,
482:             (
483:                 repo,
484:                 action.get("requirement", ""),
485:                 action.get("capability", ""),
486:                 action.get("candidate", ""),
487:                 action.get("id", ""),
488:                 now,
489:                 json.dumps({"action": action, "snapshot": snapshot}),
490:             ),
491:         )
492:         conn.commit()
493:         return res_id
494:     except Exception:
495:         conn.rollback()
496:         raise
497:     finally:
498:         conn.close()

501: def has_other_primary(db_path: Path, reservation_id: str) -> bool:
502:     with closing(sqlite3.connect(db_path)) as conn:
503:         return conn.execute(
504:             "SELECT 1 FROM reservations WHERE status='active' "
505:             "AND action_kind IN ('implement', 'repair') AND id != ? LIMIT 1",
506:             (reservation_id,),
507:         ).fetchone() is not None

510: def finish(db_path: Path, reservation_id: str, receipt: dict | None = None, status: str = "finished"):
511:     conn = init_db(db_path)
512:     try:
513:         conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
514:         now = _now_iso()
515:         cur = conn.execute(
516:             "SELECT repository, requirement, capability, candidate, action_id FROM reservations WHERE id = ?",
517:             (reservation_id,),
518:         )
519:         row = cur.fetchone()
520:         receipt_str = json.dumps(receipt or {})
521:         conn.execute(
522:             """
523:             UPDATE reservations
524:             SET status = ?, finished_at = ?, receipt_json = ?
525:             WHERE id = ?
526:             """,
527:             (status, now, receipt_str, reservation_id),
528:         )
529:         if row:
530:             repo, req, cap, cand, act_id = row
531:             conn.execute(
532:                 """
533:                 INSERT INTO events (
534:                     repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
535:                     action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
536:                 ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'action_end', ?, ?)
537:                 """,
538:                 (repo, req, cap, cand, act_id, now, receipt_str),
539:             )
540:         conn.commit()
541:     except Exception:
542:         conn.rollback()
543:         raise
544:     finally:
545:         conn.close()

549:     db_path: Path,
550:     repo: str,
551:     requirement: str,
552:     capability: str,
553:     candidate: str,
554:     action_id: str,
555:     event_kind: str,
556:     payload: dict,
557: ):
558:     conn = init_db(db_path)
559:     try:
560:         with conn:
561:             conn.execute(
562:                 """
563:                 INSERT INTO events (
564:                     repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
565:                     action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
566:                 ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
567:                 """,
568:                 (
569:                     repo,
570:                     requirement,
571:                     capability,
572:                     candidate,
573:                     action_id,
574:                     event_kind,
575:                     _now_iso(),

579:     finally:

583: def record_review(db_path: Path, repo: str, receipt_data: dict, action: dict | None = None):
584:     if not isinstance(receipt_data, dict):
585:         raise StoreError("Receipt must be a JSON object")
586: 
587:     author = receipt_data.get("author")
588:     reviewer = (
589:         receipt_data.get("reviewer")
590:         or receipt_data.get("reviewerObserved")
591:         or receipt_data.get("reviewerRequested")
592:     )
593:     if not reviewer or not isinstance(reviewer, str):
594:         raise StoreError("Reviewer identity required in review receipt")
595:     if author and isinstance(author, str):
596:         a_low = author.lower().strip()
597:         r_low = reviewer.lower().strip()
598:         if a_low in r_low or r_low in a_low:
599:             raise StoreError("Author self-review is forbidden")
600: 
601:     cand = receipt_data.get("candidateHash", receipt_data.get("candidate", ""))
602:     if not cand or not isinstance(cand, str) or len(cand) < 16:
603:         raise StoreError("Valid candidateHash required in review receipt")
604: 
605:     scope = receipt_data.get("scope", receipt_data.get("auditScope", ""))
606:     if not scope or not isinstance(scope, str) or scope.strip() == "" or scope.strip().lower() == "none":
607:         raise StoreError("Valid non-empty scope required in review receipt")
608: 
609:     if action:
610:         action_cand = action.get("candidate", cand)
611:         if cand != action_cand:
612:             raise StoreError(f"Candidate mismatch: receipt candidate {cand} does not match action candidate {action_cand}")
613:         req = action.get("requirement", receipt_data.get("requirement", ""))
614:         cap = action.get("capability", receipt_data.get("capability", ""))
615:         act_id = action.get("id", "review")
616:     else:
617:         req = receipt_data.get("requirement", "")
618:         cap = receipt_data.get("capability", "")
619:         act_id = receipt_data.get("actionId", "review")
620: 
621:     verdict = receipt_data.get("verdict", receipt_data.get("scopedVerdict", ""))
622:     _resolution_ids(receipt_data)  # Validate before opening or mutating the store.
623:     findings = receipt_data.get("findings")
624:     if findings is None:
625:         findings = receipt_data.get("blockers", [])
626: 
627:     conn = init_db(db_path)
628:     now = _now_iso()
629:     try:
630:         with conn:
631:             event_kind = "result_review" if verdict == "APPROVED" else "result_review_failure"
632:             conn.execute(
633:                 """
634:                 INSERT INTO events (
635:                     repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
636:                     action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
637:                 ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
638:                 """,
639:                 (
640:                     repo,
641:                     req,
642:                     cap,
643:                     cand,
644:                     act_id,
645:                     event_kind,
646:                     now,
647:                     json.dumps(receipt_data),
648:                 ),
649:             )
650:             if verdict != "APPROVED":
651:                 if not findings:
652:                     fid = receipt_data.get("findingId") or f"REVIEW-FAIL-{cand[:8]}"
653:                     findings = [{"findingId": fid, "defectClass": "review_rejected", "description": "Review not approved"}]
654:                 for finding in findings:
655:                     if isinstance(finding, str):
656:                         finding = {"findingId": finding, "defectClass": finding}
657:                     conn.execute(
658:                         """
659:                         INSERT INTO events (
660:                             repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
661:                             action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
662:                         ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'defect_failure', ?, ?)
663:                         """,
664:                         (
665:                             repo,
666:                             req,
667:                             cap,
668:                             cand,
669:                             act_id,
670:                             now,
671:                             json.dumps(finding),
672:                         ),
673:                     )
674:     finally:
675:         conn.close()
676:

678: def record_admin_interval(db_path: Path, repo: str, start_time: float, end_time: float, activity_type: str):
679:     conn = init_db(db_path)
680:     try:
681:         with conn:
682:             conn.execute(
683:                 """
684:                 INSERT INTO admin_intervals (repository, start_time, end_time, activity_type)
685:                 VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
686:                 """,
687:                 (repo, start_time, end_time, activity_type),
688:             )
689:             conn.execute(
690:                 """
691:                 INSERT INTO events (
692:                     repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
693:                     action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
694:                 ) VALUES (?, '', '', '', 'admin', 'admin_cycle', ?, ?)
695:                 """,
696:                 (
697:                     repo,
698:                     _now_iso(),
699:                     json.dumps({
700:                         "startTime": start_time,
701:                         "endTime": end_time,
702:                         "duration": end_time - start_time,
703:                         "type": activity_type,
704:                     }),
705:                 ),
706:             )
707:     finally:
708:         conn.close()

710: 
711: def enqueue_tx(db_path: Path, repo: str, tx_id: str, status: str, details: dict):
712:     if not _owns_store_db(db_path, repo):
713:         raise StoreError("Transaction database belongs to another repository")
714:     if status != "submitted":
715:         raise StoreError("Only a selected public submitted transaction may enter the outbox")
716:     if not isinstance(tx_id, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9:_-]{1,256}", tx_id) or not isinstance(details, dict):
717:         raise StoreError("Transaction id and public details are required")
718:     forbidden_subs = ("seed", "sk", "secret", "private", "witness", "password", "token", "auth", "spending_key", "signing_key")
719:     def _check_forbidden(d):
720:         if isinstance(d, dict):
721:             for k, v in d.items():
722:                 k_low = k.lower()
723:                 for sub in forbidden_subs:
724:                     if sub in k_low and k_low not in ("publicsink", "public_sink"):
725:                         raise ValueError(f"Forbidden private field in transaction details: {k}")
726:                 _check_forbidden(v)
727:         elif isinstance(d, list):
728:             for item in d:
729:                 _check_forbidden(item)
730: 
731:     _check_forbidden(details)
732:     # Closed public fields: arbitrary notes/objects can contain witness data
733:     # even when their keys do not look private. The CLI supplies only network.
734:     if set(details) - {"network", "blockNumber", "contractAddress"}:
735:         raise ValueError("Only network, blockNumber and contractAddress are public outbox fields")
736:     if "network" in details and details["network"] != "preview":
737:         raise ValueError("Only Preview public observations are supported")
738:     if "blockNumber" in details and (type(details["blockNumber"]) is not int or details["blockNumber"] < 0):
739:         raise ValueError("Public blockNumber must be a nonnegative integer")
740:     if "contractAddress" in details and (not isinstance(details["contractAddress"], str)
741:             or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9:_-]{1,256}", details["contractAddress"])):
742:         raise ValueError("Invalid public contractAddress")
743: 
744:     conn = init_db(db_path)
745:     try:
746:         conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
747:         try:
748:             existing = conn.execute("SELECT repository FROM outbox WHERE tx_id = ?", (tx_id,)).fetchone()
749:             if existing:
750:                 # The verified database owns historical outbox rows even when
751:                 # their original checkout no longer exists.
752:                 conn.commit()
753:                 return
754:             conn.execute(
755:                 """
756:                 INSERT INTO outbox (repository, tx_id, status, enqueued_at, details_json)
757:                 VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
758:                 """,
759:                 (repo, tx_id, status, _now_iso(), json.dumps(details)),
760:             )
761:             conn.execute(
762:                 """
763:                 INSERT INTO events (
764:                     repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
765:                     action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
766:                 ) VALUES (?, '', '', '', 'tx', 'tx_notification', ?, ?)
767:                 """,
768:                 (
769:                     repo,
770:                     _now_iso(),
771:                     json.dumps({"txId": tx_id, "status": status, "details": details}),
772:                 ),
773:             )
774:             conn.commit()
775:         except Exception:
776:             conn.rollback()
777:             raise
778:     finally:
779:         conn.close()
780: 
781: 
782: VALID_TX_STATUSES = {"submitted", "unknown-finality", "failed", "confirmed"}
783: TX_TRANSITIONS = {
784:     "submitted": {"unknown-finality", "failed", "confirmed"},
785:     "unknown-finality": {"failed", "confirmed"},
786:     "failed": set(),
787:     "confirmed": set(),
788:     "delivered": set(),
789: }
790: 
791: 
792: def update_tx_status(db_path: Path, tx_id: str, new_status: str, note: str | None = None):
793:     if new_status not in VALID_TX_STATUSES:
794:         raise StoreError(f"Invalid transaction status: {new_status}")
795:     if note is not None:
796:         raise StoreError("Free-form notes are not public transaction fields")
797:     conn = init_db(db_path)
798:     try:
799:         conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
800:         try:
801:             cur = conn.execute("SELECT repository, status, details_json FROM outbox WHERE tx_id = ?", (tx_id,))
802:             row = cur.fetchone()
803:             if not row:
804:                 raise StoreError(f"Transaction not found in outbox: {tx_id}")
805:             repo, old_status, details_str = row
806:             if new_status == old_status:
807:                 conn.commit()
808:                 return
809:             if new_status not in TX_TRANSITIONS.get(old_status, set()):
810:                 raise StoreError(f"Invalid transaction transition: {old_status} -> {new_status}")
811:             try:
812:                 details = json.loads(details_str)
813:             except Exception:
814:                 details = {}
815:             conn.execute(
816:                 "UPDATE outbox SET status = ?, details_json = ?, delivered_at = NULL, enqueued_at = ? WHERE tx_id = ?",
817:                 (new_status, json.dumps(details), _now_iso(), tx_id),
818:             )
819:             conn.execute(
820:                 """
821:                 INSERT INTO events (
822:                     repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
823:                     action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
824:                 ) VALUES (?, '', '', '', 'tx', 'tx_status_change', ?, ?)
825:                 """,
826:                 (repo, _now_iso(), json.dumps({"txId": tx_id, "newStatus": new_status, "note": note})),
827:             )
828:             conn.commit()
829:         except Exception:
830:             conn.rollback()
831:             raise
832:     finally:
833:         conn.close()
834: 
835: 
836: def get_undelivered_txs(db_path: Path, repo: str | None = None, *, timeout: float = 5.0, limit: int | None = None) -> list[dict]:
837:     if not db_path.is_file() or (repo is not None and not _owns_store_db(db_path, repo)):
838:         return []
839:     conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path), timeout=timeout)
840:     try:
841:         cur = conn.execute(
842:             "SELECT tx_id, status, details_json, enqueued_at, repository FROM outbox WHERE delivered_at IS NULL ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT ?",
843:             (-1 if limit is None else limit,),
844:         )
845:         rows = cur.fetchall()
846:         result = []
847:         for tx_id, status, details_str, enq, owner in rows:
848:             try:
849:                 details = json.loads(details_str)
850:             except Exception:
851:                 details = {}
852:             result.append({
853:                 "txId": tx_id,
854:                 "status": status,
855:                 "enqueuedAt": enq,
856:                 "details": details,
857:             })
858:         return result
859:     finally:
860:         conn.close()
861: 
862: 
863: def mark_delivered(db_path: Path, tx_id: str, acknowledgement: dict | None = None):
864:     if not isinstance(acknowledgement, dict) or acknowledgement.get("txId") != tx_id:
865:         raise StoreError("Actual delivery acknowledgement for this transaction is required")
866:     delivery_id = acknowledgement.get("deliveryId")
867:     if not isinstance(delivery_id, str) or not delivery_id:
868:         raise StoreError("Delivery acknowledgement id is required")
869:     if acknowledgement.get("source") != "codex-session-message" or not acknowledgement.get("contentSha256"):
870:         raise StoreError("Host-observed message acknowledgement required")
871:     conn = init_db(db_path)
872:     now = _now_iso()
873:     try:
874:         conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
875:         try:
876:             row = conn.execute("SELECT repository, status, enqueued_at, delivered_at FROM outbox WHERE tx_id = ?", (tx_id,)).fetchone()
877:             if not row:
878:                 raise StoreError("Transaction not found in outbox")
879:             repo, status, enqueued_at, delivered_at = row
880:             if delivered_at is not None:
881:                 raise StoreError("Transaction is already delivered")
882:             if acknowledgement.get("status") != status or acknowledgement.get("enqueuedAt") != enqueued_at:
883:                 raise StoreError("Delivery observation is stale for current transaction status")
884:             conn.execute(
885:                 "UPDATE outbox SET delivered_at = ? WHERE tx_id = ?",
886:                 (now, tx_id),
887:             )
888:             conn.execute(
889:                 """
890:                 INSERT INTO events (
891:                     repository, requirement, capability, candidate,
892:                     action_id, event_kind, observed_at, payload_json
893:                 ) VALUES (?, '', '', '', 'tx', 'tx_delivered', ?, ?)
894:                 """,
895:                 (repo, now, json.dumps({**acknowledgement, "deliveredAt": now})),
896:             )
897:             conn.commit()
898:         except Exception:
899:             conn.rollback()
900:             raise
901:     finally:
902:         conn.close()

FILE plugins/moriarty-dev/tests/fixtures/README.md
1: The campaign register fixture preserves the reviewed register from commit 607726e. Tests intentionally exercise this historical admission with its retained bindings. The live register now references the separately verified current SP01 design action. No historical acceptance file is changed.
2: 
3: `sp01-financial-contract-and-execution-admission.md` preserves the exact sprint input from commit `f173efe91f598e1a862d941c1c854692d9219a6b`, SHA-256 `fdb586d9bf05f2fbf7e9c2294be49854528db20e575faf9aaa1144b033908172`. It matches the retained loan binding and candidate-04 manifest. Tests copy this input into temporary repositories instead of using the mutable current sprint document. No expected digest or historical binding is rewritten. Other retained source and receipt inputs continue to be checked against their original commitments. Synthetic current accounting and historical-observation fixtures are test-only and grant no production authority.

FILE plugins/moriarty-dev/tests/reproduce_driver.py
1: #!/usr/bin/env python3
2: """Reproducer for SP05 public ledger driver defect.
3: Calls the admitted local financial driver through controlled inert transport
4: with no wallets, private keys, or network endpoints.
5: Asserts that deploy, initialize, and case-specific calls are executed.
6: Fails when the driver returns 'admitted-not-executed' or fails to execute calls.
7: """
8: import json
9: import os
10: import subprocess
11: import sys
12: from pathlib import Path
13: 
14: 
15: def find_driver(repo: Path) -> Path | None:
16:     candidates = [
17:         repo / "experiments/moriarty-midnight-financial/ledger/run-local.mjs",
18:         repo.parent / "sp05-ledger-integration-grok/experiments/moriarty-midnight-financial/ledger/run-local.mjs",
19:         Path("/home/charl/Moriarty/.worktrees/sp05-ledger-integration-grok/experiments/moriarty-midnight-financial/ledger/run-local.mjs"),
20:     ]
21:     for c in candidates:
22:         if c.is_file():
23:             return c
24:     for p in repo.glob("evidence/**/run-local.mjs"):
25:         if p.is_file():
26:             return p
27:     return None
28: 
29: 
30: def main():
31:     repo = Path(os.environ.get("MORIARTY_REPO", ".")).resolve()
32:     driver_path = find_driver(repo)
33:     if not driver_path or not driver_path.is_file():
34:         sys.stderr.write("Driver file not found in repository or worktrees\n")
35:         sys.exit(3)
36: 
37:     # Node script calling runLocalFinancialCase through controlled transport
38:     node_script = f"""
39: import {{pathToFileURL}} from 'node:url';
40: 
41: async function run() {{
42:   try {{
43:     const modUrl = pathToFileURL({json.dumps(str(driver_path))}).href;
44:     const mod = await import(modUrl);
45:     if (typeof mod.runLocalFinancialCase !== 'function') {{
46:       console.log(JSON.stringify({{status: 'error', error: 'runLocalFinancialCase not exported'}}));
47:       return;
48:     }}
49:     const options = {{
50:       case: 'loan',
51:       networkAdmission: {{
52:         logicalTag: 'local',
53:         bound: true,
54:         observedProtocol: 'midnight-mock-v1',
55:         networkConfig: {{networkId: 'undeployed'}}
56:       }},
57:       provenAssetManifest: {{proven: false, inspectedProofAssets: false}},
58:       limits: {{attempts: 1, deadlineMs: 10000, spend: 0n}},
59:       roleCapabilities: {{borrower: true, lender: true}},
60:       recipientAddresses: {{
61:         borrower: 'a001010101010101010101010101010101010101010101010101010101010101',
62:         lender: 'a002020202020202020202020202020202020202020202020202020202020202'
63:       }},
64:       blockTime: 1000,
65:       operationalAdmission: {{
66:         allowLocalExecution: true,
67:         reviewed: true,
68:         privateStateLocation: '/tmp/disposable-moriarty-state'
69:       }},
70:       walletContext: {{facade: {{}}}},
71:       eventSink: () => {{}},
72:       onSubmission: () => {{}},
73:       adapters: {{
74:         privateStoragePasswordProvider: () => 'disposable',
75:         accountId: 'disposable-account',
76:         compiledAssetsLocation: '/tmp/disposable-assets'
77:       }}
78:     }};
79:     const result = await mod.runLocalFinancialCase(options);
80:     console.log(JSON.stringify(result));
81:   }} catch (err) {{
82:     console.log(JSON.stringify({{
83:       status: 'admitted-not-executed',
84:       error: String(err && err.message ? err.message : err)
85:     }}));
86:   }}
87: }}
88: run();
89: """
90: 
91:     res = subprocess.run(
92:         ["node", "--input-type=module", "-e", node_script],
93:         capture_output=True,
94:         text=True,
95:         timeout=30,
96:     )
97: 
98:     if res.returncode != 0:
99:         sys.stderr.write(f"Node execution error: {res.stderr}\n")
100:         sys.exit(4)
101: 
102:     lines = [ln for ln in res.stdout.strip().splitlines() if ln.strip()]
103:     if not lines:
104:         sys.stderr.write(f"No output from driver process (stderr: {res.stderr})\n")
105:         sys.exit(4)
106: 
107:     try:
108:         data = json.loads(lines[-1])
109:     except Exception as exc:
110:         sys.stderr.write(f"Malformed output from driver: {lines[-1]} (err: {exc})\n")
111:         sys.exit(4)
112: 
113:     status = data.get("status")
114:     executed_calls = data.get("executedCalls", [])
115: 
116:     if status == "admitted-not-executed":
117:         sys.stderr.write("Defect reproduced: driver returned 'admitted-not-executed'; deploy and initialize calls were not executed.\n")
118:         sys.exit(1)
119: 
120:     if status != "executed" or not executed_calls:
121:         sys.stderr.write(f"Defect reproduced: driver status is '{status}', expected 'executed' with observed deploy/initialize calls.\n")
122:         sys.exit(1)
123: 
124:     print("Driver successfully executed calls:", executed_calls)
125:     sys.exit(0)
126: 
127: 
128: if __name__ == "__main__":
129:     main()

19: class WireCompatibilityTests(unittest.TestCase):
20:     def invoke(self, raw, event="PreToolUse"):
21:         args = [sys.executable, str(PLUGIN / "scripts/moriarty_dev/hook.py")]
22:         if event is not None:
23:             args.append(event)
24:         result = subprocess.run(args, input=raw, capture_output=True, timeout=2)
25:         self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
26:         self.assertEqual(result.stderr, b"")
27:         self.assertLessEqual(len(result.stdout), 2048)
28:         return json.loads(result.stdout)

30:     def test_invalid_inputs_produce_common_diagnostics(self):
31:         for raw in (b"not-json", b"[]", b"null", b"\xff", b'{"x":"' + b"x" * 8192 + b'"}',
32:                     json.dumps({"cwd": 3}).encode(), json.dumps({"cwd": ""}).encode()):
33:             with self.subTest(raw=raw[:60]):
34:                 response = self.invoke(raw)

100: class InstallationCompatibilityTests(unittest.TestCase):

110:     def run_cli(self, command, plugin=PLUGIN, before=False, extra=()):
111:         args = [sys.executable, str(plugin / "scripts/moriarty_dev/cli.py"), "--repo", str(self.repo)]
112:         args += ["--json", command] if before else [command, "--json"]
113:         result = subprocess.run(args + list(extra), env={**os.environ, "CODEX_HOME": str(self.home)}, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
114:         self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)

153:     def test_coexisting_versions_execute_registered_commands(self):
154:         old = self.copy_plugin("old-version")
155:         old_bytes = (old / "scripts/moriarty_dev/hook.py").read_bytes()
156:         new = self.copy_plugin("new-version")
157:         for plugin in (old, new):
158:             definition = json.loads((plugin / "hooks/hooks.json").read_text())
159:             for event, groups in definition["hooks"].items():
160:                 command = groups[0]["hooks"][0]["command"]
161:                 result = subprocess.run(["bash", "-c", command], input=json.dumps({"cwd": str(self.repo)}), env={**os.environ, "PLUGIN_ROOT": str(plugin), "CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT": "/stale"}, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=2)
162:                 self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr)
163:                 output = json.loads(result.stdout)
164:                 self.assertEqual(output, {} if event == "Stop" else {"hookSpecificOutput": {"hookEventName": event}})
165:         self.assertEqual(old_bytes, (old / "scripts/moriarty_dev/hook.py").read_bytes())
166:         result = self.run_cli("doctor")
167:         self.assertEqual(len(result["compatibility"]["cachedRoots"]), 2)
