256: class SprintCompletionTests(unittest.TestCase):
257:     def reports(self, sprints):
258:         with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
259:             root = Path(directory)
260:             (root / "openspec/sprints").mkdir(parents=True)
261:             (root / "openspec/sprints/sprints.json").write_text(json.dumps({"sprints": sprints}))
262:             (root / "openspec/moriarty-completion-program.json").write_text(json.dumps({"reportReconciliation": {"stageAdmission": {"stages": [{"id": "done", "status": "complete"}]}}}))
263:             return {row["sprint"]: row for row in cli._derive_sprint_reports(root)}
264: 
265:     def sprint(self, name, required=(), stages=("done",)):
266:         return {"id": name, "title": name, "stages": list(stages), "completionRequires": list(required)}
267: 
268:     def test_dependencies_missing_and_cycles_stay_open(self):
269:         for sprints in ([self.sprint("SP05", ["SP01"])],
270:                         [self.sprint("SP05", ["SP01"]), self.sprint("SP01", stages=["open"])],
271:                         [self.sprint("SP05", ["SP01"]), self.sprint("SP01", ["SP05"])],
272:                         [self.sprint("SP05", ["SP05"])],
273:                         [self.sprint("SP05", stages=[])]):
274:             with self.subTest(sprints=sprints):
275:                 result = self.reports(sprints)["SP05"]
276:                 self.assertEqual(result["status"], "open")
277:                 self.assertFalse(result["demonstrated"])
278: 
279:     def test_completion_propagates_independent_of_list_order(self):
280:         result = self.reports([self.sprint("SP05", ["SP02"]), self.sprint("SP02", ["SP01"]), self.sprint("SP01")])
281:         self.assertTrue(all(row["demonstrated"] for row in result.values()))
282:         self.assertNotIn("completion requirements:", result["SP05"]["predicate"])

38: class ExecutionTestCase(unittest.TestCase):
39:     def setUp(self):
40:         self.temp_dir = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
41:         self.root = Path(self.temp_dir.name)
42:         # git init
43:         subprocess.run(["git", "init"], cwd=str(self.root), capture_output=True, check=True)
44:         subprocess.run(["git", "config", "user.email", "test@moriarty.local"], cwd=str(self.root), check=True)
45:         subprocess.run(["git", "config", "user.name", "Test User"], cwd=str(self.root), check=True)
46:         
47:         self.db_path = get_db_path(self.root)
48:         init_db(self.db_path).close()
49:         self.child_marker = self.root / "child-marker.txt"

52:         self.temp_dir.cleanup()

54:     def make_case(self, failures=0, action_kind="implement", candidate=None):
55:         # Set up genuine loan review family in self.root
56:         tr.copy_genuine_registers(self.root)
57:         campaigns = tr.load_json(self.root / "evidence" / "moriarty-completion-program-2026-09-07" / "report-reconciliation" / "campaign-admission.json")
58:         tr.write_current_accounting(self.root)
59:         tr.attach_current_accounting(self.root, campaigns)
60: 
61:         # Write commands.json
62:         cmd_file = self.root / "commands.json"
63:         candidate_value = candidate or tr.LOAN_CANDIDATE
64:         action_id = "sp01-loan-" + action_kind
65:         runner_program = (
66:             "import json; from pathlib import Path; "
67:             f"Path({str(self.child_marker)!r}).write_text('one launch\\n'); "
68:             f"print(json.dumps({{'candidateHash': {candidate_value!r}, 'actionId': {action_id!r}, "
69:             "'charged': True, 'chargeId': 'test-admitted-charge-01', "
70:             f"'behavioralAssertions': [{{'candidateHash': {candidate_value!r}, 'assertionId': 'test-defect', 'outcome': 'defect-observed'}}]}})); "
71:             "raise SystemExit(1)"
72:         )
73:         cmd_data = {
74:             "schema": "moriarty-dev.commands/1",
75:             "commands": {
76:                 "driver": {"admittedRunner": {
77:                     "argv": [
78:                         sys.executable,
79:                         "-c",
80:                         runner_program,
81:                     ],
82:                     "actionId": action_id,
83:                     "candidateHash": candidate_value,
84:                     "timeoutSeconds": 30,
85:                 }}
86:             }
87:         }
88:         cmd_file.write_text(json.dumps(cmd_data))
89: 
90:         action_def = tr.loan_action(action_kind, "commands.json#driver")
91:         if candidate:
92:             action_def["candidate"] = candidate
93: 
94:         tr.write_actions(self.root, [action_def, tr.loan_action("report")])
95: 
96:         for i in range(failures):
97:             record_event(
98:                 self.db_path,
99:                 str(self.root),
100:                 action_def["requirement"],
101:                 action_def["capability"],
102:                 action_def["candidate"],
103:                 action_def["id"],
104:                 "defect_failure",
105:                 {"defectId": f"DEFECT-{i+1}", "details": "failing test"},
106:             )
107: 
108:         return action_def
109: 
110:     def run_cli(self, *args):
111:         cli_py = SCRIPTS_DIR / "moriarty_dev" / "cli.py"
112:         cmd = [sys.executable, str(cli_py), "--repo", str(self.root)] + list(args)
113:         return subprocess.run(cmd, capture_output=True, text=True)

143:     def test_concurrent_reservation_denial(self):
144:         action = self.make_case(failures=0, action_kind="implement")
145:         snap = {"primaryActive": False}
146:         res_id1 = reserve(self.db_path, str(self.root), action, snap)
147:         self.assertTrue(res_id1)
148: 
149:         # Second reservation for primary implementation must fail
150:         with self.assertRaises(ReservationConflictError):
151:             reserve(self.db_path, str(self.root), action, snap)
152: 
153:         # Finish first reservation
154:         finish(self.db_path, res_id1, {"exitCode": 0})
155: 
156:         # Now reservation succeeds
157:         res_id2 = reserve(self.db_path, str(self.root), action, snap)
158:         self.assertTrue(res_id2)
159:         finish(self.db_path, res_id2, {"exitCode": 0})

161:     def test_linked_worktrees_share_db(self):
162:         readme = self.root / "README.md"
163:         readme.write_text("initial")
164:         subprocess.run(["git", "add", "README.md"], cwd=str(self.root), check=True, capture_output=True)
165:         subprocess.run(["git", "commit", "-m", "initial commit"], cwd=str(self.root), check=True, capture_output=True)
166: 
167:         wt_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="wt-test-"))
168:         try:
169:             subprocess.run(["git", "worktree", "add", "-b", "branch-b", str(wt_dir)], cwd=str(self.root), check=True, capture_output=True)
170:             wt_db_path = get_db_path(wt_dir)
171:             self.assertEqual(wt_db_path.resolve(), self.db_path.resolve())
172: 
173:             action = {"id": "worktree-primary", "kind": "implement"}
174:             first = reserve(self.db_path, str(self.root), action, {})
175:             with self.assertRaises(ReservationConflictError):
176:                 reserve(wt_db_path, str(wt_dir), action, {})
177:             finish(self.db_path, first, {"exitCode": 0})
178:             successor = reserve(wt_db_path, str(wt_dir), action, {})
179:             finish(wt_db_path, successor, {"exitCode": 0})
180: 
181:             record_event(wt_db_path, str(self.root), "SP05", "fixed-financial-driver", "cand-x", "act-x", "defect_failure", {})
182:             history = get_history(self.db_path, str(self.root), "SP05", "fixed-financial-driver")
183:             self.assertEqual(history["sameDefectFailures"], 1)
184:         finally:
185:             subprocess.run(["git", "worktree", "remove", "--force", str(wt_dir)], cwd=str(self.root), check=False, capture_output=True)
186:             shutil.rmtree(wt_dir, ignore_errors=True)

188:     def test_crashed_reservation_recovery(self):
189:         action = self.make_case(failures=0, action_kind="implement")
190:         snap = {"primaryActive": False}
191:         res_id = reserve(self.db_path, str(self.root), action, snap)
192: 
193:         # Simulate dead process by setting PID to a non-existent PID (e.g. 999999)
194:         conn = init_db(self.db_path)
195:         with conn:
196:             conn.execute("UPDATE reservations SET pid = 999999 WHERE id = ?", (res_id,))
197:         conn.close()
198: 
199:         # A dead parent says nothing about its child or durable charge. Keep
200:         # this reservation blocking until actual completion is reconciled.
201:         with self.assertRaises(ReservationConflictError):
202:             reserve(self.db_path, str(self.root), action, snap)
203:         history = get_history(self.db_path, str(self.root), action["requirement"], action["capability"])
204:         self.assertTrue(history["primaryActive"])
205:         conn = init_db(self.db_path)
206:         self.assertEqual(conn.execute("SELECT status FROM reservations WHERE id = ?", (res_id,)).fetchone()[0], "active")
207:         conn.close()

13:     sys.path.insert(0, str(SCRIPTS_DIR))
14: if str(TESTS_DIR) not in sys.path:
15:     sys.path.insert(0, str(TESTS_DIR))

18: from moriarty_dev.store import get_db_path, init_db, record_event

21: class HookContractTestCase(unittest.TestCase):
22:     def setUp(self):
23:         self.temp_dir = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
24:         self.root = Path(self.temp_dir.name)
25:         subprocess.run(["git", "init"], cwd=str(self.root), capture_output=True, check=True)
26:         subprocess.run(["git", "config", "user.email", "test@moriarty.local"], cwd=str(self.root), check=True)
27:         subprocess.run(["git", "config", "user.name", "Test User"], cwd=str(self.root), check=True)
28:         self.db_path = get_db_path(self.root)
29:         init_db(self.db_path).close()
30:         self.hook_py = SCRIPTS_DIR / "moriarty_dev" / "hook.py"
31: 
32:     def tearDown(self):
33:         self.temp_dir.cleanup()
34: 
35:     def test_registered_commands_resolve_native_and_claude_plugin_roots(self):
36:         plugin_root = SCRIPTS_DIR.parent
37:         config = json.loads((plugin_root / "hooks" / "hooks.json").read_text())
38:         # Exercise the registered shell commands: direct hook.py calls cannot
39:         # detect a missing host-specific environment variable.
40:         with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="plugin path ") as directory:
41:             spaced_root = Path(directory) / "moriarty plugin"
42:             spaced_root.symlink_to(plugin_root, target_is_directory=True)
43:             for host in ("codex", "claude", "both"):
44:                 env = dict(os.environ)
45:                 env.pop("PLUGIN_ROOT", None)
46:                 env.pop("CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT", None)
47:                 if host in ("codex", "both"):
48:                     env["PLUGIN_ROOT"] = str(spaced_root)
49:                 if host in ("claude", "both"):
50:                     env["CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT"] = (
51:                         "/missing/stale-plugin" if host == "both" else str(spaced_root)
52:                     )
53:                 for event, groups in config["hooks"].items():
54:                     for group in groups:
55:                         for hook in group["hooks"]:
56:                             with self.subTest(host=host, event=event):
57:                                 proc = subprocess.run(
58:                                     ["bash", "-c", hook["command"]],
59:                                     input=json.dumps({"cwd": str(self.root)}),
60:                                     env=env, capture_output=True, text=True,
61:                                     timeout=hook["timeout"],
62:                                 )
63:                                 self.assertEqual(proc.returncode, 0, proc.stderr)
64:                                 self.assertEqual(proc.stderr, "")
65:                                 output = json.loads(proc.stdout)
66:                                 if event == "Stop":
67:                                     self.assertEqual(output, {})
68:                                 else:
69:                                     self.assertEqual(output["hookSpecificOutput"]["hookEventName"], event)
70: 
71:     def run_hook(self, event_name, payload):
72:         cmd = [sys.executable, str(self.hook_py), event_name]
73:         start = time.time()
74:         proc = subprocess.run(
75:             cmd,
76:             input=json.dumps(payload),
77:             capture_output=True,
78:             text=True,
79:             timeout=2.0,
80:         )
81:         elapsed = time.time() - start
82:         self.assertLessEqual(elapsed, 1.0, "Hook must execute within 1.0 second")
83:         self.assertLessEqual(len(proc.stdout.encode("utf-8")), 2048, "Hook output must be <= 2048 bytes")
84:         self.assertEqual(proc.returncode, 0)
85:         return json.loads(proc.stdout.strip() or "{}")
86: 
87:     def test_unrelated_repository_permits(self):
88:         # Empty git repo with no actions.json
89:         payload = {
90:             "hookEventName": "PreToolUse",
91:             "cwd": str(self.root),
92:             "toolName": "execute_command",
93:             "toolInput": {"command": "ls -la"},
94:         }
95:         res = self.run_hook("PreToolUse", payload)
96:         self.assertNotIn("permissionDecision", res.get("hookSpecificOutput", {}))
97: 
98:     def test_malformed_input_does_not_crash(self):
99:         cmd = [sys.executable, str(self.hook_py), "PreToolUse"]
100:         proc = subprocess.run(
101:             cmd,
102:             input="NOT_JSON_AT_ALL!!!",
103:             capture_output=True,
104:             text=True,
105:         )
106:         self.assertEqual(proc.returncode, 0)
107:         res = json.loads(proc.stdout.strip() or "{}")
108:         self.assertNotIn("permissionDecision", res.get("hookSpecificOutput", {}))
109: 
110:     def test_denied_known_dispatch(self):
111:         # Set up genuine registers in self.root with 2 failures
112:         tr.copy_genuine_registers(self.root)

114:         tr.write_current_accounting(self.root)
115:         tr.attach_current_accounting(self.root, campaigns)
116:         tr.write_commands(self.root)
117:

119:         reproduce_action = tr.loan_action("reproduce", "commands.json#driver")
120:         tr.write_actions(self.root, [implement_action, reproduce_action, tr.loan_action("report")])
121:

123:             record_event(

138:             "toolInput": {"command": "python3 plugins/moriarty-dev/scripts/moriarty_dev/cli.py run --action sp01-loan-implement"},
139:         }

141:         hook_out = res.get("hookSpecificOutput", {})

144:         self.assertIn("sp01-loan-reproduce", hook_out.get("permissionDecisionReason", ""))

104:                 out=json.loads(p.stdout)
105:                 self.assertNotIn('hookSpecificOutput',out)
106:                 self.assertNotIn('decision',out)
107:                 self.assertNotIn('continue',out)
108:                 if cwd == '\x00': self.assertIn('systemMessage',out)
109:                 else: self.assertEqual(out,{})

63:     return {
64:         "id": "ledger-driver-" + kind,
65:         "requirement": "SP05",
66:         "capability": "fixed-financial-driver",
67:         "kind": kind,
68:         "candidate": "candidate-a",

78:     body["requirement"] = "SP02"
79:     body["capability"] = "successor-frontend"
80:     body["candidate"] = "grammar-candidate"
81:     return body

83: 
84: def decision_shape(test, result):
85:     test.assertEqual(tuple(result), DECISION_KEYS)
86:     test.assertIs(type(result["allow"]), bool)
87:     test.assertIs(type(result["reasonCode"]), str)
88:     test.assertTrue(
89:         result["nextActionId"] is None or type(result["nextActionId"]) is str
90:     )
91:     test.assertIs(type(result["missingEvidence"]), list)
92:     test.assertTrue(all(type(item) is str for item in result["missingEvidence"]))

123: class PolicyBoundaries(unittest.TestCase):
124:     def test_assess_is_callable_and_input_dependent(self):
125:         self.assertTrue(callable(assess))
126:         allowed = assess(baseline(), action("implement"))
127:         blocked = assess(
128:             dict(baseline(), sameDefectFailures=2), action("implement")
129:         )
130:         decision_shape(self, allowed)
131:         decision_shape(self, blocked)
132:         self.assertTrue(allowed["allow"])
133:         self.assertEqual(allowed["reasonCode"], "ALLOWED")
134:         self.assertFalse(blocked["allow"])
135:         self.assertNotEqual(allowed["reasonCode"], blocked["reasonCode"])
136: 
137:     def test_one_failure_still_allows_implement(self):
138:         s = baseline()
139:         s["sameDefectFailures"] = 1
140:         self.assertTrue(assess(s, action("implement"))["allow"])

95: def write_json(path, payload):
96:     path = Path(path)
97:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
98:     path.write_text(json.dumps(payload, indent=2, sort_keys=False) + "\n")
99:     return path

119: def copy_relative(root, relative):
120:     src = SOURCE_ROOT / relative
121:     # This input belongs to the frozen historical admission, not today's sprint.
122:     # Do not rehash its binding to make a changed live document appear approved.
123:     if relative == "openspec/sprints/sp01-financial-contract-and-execution-admission.md":
124:         src = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "fixtures" / Path(relative).name
125:     if not src.is_file():
126:         return None
127:     dest = root / relative
128:     dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
129:     shutil.copy2(src, dest)
130:     return dest

134:     dest_program = root / "openspec" / "moriarty-completion-program.json"

145:     dest_campaign.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
146:     shutil.copy2(PROGRAM_SRC, dest_program)
147:     shutil.copy2(SPRINTS_SRC, dest_sprints)
148:     shutil.copy2(CAMPAIGN_SRC, dest_campaign)

178:     refs.extend(freeze.get("inputs", {}))
179:     refs.extend(binding.get("inputs", {}))
180:     if type(binding.get("ownedFiles")) is list:
181:         refs.extend(binding["ownedFiles"])
182:     elif type(binding.get("ownedFiles")) is dict:
183:         refs.extend(binding["ownedFiles"])
184:     refs.extend((binding.get("correctionInputs") or {}))

193:     for rel in refs:
194:         if type(rel) is str and rel != "":
195:             copy_relative(root, rel)
196:     return dest_program, dest_sprints, dest_campaign

392: def overlay_supported_prereq(campaigns, program, loan):
393:     profile = loan["acceptedProfile"]
394:     digest = loan["candidateHash"]
395:     for stage_id in ("atomic-prepare", "atomic-accept"):
396:         stage = stage_by_id(program, stage_id)
397:         campaign_id = stage["campaignRecordId"]
398:         record = dict(campaigns["campaigns"][campaign_id])
399:         for key in (
400:             "binding",
401:             "bindingSha256",
402:             "candidateHash",
403:             "candidateManifest",
404:             "acceptance",
405:             "review",
406:             "acceptedProfile",
407:             "scope",
408:         ):
409:             record[key] = loan[key]
410:         record["stage"] = stage_id
411:         record["status"] = "complete"
412:         record["owner"] = "MC01"
413:         campaigns["campaigns"][campaign_id] = record
414:         stage["candidateHash"] = digest
415:         stage["acceptedProfile"] = profile

418: def use_supported_i2_prereq(program, sprints):
419:     stage_by_id(program, "i2")["requires"] = ["rp01-mc02"]
420:     for sprint in sprints["sprints"]:
421:         for gate in sprint.get("entryGates", []):
422:             tasks = gate.get("tasks", [])
423:             if type(tasks) is list and any(
424:                 type(item) is str and item.startswith("SP05.") for item in tasks
425:             ):
426:                 gate["requires"] = ["rp01-mc02"]
427: 
428: 
429: def require_supported_prereq(program_path, sprints_path):
430:     program = load_json(program_path)
431:     sprints = load_json(sprints_path)
432:     for sprint in sprints["sprints"]:
433:         for gate in sprint.get("entryGates", []):
434:             if "SP01.6" in gate.get("tasks", []):
435:                 gate["requires"] = ["rp01-mc02"]
436:     dump_json(sprints_path, sprints)
437:     dump_json(program_path, program)
438:     return program
439: 
440: 
441: def verified_history(failures=0, **extra):
442:     payload = {
443:         "sameDefectFailures": failures,
444:         "adminCycles": 0,
445:         "adminSeconds": 0,
446:         "primaryActive": False,
447:         "reproducerVerified": False,
448:         "approachChanged": False,
449:     }
450:     payload.update(extra)
451: 
452:     def reader(lineage):
453:         if set(lineage) != {"repository", "requirement", "capability"}:
454:             raise AssertionError("history reader received non-lineage keys")
455:         return dict(payload)
456: 
457:     return reader
458: 
459: 
460: def snapshot_shape(test, snap):
461:     test.assertEqual(tuple(snap), SNAPSHOT_KEYS)
462:     for key in (
463:         "authorityCurrent",
464:         "entryEligible",
465:         "candidateCurrent",
466:         "resourceAdmitted",
467:         "primaryActive",
468:         "reproducerVerified",
469:         "approachChanged",
470:     ):
471:         test.assertIs(type(snap[key]), bool, key)
472:     has_hist_gap = any(
473:         isinstance(item, str) and (item == "operational-history" or item.startswith("operational-history"))
474:         for item in snap.get("missingEvidence", [])
475:     )
476:     for key in ("sameDefectFailures", "adminCycles", "adminSeconds"):
477:         if has_hist_gap and snap[key] is None:
478:             continue
479:         if snap[key] is None and key in ("adminCycles", "adminSeconds"):
480:             continue
481:         test.assertIs(type(snap[key]), int, key)
482:         test.assertFalse(isinstance(snap[key], bool))
483:     test.assertTrue(snap["nextActionId"] is None or type(snap["nextActionId"]) is str)
484:     test.assertIs(type(snap["missingEvidence"]), list)
485:     test.assertTrue(all(type(item) is str for item in snap["missingEvidence"]))
486: 
487: 
488: class GenuineRegisters(unittest.TestCase):
489:     def setUp(self):
490:         self.temp, self.root = make_repo()
491:         self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
492:         if MARKER.exists():
493:             MARKER.unlink()
494:         self.program_path, self.sprints_path, self.campaign_path = copy_genuine_registers(
495:             self.root
496:         )

498:         self.campaigns = load_json(self.campaign_path)
499:         self.candidate_path = self.root / "experiments" / "driver-candidate.txt"
500:         self.candidate_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
501:         self.candidate_path.write_bytes(b"ledger-driver-candidate-v1\n")
502:         self.candidate = sha256_file(self.candidate_path)
503:         self.binding = "evidence/moriarty-completion-program-2026-09-07/SP05/ledger-source-packet-01/binding.json"
504:         self.resource = "evidence/moriarty-completion-program-2026-09-07/SP05/ledger-source-packet-01/resource-amendment.json"
505:         self.binding_hash = write_binding(self.root, self.binding, self.candidate)
506:         write_resource(self.root, self.resource)
507:         write_commands(self.root)
508:         copy_relative(
509:             self.root,
510:             "raw/assignments/moriarty-twelve-sprint-afk-execution-2026-09-07.md",
511:         )
512:         dump_json(self.program_path, self.program)
513:         dump_json(self.campaign_path, self.campaigns)

515:     def _isolate_i2(self):

518:         dump_json(self.program_path, self.program)
519:         dump_json(self.sprints_path, sprints)

521:     def _install_admitted_sp05(self, *, f0_blocked=True):

530:             self.resource,
531:         )

533:         dump_json(self.campaign_path, self.campaigns)
534:         write_actions(
535:             self.root,
536:             [
537:                 sp05_action("implement", self.candidate, "campaign:sp05-ledger-driver-01"),
538:                 sp05_action("reproduce", self.candidate, "campaign:sp05-ledger-driver-01"),
539:                 sp05_action("repair", self.candidate, "campaign:sp05-ledger-driver-01"),
540:                 sp05_action("report", self.candidate, "campaign:sp05-ledger-driver-01", ""),
541:                 {
542:                     "id": "sp02-frontend-implement",
543:                     "requirement": "SP02.1",
544:                     "capability": "successor-frontend",
545:                     "kind": "implement",
546:                     "candidate": self.candidate,
547:                     "admissionRef": "campaign:sp05-ledger-driver-01",
548:                     "commandRef": "commands.json#driver",
549:                     "evidenceProfile": "syntax",
550:                 },
551:                 {
552:                     "id": "sp04-f0a-implement",
553:                     "requirement": "SP04.1",
554:                     "capability": "native-component-authorship",
555:                     "kind": "implement",
556:                     "candidate": self.candidate,
557:                     "admissionRef": "campaign:sp05-ledger-driver-01",
558:                     "commandRef": "commands.json#driver",

563:         )

566:         if integer_budget:

568:         write_actions(self.root, actions or [loan_action("report")])

570:     def _install_funded_loan_review(self, accounting_patch=None, extra_accounting=None):
571:         self.program_path, self.sprints_path, self.campaign_path = copy_genuine_registers(
572:             self.root
573:         )

575:         self.campaigns = load_json(self.campaign_path)
576:         write_commands(self.root)
577:         write_current_accounting(self.root, accounting_patch)
578:         attach_current_accounting(
579:             self.root, self.campaigns, extra=extra_accounting
580:         )
581:         self.campaigns = load_json(self.campaign_path)
582:         write_actions(
583:             self.root,
584:             [loan_action("review", "commands.json#driver"), loan_action("report")],
585:         )

864:     def test_linked_worktree_uses_git_common_dir(self):
865:         self._install_loan_family()
866:         subprocess.run(["git", "add", "."], cwd=self.root, check=True, capture_output=True)
867:         subprocess.run(
868:             ["git", "-c", "user.name=Fixture", "-c", "user.email=fixture@example.invalid", "commit", "-m", "fixture"],
869:             cwd=self.root,
870:             check=True,
871:             capture_output=True,
872:         )
873:         seen = []
874: 
875:         def reader(lineage):
876:             seen.append(lineage["repository"])
877:             return verified_history()(lineage)
878: 
879:         with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir="/tmp") as tmp:
880:             linked = Path(tmp) / "linked"
881:             subprocess.run(
882:                 ["git", "worktree", "add", "--detach", str(linked), "HEAD"],
883:                 cwd=self.root,
884:                 check=True,
885:                 capture_output=True,
886:             )
887:             load_snapshot(str(self.root), "sp01-loan-report", history_reader=reader)
888:             load_snapshot(str(linked), "sp01-loan-report", history_reader=reader)
889:         self.assertEqual(seen[0], seen[1])
890:         self.assertNotEqual(seen[0], str(self.root.resolve()))

1171: class AdmissionTraversal(unittest.TestCase):
1172:     setUp = GenuineRegisters.setUp
1173:     _install_funded_loan_review = GenuineRegisters._install_funded_loan_review
1174:     def _positive_review(self):
1175:         self._install_funded_loan_review()
1176:         snap = load_snapshot(
1177:             str(self.root), "sp01-loan-review", history_reader=verified_history()
1178:         )
1179:         action = [row for row in read_actions(str(self.root)) if row["id"] == "sp01-loan-review"][0]
1180:         self.assertTrue(assess(snap, action)["allow"], snap["missingEvidence"])
1181:         self.assertTrue(snap["resourceAdmitted"])
1182:         self.assertTrue(snap["candidateCurrent"])
1183:         return snap

1185:     def test_actual_master_family_admits_review(self):
1186:         self._install_funded_loan_review()
1187:         dest = self.root / CURRENT_ACCOUNTING_REL
1188:         budget_data = load_json(MASTER_BUDGET_SRC)
1189:         # Live master budget without manufactured seconds
1190:         dump_json(dest, budget_data)
1191:         pass_bind = self.root / ".moriarty-dev" / "pass-binding.json"
1192:         pass_led = self.root / ".moriarty-dev" / "pass-ledger.json"
1193:         pass_bind.write_bytes(PASS_BINDING_SRC.read_bytes())
1194:         pass_led.write_bytes(PASS_LEDGER_SRC.read_bytes())
1195:         parent_bind = self.root / ".moriarty-dev" / "parent-binding.json"
1196:         parent_led = self.root / ".moriarty-dev" / "parent-ledger.json"
1197:         parent_bind.write_bytes(PARENT_BINDING_SRC.read_bytes())
1198:         parent_led.write_bytes(PARENT_LEDGER_SRC.read_bytes())
1199:         attach_current_accounting(
1200:             self.root,
1201:             load_json(self.campaign_path),
1202:             extra={
1203:                 "passBinding": ".moriarty-dev/pass-binding.json",
1204:                 "passLedger": ".moriarty-dev/pass-ledger.json",
1205:             },
1206:         )
1207:         snap = load_snapshot(
1208:             str(self.root), "sp01-loan-review", history_reader=verified_history()
1209:         )
1210:         action = [row for row in read_actions(str(self.root)) if row["id"] == "sp01-loan-review"][0]
1211:         # Attaching a plugin-focused pass binding to loan campaign must be rejected

1560:         original = published.read_bytes()
1561:         published.write_text("arbitrary replacement: all financial requirements accepted\n")
1562:         acceptance["publishedControlSha256"] = sha256_file(published)
1563:         dump_json(acceptance_path, acceptance)

1566:         )

1570:             rewritten["missingEvidence"],

1572:         published.write_bytes(original)
1573:         dump_json(acceptance_path, load_json(LOAN_ACCEPT_SRC))
1574:         control = json.loads(original.decode("utf-8"))
1575:         control["findings"] = [{"id": "forged", "kind": "substitution", "statement": "x"}]
1576:         published.write_text(json.dumps(control) + "\n")
1577:         acceptance = load_json(acceptance_path)
1578:         acceptance["publishedControlSha256"] = sha256_file(published)
1579:         dump_json(acceptance_path, acceptance)

1586:     def test_publication_nested_acceptance_and_closed_status(self):
1587:         snap = self._positive_review()
1588:         self.assertTrue(snap["candidateCurrent"])
1589:         self.assertTrue(snap["entryEligible"])
1590:         loan = load_json(self.campaign_path)["campaigns"]["sp01-loan-swap-grok-01"]
1591:         acceptance_path = self.root / loan["acceptance"]
1592:         published = self.root / load_json(acceptance_path)["publishedControlPath"]
1593:         original = published.read_bytes()
1594: 
1595:         def mutate_control(patch):
1596:             published.write_bytes(original)
1597:             control = json.loads(original.decode("utf-8"))
1598:             patch(control)
1599:             published.write_text(json.dumps(control) + "\n")
1600:             acceptance = load_json(acceptance_path)
1601:             acceptance["publishedControlSha256"] = sha256_file(published)
1602:             dump_json(acceptance_path, acceptance)
1603: 
1604:         def snapshot():
1605:             return load_snapshot(
1606:                 str(self.root), "sp01-loan-review", history_reader=verified_history()
1607:             )
1608: 
1609:         def extra(control):
1610:             subset = control["subsets"]["RP01-MC02"]
1611:             subset["acceptance"]["allSprintsAccepted"] = True
1612:             subset["acceptance"]["nativeProofsAccepted"] = True
1613: 
1614:         mutate_control(extra)
1615:         added = snapshot()
1616:         self.assertFalse(added["candidateCurrent"])
1617:         self.assertTrue(added["entryEligible"])
1618:         self.assertTrue(any("publication" in item for item in added["missingEvidence"]))
1619: 
1620:         def escalate(control):
1621:             subset = control["subsets"]["RP01-MC02"]
1622:             subset["acceptance"]["scope"] = "Full RP01 native proof and financial Preview acceptance"
1623:             subset["acceptance"]["networkMilestones"] = (
1624:                 "complete; all SP05 financial settlement gates accepted"
1625:             )
1626:             subset["acceptance"]["staticVerification"] = "missing-forged-verification.json"
1627: 
1628:         mutate_control(escalate)
1629:         scoped = snapshot()
1630:         self.assertFalse(scoped["candidateCurrent"])
1631:         self.assertTrue(scoped["entryEligible"])
1632: 
1633:         def close_all(control):
1634:             subset = control["subsets"]["RP01-MC02"]
1635:             for row in subset["rowSummaries"].values():
1636:                 row["closureTask"] = (
1637:                     "All financial settlement, native proof and corpus "
1638:                     "acceptance obligations are closed."
1639:                 )
1640: 
1641:         mutate_control(close_all)
1642:         closed = snapshot()
1643:         self.assertFalse(closed["candidateCurrent"])
1644:         self.assertTrue(closed["entryEligible"])
1645:         self.assertTrue(any("closure" in item for item in closed["missingEvidence"]))
1646: 
1647:         published.write_bytes(original)
1648:         dump_json(acceptance_path, load_json(LOAN_ACCEPT_SRC))
1649:         body = load_json(acceptance_path)
1650:         body["status"] = "complete-NOT_REVIEWED"
1651:         dump_json(acceptance_path, body)
1652:         unknown = snapshot()
1653:         self.assertFalse(unknown["candidateCurrent"])
1654:         self.assertTrue(unknown["entryEligible"])
1655:         self.assertTrue(any("acceptance-status" in item for item in unknown["missingEvidence"]))
1656:         body["status"] = "complete-but-REVOKED"
1657:         dump_json(acceptance_path, body)
1658:         revoked = snapshot()
1659:         self.assertFalse(revoked["candidateCurrent"])
1660:         self.assertTrue(revoked["entryEligible"])
1661:         self.assertTrue(any("acceptance-status" in item for item in revoked["missingEvidence"]))

41:     def test_composition_green_summary_cannot_clear_failing_full_state(self):
42:         # Even if a review or summary claims all green, a failing full-state test invalidates candidateCurrent
43:         snap = {
44:             "authorityCurrent": True,
45:             "entryEligible": True,
46:             "candidateCurrent": False,
47:             "resourceAdmitted": True,
48:             "sameDefectFailures": 0,
49:             "adminCycles": 0,
50:             "adminSeconds": 0,
51:             "primaryActive": False,
52:             "reproducerVerified": False,
53:             "approachChanged": False,
54:             "nextActionId": "sp05-ledger-driver",
55:             "missingEvidence": ["publication-control-acceptance:exitCode-nonzero"],
56:         }
57:         action = {
58:             "id": "sp05-ledger-implement",
59:             "requirement": "SP05.1",
60:             "capability": "fixed-financial-driver",
61:             "kind": "implement",
62:             "candidate": "daac83578f105b0a70ce44d88c579018881daae5",
63:             "admissionRef": "campaign:sp01-loan-swap-grok-01",
64:             "commandRef": "commands.json#driver",
65:             "evidenceProfile": "local-runtime",
66:         }
67:         dec = assess(snap, action)
68:         self.assertFalse(dec["allow"])
69:         self.assertEqual(dec["reasonCode"], "EVIDENCE_STALE")

71:     def test_new_packet_cannot_erase_prior_failures(self):
72:         # Failures are keyed by lineage: (repository, requirement, capability)
73:         req = "SP05.1"
74:         cap = "fixed-financial-driver"
75:         record_event(self.db_path, str(self.root), req, cap, "candidate-01", "act-01", "defect_failure", {"defectId": "D1"})

77:

80:         self.assertEqual(history["sameDefectFailures"], 2)

120:     def test_reproducer_demonstrates_defect(self):
121:         # Running reproduce_driver.py exits with 1 because the driver returns admitted-not-executed
122:         reproducer = SCRIPTS_DIR.parent / "tests" / "reproduce_driver.py"
123:         res = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(reproducer)], capture_output=True, text=True)
124:         self.assertNotEqual(res.returncode, 0)

20: def digest(path):
21:     return hashlib.sha256(Path(path).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
22: 
23: 
24: class BoundRunnerTests(unittest.TestCase):
25:     setUp = execution.ExecutionTestCase.setUp
26:     tearDown = execution.ExecutionTestCase.tearDown
27:     make_case = execution.ExecutionTestCase.make_case
28:     run_cli = execution.ExecutionTestCase.run_cli

30:     def admit(self, program=None, kind="verify"):
31:         self.action = self.make_case(action_kind=kind)
32:         script = self.root / "approved-check.py"
33:         script.write_text(program or "from pathlib import Path\nPath('child-marker.txt').write_text('checked')\nprint('checked')\n")
34:         node = str(Path(shutil.which("node")).resolve())
35:         launcher = "/home/charl/foreman/skills/foreman/runtime/dist/foreman-launch.js"
36:         python = str(Path(sys.executable).resolve())
37:         self.plan = {
38:             "schema": "moriarty.bound-runner/1", "action": self.action,
39:             "argv": [python, "approved-check.py"],
40:             "files": {python: digest(python), "approved-check.py": digest(script)},
41:             "launcher": [node, launcher],
42:             "launcherFiles": {node: digest(node), launcher: digest(launcher)},
43:             "timeoutSeconds": 5, "graceSeconds": 2, "outputLimitBytes": 4096,
44:             "chargeId": "local-verification-01", "chargedSeconds": 120,
45:             "accountingPath": tr.CURRENT_ACCOUNTING_REL,
46:         }
47:         if kind == "reproduce":
48:             self.plan["assertion"] = {
49:                 "id": "known-behavior", "exitCode": 42,
50:                 "stdoutSha256": hashlib.sha256(b"defect observed\n").hexdigest(),
51:             }
52:         mapping = {"schema": "moriarty-dev.commands/1", "commands": {"driver": {"argv": self.plan["argv"]}}}
53:         tr.dump_json(self.root / "commands.json", mapping)
54:         campaign_path = self.root / "evidence/moriarty-completion-program-2026-09-07/report-reconciliation/campaign-admission.json"
55:         self.campaign_path = campaign_path
56:         campaigns = tr.load_json(campaign_path)
57:         campaign = campaigns["campaigns"]["sp01-loan-swap-grok-01"]
58:         binding = tr.load_json(self.root / campaign["binding"])
59:         # A new current binding preserves the original accepted historical file.
60:         binding["runners"] = {self.action["id"]: self.plan}
61:         tr.dump_json(self.root / "current-binding.json", binding)
62:         campaign["binding"] = "current-binding.json"
63:         campaign["bindingSha256"] = digest(self.root / "current-binding.json")
64:         tr.dump_json(campaign_path, campaigns)
65:         self.accounting = self.root / tr.CURRENT_ACCOUNTING_REL
66:         budget = tr.load_json(self.accounting)
67:         self.runner_digest = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(self.plan, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode()).hexdigest()
68:         # The caller's existing budget runner debits before invoking the plugin.
69:         budget["charges"].append({"id": self.plan["chargeId"], "seconds": self.plan["chargedSeconds"],
70:                                   "candidateHash": self.action["candidate"], "actionId": self.action["id"],
71:                                   "runnerDigest": self.runner_digest})
72:         tr.dump_json(self.accounting, budget)
73:         return self.action
74: 
75:     def test_bound_precharged_verify_executes_once(self):
76:         action = self.admit()
77:         budget_before = self.accounting.read_bytes()
78:         result = self.run_cli("run", "--action", action["id"], "--json")
79:         self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stdout + result.stderr)
80:         self.assertEqual(self.child_marker.read_text(), "checked")
81:         self.assertEqual(self.accounting.read_bytes(), budget_before, "plugin must not charge twice")
82:         self.child_marker.unlink()
83:         again = self.run_cli("run", "--action", action["id"], "--json")
84:         self.assertEqual(again.returncode, 2, again.stdout + again.stderr)
85:         self.assertFalse(self.child_marker.exists(), "one charge cannot authorize a second execution")
86:         with closing(sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)) as conn, conn:
87:             receipt = json.loads(conn.execute("SELECT receipt_json FROM reservations WHERE status='finished'").fetchone()[0])
88:         self.assertEqual(receipt["runnerReceipt"]["runnerDigest"], self.runner_digest)
89:         self.assertEqual(receipt["runnerReceipt"]["chargeId"], self.plan["chargeId"])
90:

139:     def test_supervisor_death_keeps_reservation_unresolved(self):
140:         action = self.admit("from pathlib import Path\nimport time\nPath('child-marker.txt').write_text('started')\ntime.sleep(2)\n")
141:         cli = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "scripts/moriarty_dev/cli.py"
142:         proc = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, str(cli), "--repo", str(self.root), "run", "--action", action["id"]],
143:                                 stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True)
144:         try:
145:             deadline = time.monotonic() + 5
146:             while not self.child_marker.exists() and proc.poll() is None and time.monotonic() < deadline:
147:                 time.sleep(0.01)
148:             self.assertTrue(self.child_marker.exists(), "actual child must have started")
149:             children = Path(f"/proc/{proc.pid}/task/{proc.pid}/children").read_text().split()
150:             self.assertEqual(len(children), 1)
151:             os.kill(int(children[0]), signal.SIGKILL)
152:             proc.communicate(timeout=10)
153:             with closing(sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)) as conn, conn:
154:                 self.assertEqual(conn.execute("SELECT status FROM reservations").fetchone()[0], "active")
155:         finally:
156:             if proc.poll() is None:
157:                 proc.kill()
158:             proc.communicate(timeout=10)

FILE plugins/moriarty-dev/tests/test_store_connections.py
1: """Store connections close on both successful reads and failed initialization."""
2: import sqlite3
3: import sys
4: import tempfile
5: import unittest
6: from pathlib import Path
7: from unittest.mock import patch
8: 
9: sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "scripts"))
10: from moriarty_dev import store
11: 
12: 
13: class StoreConnections(unittest.TestCase):
14:     def setUp(self):
15:         self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
16:         self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
17:         self.path = Path(self.temp.name) / "state.sqlite3"
18:         self.connections = []
19:         self.fail_on = None
20:         owner = self
21: 
22:         class TrackedConnection(sqlite3.Connection):
23:             closed = False
24: 
25:             def execute(self, sql, *args):
26:                 if sql == owner.fail_on:
27:                     raise sqlite3.OperationalError("injected SQL failure")
28:                 return super().execute(sql, *args)
29: 
30:             def close(self):
31:                 self.closed = True
32:                 super().close()
33: 
34:         real_connect = sqlite3.connect
35: 
36:         def connect(*args, **kwargs):
37:             conn = real_connect(*args, **kwargs, factory=TrackedConnection)
38:             self.connections.append(conn)
39:             self.addCleanup(conn.close)
40:             return conn
41: 
42:         self.patcher = patch.object(store.sqlite3, "connect", connect)
43:         self.patcher.start()
44:         self.addCleanup(self.patcher.stop)
45: 
46:     def test_primary_query_closes_success_and_failure(self):
47:         store.init_db(self.path).close()
48:         self.assertFalse(store.has_other_primary(self.path, "none"))
49:         self.assertTrue(all(c.closed for c in self.connections))
50:         with self.assertRaises(sqlite3.OperationalError):
51:             store.has_other_primary(Path(self.temp.name) / "empty.sqlite3", "none")
52:         self.assertTrue(all(c.closed for c in self.connections))
53: 
54:     def test_failed_integrity_probe_closes_connection(self):
55:         store.init_db(self.path).close()
56:         self.fail_on = "PRAGMA schema_version;"
57:         with self.assertRaises(store.StoreError):
58:             store.init_db(self.path)
59:         self.assertTrue(all(c.closed for c in self.connections))
60: 
61:     def test_failed_initialization_closes_connection(self):
62:         self.fail_on = "PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;"
63:         with self.assertRaises(sqlite3.OperationalError):
64:             store.init_db(self.path)
65:         self.assertTrue(all(c.closed for c in self.connections))
